  const sigAt=pos=>{let o=0;for(const r of runs||[]){const n=String(r.text||'').length;if(pos<o+n)return (Number.isFinite(r.scale)&&r.scale>0?r.scale:1)+'|'+(r.emphasis?1:0)+'|'+(r.color||'');o+=n;}return '1|0|';};
  let list=[];
  for(const q of out){
    let a=q.a;
    for(let i=q.a+1;i<=q.b;i++){
      if(i===q.b||sigAt(q.off+i)!==sigAt(q.off+i-1)){
        const s=q.line.slice(a,i),sig=sigAt(q.off+a);
        if(s.length>CFG.maxChars&&s.charCodeAt(0)>255){
          const k=Math.ceil(s.length/CFG.maxChars),sz=Math.ceil(s.length/k);
          for(let j=0;j<s.length;j+=sz)list.push({...q,a:a+j,b:a+Math.min(s.length,j+sz),sig});
        }else list.push({...q,a,b:i,sig});
        a=i;
      }
    }
  }
  const squash=(strict,limit)=>{
    while(list.length>limit){
      let bi=-1,bl=1e9;
      for(let i=0;i<list.length-1;i++){
        if(list[i].row!==list[i+1].row||(strict&&list[i].sig!==list[i+1].sig))continue;
        const l=list[i+1].b-list[i].a;if(l<bl){bl=l;bi=i;}
      }
      if(bi<0)break;
      list.splice(bi,2,{...list[bi],b:list[bi+1].b});
    }
  };
  squash(true,CFG.cap);
  squash(false,Math.max(CFG.cap,14));
  if(list.length===1&&list[0].b-list[0].a>=4){
    const q=list[0],h=Math.ceil((q.b-q.a)/2);
    list=[{...q,b:q.a+h},{...q,a:q.a+h}];
  }
  return list;
})();
const root=new THREE.Group();scene.add(root);
const disposables=[];
const items=[];
const userScaled=(runs||[]).some(r=>Number.isFinite(r.scale)&&r.scale>0&&r.scale!==1);
wordList.forEach((w,i)=>{
  const s=w.line.slice(w.a,w.b),start=w.off+w.a,info=runInfo(start,start+s.length);
  // 指定がなければ、大・小・大・小…と語ごとに大きさを変える（run の scale があるときはそれを活かす）
  const big=userScaled?1+(random()-.5)*.14:(i%2?.8+.18*random():1.04+.28*random());
  const l={i,s,start,row:w.row,info,sc:info.scale,big,r:[random(),random(),random(),random()]};
  l.fs=em*big;
  CFG.style(l);
  l.mesh=createText(s,{textStart:start,font:pickFont(s,CFG.fonts,l.r[3]),fontSize:l.fs,anchorX:'center',anchorY:'middle',color:l.ink,colorRanges:null});
  l.mesh.fillOpacity=0;l.mesh.visible=false;
  l.texts=[l.mesh];
  items.push(l);
});
const N=items.length;
const spread=N>1?Math.min(life*.3,2.2):0,ent=Math.min(.5,life*.17),outro=Math.min(.5,life*.17),ostag=Math.min(.12,life*.05);
items.forEach((l,k)=>{l.t0=N>1?spread*k/(N-1):0;l.o=N>1?k/(N-1):.5;});
let fit=1,laid=false,dead=false;
function measure(l){
  const b=l.mesh.textRenderInfo&&l.mesh.textRenderInfo.blockBounds;
  l.tw0=(b&&b.length===4&&b.every(Number.isFinite))?Math.max(l.fs*.3,b[2]-b[0]):l.fs*Array.from(l.s).length*.95;
  const z=CFG.size(l);
  l.pw0=z[0];l.ph0=z[1];l.pw=l.pw0*l.sc;l.ph=l.ph0*l.sc;
}
function flow(r,k,gap,limit){
  const total=r.reduce((s,l,j)=>s+l.pw+(j?gap:0),0),cap=k>1?total/k*1.15+gap:limit;
  const out=[];let cur=[],x=0;
  for(const l of r){if(cur.length&&x+gap+l.pw>cap){out.push(cur);cur=[];x=0;}x+=(cur.length?gap:0)+l.pw;cur.push(l);}
  out.push(cur);return out;
}
function layout(){
  const gap=em*CFG.gap,vgap=em*CFG.vgap,limit=W*.84;
  const hard=[];for(const l of items)(hard[l.row]=hard[l.row]||[]).push(l);
  const rowsList=hard.filter(Boolean);
  let best=null;
  for(let k=1;k<=6;k++){
    const lines=[];for(const r of rowsList)lines.push(...flow(r,k,gap,1e9));
    const lh=lines.map(r=>Math.max(...r.map(l=>l.ph)));
    let totalH=0;lines.forEach((r,j)=>{totalH+=lh[j]+(j?vgap:0);});
    const maxW=Math.max(1,...lines.map(r=>r.reduce((s,l,j)=>s+l.pw+(j?gap:0),0)));
    const f=Math.min(1,W*.84/maxW,H*.7/Math.max(1,totalH));
    if(!best||f>best.f+.001)best={f,lines,lh,totalH};
  }
  let y=best.totalH/2;
  best.lines.forEach((r,k)=>{
    const w=r.reduce((s,l,j)=>s+l.pw+(j?gap:0),0);
    let x=-w/2;y-=best.lh[k]/2;
    for(const l of r){l.x=x+l.pw/2;l.y=y+(l.r[2]-.5)*l.ph*CFG.wob;x+=l.pw+gap;}
    y-=best.lh[k]/2+vgap;
  });
  fit=best.f;
  root.scale.setScalar(fit);
}
function update(time){
  if(dead)return;
  const t=clamp(Number.isFinite(time)?time:0,0,life);
  let changed=!laid;
  for(const l of items){const inf=l.mesh.textRenderInfo;if(inf&&l.info2!==inf){l.info2=inf;changed=true;}}
  if(changed){for(const l of items)measure(l);layout();laid=true;}
  pose(t);
}
function dispose(){
  if(dead)return;dead=true;
  for(const l of items)for(const m of l.texts){root.remove(m);m.dispose();}
  for(const d of disposables)d.dispose();
  scene.remove(root);
}

const TAPE_FRAG=[GLSL_COMMON,
'uniform vec2 u_half;uniform vec2 u_size;uniform float u_alpha;uniform float u_prog;uniform float u_from;uniform float u_unit;',
'uniform vec3 u_col;uniform vec3 u_hi;',
'void main(){',
'  vec2 p=(vUv-.5)*u_size;',
'  float dBox=sdRB(p,u_half,1.2*u_unit);',
'  float dCh=(abs(p.x)-u_half.x+abs(p.y)-u_half.y+3.4*u_unit)*.7071;',        // 角を斜めに落とした帯
'  float d=max(dBox,dCh);',
'  float xr=-u_half.x+u_prog*2.*u_half.x,xl=-u_half.x+u_from*2.*u_half.x;',
'  float clip=smoothstep(xr+.7,xr-.7,p.x)*smoothstep(xl-.7,xl+.7,p.x);',
'  float a=(1.-smoothstep(-.6,.6,d))*clip;',
'  float sh=(1.-smoothstep(-2.*u_unit,2.*u_unit,sdRB(p-vec2(1.3,-2.4)*u_unit,u_half,u_unit)))*.38*clip;',
'  float gy=p.y/u_half.y*.5+.5;',
'  float sheen=exp(-pow((gy-.74)*3.2,2.))*.14;',
'  vec3 col=u_col*(.9+.1*gy)+sheen*(vec3(1.)-u_col*.5);',
'  col*=.965+.07*hash21(floor(p*1.3+3.));',                                    // ビニールの細かいざらつき
'  float ins=4.2*u_unit;',
'  float ln=1.-smoothstep(0.,1.15*u_unit,abs(sdRB(p,u_half-ins,u_unit*.8)));',   // エンボスの縁取り線
'  float ln2=1.-smoothstep(0.,1.15*u_unit,abs(sdRB(p-vec2(.9,-.9)*u_unit,u_half-ins,u_unit*.8)));',
'  col=mix(col,vec3(0.),ln2*.4*(1.-ln));',
'  col=mix(col,u_hi,ln*.85);',
'  float head=smoothstep(9.*u_unit,0.,xr-p.x)*step(.001,u_prog)*step(u_prog,.999);',
'  col=mix(col,vec3(1.),head*.2);',                                            // 印字ヘッドのあたり
'  float oa=a+sh*(1.-a);',
'  vec3 rgb=oa>.001?col*a/oa:vec3(0.);',
'  gl_FragColor=vec4(rgb,oa*u_alpha);',
'}'].join(NL);
const MARGIN=Math.round(Math.max(10,em*.3));
const big=1e4;
function initItem(l){
  l.g=new THREE.Group();root.add(l.g);
  const un=Math.max(.5,l.fs/42);
  const hi=new THREE.Color(l.paper);
  const lightTape=luma(l.paper)>.55;
  l.mat=new THREE.ShaderMaterial({transparent:true,depthTest:false,depthWrite:false,vertexShader:VERT,fragmentShader:TAPE_FRAG,
    uniforms:{u_half:{value:new THREE.Vector2(1,1)},u_size:{value:new THREE.Vector2(1,1)},u_alpha:{value:0},u_prog:{value:0},u_from:{value:0},u_unit:{value:un},
      u_col:{value:rgb(l.paper)},u_hi:{value:lightTape?new THREE.Vector3(.2,.2,.22):rgb(l.paper).multiplyScalar(.35).add(new THREE.Vector3(.55,.55,.55))}}});
  disposables.push(l.mat);
  l.quad=makeQuad(l.mat,1,1);l.quad.renderOrder=l.i*4;l.g.add(l.quad);disposables.push(l.quad.geometry);
  // 文字の凹凸：少しずらした影の文字をもう1枚
  const dark=luma(l.ink)>.5;
  l.shade=createText(l.s,{textStart:l.start,font:l.mesh.font,fontSize:l.fs,anchorX:'center',anchorY:'middle',color:dark?'#000000':'#ffffff',colorRanges:null});
  l.shade.fillOpacity=0;l.shade.visible=false;l.shade.renderOrder=l.i*4+1;l.shade.material.depthTest=false;l.shade.material.depthWrite=false;
  l.shade.position.set(1.2*un,-1.2*un,0);
  l.texts.push(l.shade);
  l.mesh.renderOrder=l.i*4+2;l.mesh.material.depthTest=false;l.mesh.material.depthWrite=false;
  l.g.add(l.shade);l.g.add(l.mesh);
  l.g.visible=false;
  l.steps=4+Array.from(l.s).length*2;
  l.rot0=(l.r[1]-.5)*.07;
}
// 「ジ・ジ・ジー」と刻むように進む：段ごとに素早く動いて止まる
const stepped=(u,n)=>{const s=clamp(u)*n,f=s-Math.floor(s);return clamp((Math.floor(s)+smooth(f/.45))/n);};
function pose(t){
  for(const l of items){
    if(!l.pw)continue;
    const pu=clamp((t-l.t0)/(ent*1.2)),qu=1-clamp((life-t-ostag*(1-l.o))/(outro*1.1));
    const prog=stepped(pu,l.steps),from=stepped(qu,l.steps);
    const vis=pu>0&&t<life&&from<.999;
    l.g.visible=vis;
    if(!vis){l.mesh.visible=false;l.shade.visible=false;continue;}
    const printing=prog<.999&&prog>0;
    const jit=printing?Math.sin(t*95+l.i*2)*.9*(l.fs/42):0;
    l.g.position.set(l.x,l.y+jit,0);
    l.g.rotation.z=l.rot0;
    l.g.scale.setScalar(l.sc);
    const w=l.pw0,h=l.ph0;
    l.quad.scale.set(w+2*MARGIN,h+2*MARGIN,1);
    const m=l.mat.uniforms;
    m.u_half.value.set(w/2,h/2);m.u_size.value.set(w+2*MARGIN,h+2*MARGIN);m.u_alpha.value=1;m.u_prog.value=prog;m.u_from.value=from;
    const xr=-w/2+prog*w,xl=-w/2+from*w;
    for(const tx of [l.mesh,l.shade]){tx.visible=true;tx.clipRect=[xl,-big,xr,big];}
    l.mesh.fillOpacity=1;l.shade.fillOpacity=.55;
  }
}

items.forEach(initItem);
update(0);
return {update,dispose};
文字モーション（コード）10/14 追加

文スタンプでドンNEWSTAMP-INK

語ごとに色違いの二重枠の印を、上からドンと押し当てます。少し沈んで、インクがかすれます。

メソッド工房の「文字モーション」のサンプルから選びます。

コードを見る
// @fonts
// 語ごとに色違いの二重枠の印を、上からドンと押し当てる。少し沈み、インクがかすれてムラになる。
const {THREE,scene,createText,text,runs,width,height,fontSize,duration,random,accent,color,fonts,fontsFor,fontURL}=api;
const NL=String.fromCharCode(10);
const clamp=(x,a=0,b=1)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));
const smooth=x=>{x=clamp(x);return x*x*(3-2*x)};
const mix=(a,b,k)=>a+(b-a)*k;
const spring=x=>{x=clamp(x);return 1-(1-x)*Math.exp(-3.4*x)*Math.cos(6.6*x)};
const hash=(a,b)=>{const s=Math.sin(a*127.1+b*311.7)*43758.5453;return s-Math.floor(s)};
const W=Math.max(1,width),H=Math.max(1,height),em=Math.max(8,fontSize||H*.18),life=Math.max(.05,duration);
const hexNum=hex=>{let h=String(hex).trim().replace('#','');if(h.length===3)h=h[0]+h[0]+h[1]+h[1]+h[2]+h[2];return parseInt(h.slice(0,6),16)||0;};
const luma=hex=>{const n=hexNum(hex);return (.299*(n>>16&255)+.587*(n>>8&255)+.114*(n&255))/255};
// シェーダーに渡す色は、色空間の変換をしない生の値で渡す
const rgb=hex=>{const n=hexNum(hex);return new THREE.Vector3((n>>16&255)/255,(n>>8&255)/255,(n&255)/255);};
const PUNCT='、。,.!?！？…「」『』（）()・～〜ー-';
const PART='のはがをにへともやでよねかなてたも';
// 語に当たる run の大きさ・強調・色を、歌詞の文字位置 [start,end) から求める
const runInfo=(start,end)=>{
  let o=0,sum=0,n=0,emph=false,col=null;
  for(const r of runs||[]){
    const len=String(r.text||'').length,a=Math.max(start,o),b=Math.min(end,o+len);
    if(b>a){const s=Number.isFinite(r.scale)&&r.scale>0?r.scale:1;sum+=s*(b-a);n+=b-a;if(r.emphasis)emph=true;if(r.color&&!col)col=r.color;}
    o+=len;
  }
  return {scale:n?sum/n:1,emph,col};
};
// その語の全部の文字を持っている書体だけから選ぶ（なければ選んだ書体）
const pickFont=(s,names,r)=>{
  const cand=[];
  for(const k of names){
    if(fonts&&fonts[k]&&Array.from(s).every(c=>!c.trim()||fontsFor(c).includes(k)))cand.push(fonts[k]);
  }
  cand.push(fontURL);
  return cand[Math.min(cand.length-1,Math.floor(r*cand.length))];
};
const makeQuad=(mat,w,h)=>{const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(w||1,h||1),mat);m.frustumCulled=false;return m;};
const VERT=['varying vec2 vUv;','void main(){vUv=uv;gl_Position=projectionMatrix*modelViewMatrix*vec4(position,1.0);}'].join(NL);
const GLSL_COMMON=[
'precision highp float;',
'varying vec2 vUv;',
'float hash21(vec2 p){return fract(sin(dot(p,vec2(127.1,311.7)))*43758.5453);}',
'float vnoise(vec2 p){vec2 i=floor(p),f=fract(p);f=f*f*(3.-2.*f);',
'  return mix(mix(hash21(i),hash21(i+vec2(1.,0.)),f.x),mix(hash21(i+vec2(0.,1.)),hash21(i+vec2(1.,1.)),f.x),f.y);}',
'float sdRB(vec2 p,vec2 b,float r){vec2 q=abs(p)-b+r;return length(max(q,0.))+min(max(q.x,q.y),0.)-r;}'
].join(NL);

const CFG={cap:7,maxChars:4,fonts:['dela','mincho'],gap:.18,vgap:.2,wob:.1,
  inks:['#d92b3a','#2352c8','#1f8f55','#7b3fb2','#23242b','#e0661f'],
  papers:['#f8f2e2','#fbe8dc','#e6f1f7','#eef3df','#ece3f2','#ead7ae'],shift:Math.floor(random()*6),
  style(l){
    // 印の色は語ごとに回す。強調語はアクセント色（明るすぎるときは濃くする）
    let ink=CFG.inks[(l.i+CFG.shift)%CFG.inks.length];
    const given=l.info.col||(l.info.emph?accent:null);
    if(given){
      const n=hexNum(given),k=luma(given)>.55?.55:1;
      const c=v=>Math.round(v*k).toString(16).padStart(2,'0');
      ink='#'+c(n>>16&255)+c(n>>8&255)+c(n&255);
    }
    l.ink=ink;l.paper=CFG.papers[(l.i*2+CFG.shift)%CFG.papers.length];
  },
  size(l){return [l.tw0+l.fs*.95,l.fs*1.5];}};

// 歌詞を語に区切る（日本語は Intl.Segmenter。助詞と句読点は前の語につけ、長い語は割り、語が多すぎるときはまとめる）
const wordList=(()=>{
  const seg=(typeof Intl!=='undefined'&&Intl.Segmenter)?new Intl.Segmenter('ja',{granularity:'word'}):null;
  const out=[];let off=0;
  String(text||'').split(NL).forEach((line,row)=>{
    const parts=[];
    if(seg){for(const p of seg.segment(line)){if(p.segment.trim())parts.push({a:p.index,b:p.index+p.segment.length});}}
    else{for(let i=0;i<line.length;i+=2)parts.push({a:i,b:Math.min(line.length,i+2)});}
    const m=[];
    for(const p of parts){
      const s=line.slice(p.a,p.b),last=m[m.length-1];
      const isP=Array.from(s).every(c=>PUNCT.includes(c));
      const isJ=s.length===1&&PART.includes(s);
      if(last&&(isP||(isJ&&last.b-last.a<CFG.maxChars)))last.b=p.b;else m.push({a:p.a,b:p.b});
    }
    for(const q of m)out.push({a:q.a,b:q.b,line,row,off});
    off+=line.length+1;
  });
  // run の大きさ・強調・色が変わるところでは語を分ける（私 は 元気 です…と語ごとに大きさを変えられるように）
  const sigAt=pos=>{let o=0;for(const r of runs||[]){const n=String(r.text||'').length;if(pos<o+n)return (Number.isFinite(r.scale)&&r.scale>0?r.scale:1)+'|'+(r.emphasis?1:0)+'|'+(r.color||'');o+=n;}return '1|0|';};
  let list=[];
  for(const q of out){
    let a=q.a;
    for(let i=q.a+1;i<=q.b;i++){
      if(i===q.b||sigAt(q.off+i)!==sigAt(q.off+i-1)){
        const s=q.line.slice(a,i),sig=sigAt(q.off+a);
        if(s.length>CFG.maxChars&&s.charCodeAt(0)>255){
          const k=Math.ceil(s.length/CFG.maxChars),sz=Math.ceil(s.length/k);
          for(let j=0;j<s.length;j+=sz)list.push({...q,a:a+j,b:a+Math.min(s.length,j+sz),sig});
        }else list.push({...q,a,b:i,sig});
        a=i;
      }
    }
  }
  const squash=(strict,limit)=>{
    while(list.length>limit){
      let bi=-1,bl=1e9;
      for(let i=0;i<list.length-1;i++){
        if(list[i].row!==list[i+1].row||(strict&&list[i].sig!==list[i+1].sig))continue;
        const l=list[i+1].b-list[i].a;if(l<bl){bl=l;bi=i;}
      }
      if(bi<0)break;
      list.splice(bi,2,{...list[bi],b:list[bi+1].b});
    }
  };
  squash(true,CFG.cap);
  squash(false,Math.max(CFG.cap,14));
  if(list.length===1&&list[0].b-list[0].a>=4){
    const q=list[0],h=Math.ceil((q.b-q.a)/2);
    list=[{...q,b:q.a+h},{...q,a:q.a+h}];
  }
  return list;
})();
const root=new THREE.Group();scene.add(root);
const disposables=[];
const items=[];
const userScaled=(runs||[]).some(r=>Number.isFinite(r.scale)&&r.scale>0&&r.scale!==1);
wordList.forEach((w,i)=>{
  const s=w.line.slice(w.a,w.b),start=w.off+w.a,info=runInfo(start,start+s.length);
  // 指定がなければ、大・小・大・小…と語ごとに大きさを変える（run の scale があるときはそれを活かす）
  const big=userScaled?1+(random()-.5)*.14:(i%2?.8+.18*random():1.04+.28*random());
  const l={i,s,start,row:w.row,info,sc:info.scale,big,r:[random(),random(),random(),random()]};
  l.fs=em*big;
  CFG.style(l);
  l.mesh=createText(s,{textStart:start,font:pickFont(s,CFG.fonts,l.r[3]),fontSize:l.fs,anchorX:'center',anchorY:'middle',color:l.ink,colorRanges:null});
  l.mesh.fillOpacity=0;l.mesh.visible=false;
  l.texts=[l.mesh];
  items.push(l);
});
const N=items.length;
const spread=N>1?Math.min(life*.3,2.2):0,ent=Math.min(.5,life*.17),outro=Math.min(.5,life*.17),ostag=Math.min(.12,life*.05);
items.forEach((l,k)=>{l.t0=N>1?spread*k/(N-1):0;l.o=N>1?k/(N-1):.5;});
let fit=1,laid=false,dead=false;
function measure(l){
  const b=l.mesh.textRenderInfo&&l.mesh.textRenderInfo.blockBounds;
  l.tw0=(b&&b.length===4&&b.every(Number.isFinite))?Math.max(l.fs*.3,b[2]-b[0]):l.fs*Array.from(l.s).length*.95;
  const z=CFG.size(l);
  l.pw0=z[0];l.ph0=z[1];l.pw=l.pw0*l.sc;l.ph=l.ph0*l.sc;
}
function flow(r,k,gap,limit){
  const total=r.reduce((s,l,j)=>s+l.pw+(j?gap:0),0),cap=k>1?total/k*1.15+gap:limit;
  const out=[];let cur=[],x=0;
  for(const l of r){if(cur.length&&x+gap+l.pw>cap){out.push(cur);cur=[];x=0;}x+=(cur.length?gap:0)+l.pw;cur.push(l);}
  out.push(cur);return out;
}
function layout(){
  const gap=em*CFG.gap,vgap=em*CFG.vgap,limit=W*.84;
  const hard=[];for(const l of items)(hard[l.row]=hard[l.row]||[]).push(l);
  const rowsList=hard.filter(Boolean);
  let best=null;
  for(let k=1;k<=6;k++){
    const lines=[];for(const r of rowsList)lines.push(...flow(r,k,gap,1e9));
    const lh=lines.map(r=>Math.max(...r.map(l=>l.ph)));
    let totalH=0;lines.forEach((r,j)=>{totalH+=lh[j]+(j?vgap:0);});
    const maxW=Math.max(1,...lines.map(r=>r.reduce((s,l,j)=>s+l.pw+(j?gap:0),0)));
    const f=Math.min(1,W*.84/maxW,H*.7/Math.max(1,totalH));
    if(!best||f>best.f+.001)best={f,lines,lh,totalH};
  }
  let y=best.totalH/2;
  best.lines.forEach((r,k)=>{
    const w=r.reduce((s,l,j)=>s+l.pw+(j?gap:0),0);
    let x=-w/2;y-=best.lh[k]/2;
    for(const l of r){l.x=x+l.pw/2;l.y=y+(l.r[2]-.5)*l.ph*CFG.wob;x+=l.pw+gap;}
    y-=best.lh[k]/2+vgap;
  });
  fit=best.f;
  root.scale.setScalar(fit);
}
function update(time){
  if(dead)return;
  const t=clamp(Number.isFinite(time)?time:0,0,life);
  let changed=!laid;
  for(const l of items){const inf=l.mesh.textRenderInfo;if(inf&&l.info2!==inf){l.info2=inf;changed=true;}}
  if(changed){for(const l of items)measure(l);layout();laid=true;}
  pose(t);
}
function dispose(){
  if(dead)return;dead=true;
  for(const l of items)for(const m of l.texts){root.remove(m);m.dispose();}
  for(const d of disposables)d.dispose();
  scene.remove(root);
}

const STAMP_FRAG=[GLSL_COMMON,
'uniform vec2 u_half;uniform vec2 u_size;uniform float u_alpha;uniform float u_unit;uniform float u_seed;uniform float u_mode;',
'uniform float u_lift;uniform float u_wet;uniform float u_inkA;uniform vec3 u_paper;uniform vec3 u_ink;uniform vec2 u_frame;',
// インクの残り具合（0 でかすれて抜ける、1 で濃い）。大きなムラと細かい粒と、押す強さの偏り
'float inkV(vec2 p){',
'  float blotch=vnoise(p*.045+u_seed*9.);',
'  float grain=vnoise(p*.62+u_seed*3.7);',
'  float fine=hash21(floor(p*1.1)+u_seed*5.);',
'  float press=dot(normalize(vec2(.55,.83)),p/u_half);',
'  return grain*.5+blotch*.55+fine*.14+press*.14+.06+u_wet*.12;',
'}',
'void main(){',
'  vec2 p=(vUv-.5)*u_size;',
'  float U=u_unit;',
'  float dCard=sdRB(p,u_half,1.6*U);',
'  float cardA=1.-smoothstep(-.7,.7,dCard);',
'  vec3 pap=u_paper*(.985+.04*vnoise(p*.55+3.))*(.97+.05*smoothstep(-u_half.y,u_half.y,p.y));',
'  pap*=1.-.08*smoothstep(-5.*U,0.,dCard+0.)*.0;',
'  float v=inkV(p);',
'  float k=smoothstep(.34,.52,v);',               // 枠のかすれ
'  float kt=smoothstep(.22,.40,v);',              // 文字のかすれ（読めるように、枠より控えめ）
'  if(u_mode>.5){',                                           // 文字の上に重ねる：インクが抜けたところだけ紙の色を返す
'    float inside=1.-smoothstep(0.,2.,abs(sdRB(p,u_half-vec2(3.*U),U))-2.)*0.;',
'    gl_FragColor=vec4(pap,cardA*(1.-kt)*.9*u_alpha*u_inkA);',
'    return;',
'  }',
'  float w1=(2.7+u_wet*.9)*U,gap=2.4*U,w2=1.2*U;',
'  float dO=abs(sdRB(p,u_frame,3.*U))-w1*.5;',
'  float dI=abs(sdRB(p,u_frame-vec2(w1*.5+gap+w2*.5),2.*U))-w2*.5;',
'  float fr=max(1.-smoothstep(-.5,.5,dO),1.-smoothstep(-.5,.5,dI));',
'  float inkA=fr*k*(.82+.18*u_wet)*u_inkA;',
'  vec3 inkC=mix(u_ink,u_ink*.78,u_wet);',
'  vec3 col=mix(pap,inkC,inkA);',
'  float sh=(1.-smoothstep(-2.*U-u_lift*6.*U,2.*U+u_lift*6.*U,sdRB(p-vec2(1.4+u_lift*4.,-2.6-u_lift*8.)*U,u_half,1.6*U)))*(.34-.12*u_lift);',
'  float oa=cardA+sh*(1.-cardA);',
'  vec3 rgb=oa>.001?col*cardA/oa:vec3(0.);',
'  gl_FragColor=vec4(rgb,oa*u_alpha);',
'}'].join(NL);
const MARGIN=Math.round(Math.max(22,em*.7));
function initItem(l){
  l.outer=new THREE.Group();root.add(l.outer);
  const un=Math.max(.5,l.fs/42);
  const mk=mode=>new THREE.ShaderMaterial({transparent:true,depthTest:false,depthWrite:false,vertexShader:VERT,fragmentShader:STAMP_FRAG,
    uniforms:{u_half:{value:new THREE.Vector2(1,1)},u_size:{value:new THREE.Vector2(1,1)},u_alpha:{value:0},u_unit:{value:un},u_seed:{value:l.r[0]},u_mode:{value:mode},
      u_lift:{value:0},u_wet:{value:0},u_inkA:{value:0},u_paper:{value:rgb(l.paper)},u_ink:{value:rgb(l.ink)},u_frame:{value:new THREE.Vector2(1,1)}}});
  l.mat=mk(0);l.matTop=mk(1);disposables.push(l.mat,l.matTop);
  l.quad=makeQuad(l.mat,1,1);l.quad.renderOrder=l.i*4;
  l.top=makeQuad(l.matTop,1,1);l.top.renderOrder=l.i*4+2;
  disposables.push(l.quad.geometry,l.top.geometry);
  l.mesh.renderOrder=l.i*4+1;l.mesh.material.depthTest=false;l.mesh.material.depthWrite=false;
  l.outer.add(l.quad,l.mesh,l.top);
  l.outer.visible=false;
  l.rot0=(l.r[1]-.5)*.2+(l.r[1]>.5?.03:-.03);
  l.dir=l.i%2?1:-1;
}
function pose(t){
  for(const l of items){
    if(!l.pw)continue;
    const u=clamp((t-l.t0)/ent),q=1-clamp((life-t-ostag*(1-l.o))/outro);
    const hit=.4;                                             // 押し当てる瞬間（登場の半ば）
    const down=clamp(u/hit),after=clamp((u-hit)/(1-hit));
    const a=smooth(u*9)*(1-smooth((q-.15)/.85));
    l.outer.visible=a>.003&&t<life;
    if(!l.outer.visible){l.mesh.visible=false;continue;}
    const sink=u>=hit?Math.exp(-7*after)*Math.cos(11*after):0;     // 押された瞬間に少し沈んで戻る
    const s=(1+.6*(1-down)*(1-down))*(1-.07*sink)*(1+.05*q);
    const lift=(1-down)+q*.9;
    const wet=u>=hit?Math.exp(-3.2*after*(ent/.5)):0;
    const ink=smooth((u-hit+.02)*14)*(1-smooth((q-.15)/.85));
    const tw=(1-down)*l.dir*.3+q*q*l.dir*.5;
    l.outer.position.set(l.x+q*q*l.dir*W*.06/fit,l.y+(1-down)*(1-down)*H*.09/fit-q*q*H*.2/fit,0);
    l.outer.rotation.z=l.rot0+tw;
    l.outer.scale.setScalar(l.sc*s);
    const w=l.pw0,h=l.ph0;
    const sz=[w+2*MARGIN,h+2*MARGIN];
    for(const [qd,mt] of [[l.quad,l.mat],[l.top,l.matTop]]){
      qd.scale.set(sz[0],sz[1],1);
      const m=mt.uniforms;
      m.u_half.value.set(w/2,h/2);m.u_size.value.set(sz[0],sz[1]);m.u_alpha.value=a;m.u_lift.value=lift;m.u_wet.value=wet;m.u_inkA.value=ink;
      m.u_frame.value.set(w/2-l.fs*.18,h/2-l.fs*.18);
    }
    l.mesh.visible=true;l.mesh.fillOpacity=a*.94*ink;
  }
}

items.forEach(initItem);
update(0);
return {update,dispose};
文字モーション（コード）10/14 追加

文蛍光ペンで線を引くNEWMARKER-HIGHLIGHT

語の後ろへ蛍光ペンの帯を左から引いて塗ります。強調語は別の色で二度塗りします。

メソッド工房の「文字モーション」のサンプルから選びます。

コードを見る
// @fonts
// 語の後ろに蛍光ペンの帯を左から引いて、塗られた文字は濃い色に変わる。強調語は別の色で二度塗り。ペンが語から語へ移っていく。
const {THREE,scene,createText,text,runs,width,height,fontSize,duration,random,accent,color,fonts,fontsFor,fontURL}=api;
const NL=String.fromCharCode(10);
const clamp=(x,a=0,b=1)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));
const smooth=x=>{x=clamp(x);return x*x*(3-2*x)};
const mix=(a,b,k)=>a+(b-a)*k;
const spring=x=>{x=clamp(x);return 1-(1-x)*Math.exp(-3.4*x)*Math.cos(6.6*x)};
const hash=(a,b)=>{const s=Math.sin(a*127.1+b*311.7)*43758.5453;return s-Math.floor(s)};
const W=Math.max(1,width),H=Math.max(1,height),em=Math.max(8,fontSize||H*.18),life=Math.max(.05,duration);
const hexNum=hex=>{let h=String(hex).trim().replace('#','');if(h.length===3)h=h[0]+h[0]+h[1]+h[1]+h[2]+h[2];return parseInt(h.slice(0,6),16)||0;};
const luma=hex=>{const n=hexNum(hex);return (.299*(n>>16&255)+.587*(n>>8&255)+.114*(n&255))/255};
// シェーダーに渡す色は、色空間の変換をしない生の値で渡す
const rgb=hex=>{const n=hexNum(hex);return new THREE.Vector3((n>>16&255)/255,(n>>8&255)/255,(n&255)/255);};
const PUNCT='、。,.!?！？…「」『』（）()・～〜ー-';
const PART='のはがをにへともやでよねかなてたも';
// 語に当たる run の大きさ・強調・色を、歌詞の文字位置 [start,end) から求める
const runInfo=(start,end)=>{
  let o=0,sum=0,n=0,emph=false,col=null;
  for(const r of runs||[]){
    const len=String(r.text||'').length,a=Math.max(start,o),b=Math.min(end,o+len);
    if(b>a){const s=Number.isFinite(r.scale)&&r.scale>0?r.scale:1;sum+=s*(b-a);n+=b-a;if(r.emphasis)emph=true;if(r.color&&!col)col=r.color;}
    o+=len;
  }
  return {scale:n?sum/n:1,emph,col};
};
// その語の全部の文字を持っている書体だけから選ぶ（なければ選んだ書体）
const pickFont=(s,names,r)=>{
  const cand=[];
  for(const k of names){
    if(fonts&&fonts[k]&&Array.from(s).every(c=>!c.trim()||fontsFor(c).includes(k)))cand.push(fonts[k]);
  }
  cand.push(fontURL);
  return cand[Math.min(cand.length-1,Math.floor(r*cand.length))];
};
const makeQuad=(mat,w,h)=>{const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(w||1,h||1),mat);m.frustumCulled=false;return m;};
const VERT=['varying vec2 vUv;','void main(){vUv=uv;gl_Position=projectionMatrix*modelViewMatrix*vec4(position,1.0);}'].join(NL);
const GLSL_COMMON=[
'precision highp float;',
'varying vec2 vUv;',
'float hash21(vec2 p){return fract(sin(dot(p,vec2(127.1,311.7)))*43758.5453);}',
'float vnoise(vec2 p){vec2 i=floor(p),f=fract(p);f=f*f*(3.-2.*f);',
'  return mix(mix(hash21(i),hash21(i+vec2(1.,0.)),f.x),mix(hash21(i+vec2(0.,1.)),hash21(i+vec2(1.,1.)),f.x),f.y);}',
'float sdRB(vec2 p,vec2 b,float r){vec2 q=abs(p)-b+r;return length(max(q,0.))+min(max(q.x,q.y),0.)-r;}'
].join(NL);

const CFG={cap:8,maxChars:5,fonts:['marker'],gap:.2,vgap:.3,wob:.08,
  bands:['#fff04d','#ff7fc4','#8dff6a','#59e8ff','#ffb347'],shift:Math.floor(random()*5),
  style(l){
    // 蛍光ペンの色は語ごとに回す。強調語はアクセント色で、二度塗り
    let band;
    if(l.info.col)band=l.info.col;
    else if(l.info.emph)band=accent;
    else band=CFG.bands[(l.i+CFG.shift)%CFG.bands.length];
    l.band=band;
    l.mode=(l.info.emph||l.info.col)?2:(l.r[0]<.45?0:(l.r[0]<.75?1:2));
    l.ink=color;                                    // 塗られる前は歌詞の文字色
    l.hiInk=luma(band)>.5?'#1b1c24':'#ffffff';      // 塗られたところの文字色
  },
  size(l){return [l.tw0+l.fs*.55,l.fs*1.25];}};

// 歌詞を語に区切る（日本語は Intl.Segmenter。助詞と句読点は前の語につけ、長い語は割り、語が多すぎるときはまとめる）
const wordList=(()=>{
  const seg=(typeof Intl!=='undefined'&&Intl.Segmenter)?new Intl.Segmenter('ja',{granularity:'word'}):null;
  const out=[];let off=0;
  String(text||'').split(NL).forEach((line,row)=>{
    const parts=[];
    if(seg){for(const p of seg.segment(line)){if(p.segment.trim())parts.push({a:p.index,b:p.index+p.segment.length});}}
    else{for(let i=0;i<line.length;i+=2)parts.push({a:i,b:Math.min(line.length,i+2)});}
    const m=[];
    for(const p of parts){
      const s=line.slice(p.a,p.b),last=m[m.length-1];
      const isP=Array.from(s).every(c=>PUNCT.includes(c));
      const isJ=s.length===1&&PART.includes(s);
      if(last&&(isP||(isJ&&last.b-last.a<CFG.maxChars)))last.b=p.b;else m.push({a:p.a,b:p.b});
    }
    for(const q of m)out.push({a:q.a,b:q.b,line,row,off});
    off+=line.length+1;
  });
  // run の大きさ・強調・色が変わるところでは語を分ける（私 は 元気 です…と語ごとに大きさを変えられるように）
  const sigAt=pos=>{let o=0;for(const r of runs||[]){const n=String(r.text||'').length;if(pos<o+n)return (Number.isFinite(r.scale)&&r.scale>0?r.scale:1)+'|'+(r.emphasis?1:0)+'|'+(r.color||'');o+=n;}return '1|0|';};
  let list=[];
  for(const q of out){
    let a=q.a;
    for(let i=q.a+1;i<=q.b;i++){
      if(i===q.b||sigAt(q.off+i)!==sigAt(q.off+i-1)){
        const s=q.line.slice(a,i),sig=sigAt(q.off+a);
        if(s.length>CFG.maxChars&&s.charCodeAt(0)>255){
          const k=Math.ceil(s.length/CFG.maxChars),sz=Math.ceil(s.length/k);
          for(let j=0;j<s.length;j+=sz)list.push({...q,a:a+j,b:a+Math.min(s.length,j+sz),sig});
        }else list.push({...q,a,b:i,sig});
        a=i;
      }
    }
  }
  const squash=(strict,limit)=>{
    while(list.length>limit){
      let bi=-1,bl=1e9;
      for(let i=0;i<list.length-1;i++){
        if(list[i].row!==list[i+1].row||(strict&&list[i].sig!==list[i+1].sig))continue;
        const l=list[i+1].b-list[i].a;if(l<bl){bl=l;bi=i;}
      }
      if(bi<0)break;
      list.splice(bi,2,{...list[bi],b:list[bi+1].b});
    }
  };
  squash(true,CFG.cap);
  squash(false,Math.max(CFG.cap,14));
  if(list.length===1&&list[0].b-list[0].a>=4){
    const q=list[0],h=Math.ceil((q.b-q.a)/2);
    list=[{...q,b:q.a+h},{...q,a:q.a+h}];
  }
  return list;
})();
const root=new THREE.Group();scene.add(root);
const disposables=[];
const items=[];
const userScaled=(runs||[]).some(r=>Number.isFinite(r.scale)&&r.scale>0&&r.scale!==1);
wordList.forEach((w,i)=>{
  const s=w.line.slice(w.a,w.b),start=w.off+w.a,info=runInfo(start,start+s.length);
  // 指定がなければ、大・小・大・小…と語ごとに大きさを変える（run の scale があるときはそれを活かす）
  const big=userScaled?1+(random()-.5)*.14:(i%2?.8+.18*random():1.04+.28*random());
  const l={i,s,start,row:w.row,info,sc:info.scale,big,r:[random(),random(),random(),random()]};
  l.fs=em*big;
  CFG.style(l);
  l.mesh=createText(s,{textStart:start,font:pickFont(s,CFG.fonts,l.r[3]),fontSize:l.fs,anchorX:'center',anchorY:'middle',color:l.ink,colorRanges:null});
  l.mesh.fillOpacity=0;l.mesh.visible=false;
  l.texts=[l.mesh];
  items.push(l);
});
const N=items.length;
const spread=N>1?Math.min(life*.3,2.2):0,ent=Math.min(.5,life*.17),outro=Math.min(.5,life*.17),ostag=Math.min(.12,life*.05);
items.forEach((l,k)=>{l.t0=N>1?spread*k/(N-1):0;l.o=N>1?k/(N-1):.5;});
let fit=1,laid=false,dead=false;
function measure(l){
  const b=l.mesh.textRenderInfo&&l.mesh.textRenderInfo.blockBounds;
  l.tw0=(b&&b.length===4&&b.every(Number.isFinite))?Math.max(l.fs*.3,b[2]-b[0]):l.fs*Array.from(l.s).length*.95;
  const z=CFG.size(l);
  l.pw0=z[0];l.ph0=z[1];l.pw=l.pw0*l.sc;l.ph=l.ph0*l.sc;
}
function flow(r,k,gap,limit){
  const total=r.reduce((s,l,j)=>s+l.pw+(j?gap:0),0),cap=k>1?total/k*1.15+gap:limit;
  const out=[];let cur=[],x=0;
  for(const l of r){if(cur.length&&x+gap+l.pw>cap){out.push(cur);cur=[];x=0;}x+=(cur.length?gap:0)+l.pw;cur.push(l);}
  out.push(cur);return out;
}
function layout(){
  const gap=em*CFG.gap,vgap=em*CFG.vgap,limit=W*.84;
  const hard=[];for(const l of items)(hard[l.row]=hard[l.row]||[]).push(l);
  const rowsList=hard.filter(Boolean);
  let best=null;
  for(let k=1;k<=6;k++){
    const lines=[];for(const r of rowsList)lines.push(...flow(r,k,gap,1e9));
    const lh=lines.map(r=>Math.max(...r.map(l=>l.ph)));
    let totalH=0;lines.forEach((r,j)=>{totalH+=lh[j]+(j?vgap:0);});
    const maxW=Math.max(1,...lines.map(r=>r.reduce((s,l,j)=>s+l.pw+(j?gap:0),0)));
    const f=Math.min(1,W*.84/maxW,H*.7/Math.max(1,totalH));
    if(!best||f>best.f+.001)best={f,lines,lh,totalH};
  }
  let y=best.totalH/2;
  best.lines.forEach((r,k)=>{
    const w=r.reduce((s,l,j)=>s+l.pw+(j?gap:0),0);
    let x=-w/2;y-=best.lh[k]/2;
    for(const l of r){l.x=x+l.pw/2;l.y=y+(l.r[2]-.5)*l.ph*CFG.wob;x+=l.pw+gap;}
    y-=best.lh[k]/2+vgap;
  });
  fit=best.f;
  root.scale.setScalar(fit);
}
function update(time){
  if(dead)return;
  const t=clamp(Number.isFinite(time)?time:0,0,life);
  let changed=!laid;
  for(const l of items){const inf=l.mesh.textRenderInfo;if(inf&&l.info2!==inf){l.info2=inf;changed=true;}}
  if(changed){for(const l of items)measure(l);layout();laid=true;}
  pose(t);
}
function dispose(){
  if(dead)return;dead=true;
  for(const l of items)for(const m of l.texts){root.remove(m);m.dispose();}
  for(const d of disposables)d.dispose();
  scene.remove(root);
}

const BAND_FRAG=[GLSL_COMMON,
'uniform vec2 u_half;uniform vec2 u_size;uniform float u_alpha;uniform float u_prog;uniform float u_dir;uniform float u_unit;uniform float u_seed;uniform float u_skew;',
'uniform vec3 u_col;',
'void main(){',
'  vec2 p=(vUv-.5)*u_size;',
'  vec2 q=vec2(p.x-u_skew*u_unit*p.y/u_half.y,p.y);',                                   // ペン先の斜めの切り口
'  float w1=(vnoise(vec2(p.x*.05+u_seed*10.,u_seed*3.))-.5)*2.8*u_unit;',
'  float w2=(vnoise(vec2(p.x*.06+u_seed*5.,7.))-.5)*2.8*u_unit;',                      // 手で引いた縁のゆらぎ
'  float top=u_half.y*.94+w1,bot=-u_half.y*.94+w2;',
'  float a=smoothstep(top+.6,top-.6,q.y)*smoothstep(bot-.6,bot+.6,q.y);',
'  float ex=u_half.x*.96;',
'  a*=smoothstep(ex+.7,ex-.7,abs(q.x));',
'  float r=(q.x+u_half.x)/(2.*u_half.x);',
'  if(u_dir<0.)r=1.-r;',
'  a*=smoothstep(u_prog*1.03+.012,u_prog*1.03-.012,r);',
'  float streak=.92+.1*vnoise(vec2(p.x*.02,p.y*.7/u_unit+u_seed*7.));',
'  float pool=1.+.28*smoothstep(.16,0.,r);',                                             // 引き始めはインクが濃い
'  vec3 c=u_col*streak;',
'  gl_FragColor=vec4(c,clamp(a*.86*pool,0.,1.)*u_alpha);',
'}'].join(NL);
const big=1e4;
const pen=new THREE.Group();pen.visible=false;root.add(pen);
const penMats={};
(function(){
  const L=em*1.7,wp=em*.36;
  const poly=pts=>{const sh=new THREE.Shape(pts.map(p=>new THREE.Vector2(p[0],p[1])));const g=new THREE.ShapeGeometry(sh);disposables.push(g);return g;};
  const mat=(c,o)=>{const m=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:c,transparent:true,opacity:o===undefined?1:o,depthTest:false,depthWrite:false});disposables.push(m);return m;};
  const add=(g,m,z)=>{const mesh=new THREE.Mesh(g,m);mesh.renderOrder=10000+z;mesh.frustumCulled=false;pen.add(mesh);return mesh;};
  const o=1.3;
  add(poly([[-o,-wp*.2-o],[-o,wp*.2+o],[wp*.6+o,wp*.5+o],[L+o,wp*.5+o],[L+o,-wp*.5-o],[wp*.6+o,-wp*.5-o]]),mat('#16171d'),0);
  penMats.tip=mat('#fff04d');penMats.stripe=mat('#fff04d');
  add(poly([[0,-wp*.2],[0,wp*.2],[wp*.6,wp*.5],[wp*.6,-wp*.5]]),penMats.tip,1);
  add(poly([[wp*.6,-wp*.5],[wp*.6,wp*.5],[L,wp*.5],[L,-wp*.5]]),mat('#f6f6f1'),1);
  add(poly([[wp*.6,-wp*.5],[wp*.6,wp*.5],[wp*.6+L*.14,wp*.5],[wp*.6+L*.14,-wp*.5]]),penMats.stripe,2);
  add(poly([[L*.9,-wp*.5],[L*.9,wp*.5],[L,wp*.5],[L,-wp*.5]]),mat('#2b2d37'),2);
  pen.rotation.z=.95;pen.scale.setScalar(1);
})();
const penAlpha=()=>{for(const k in penMats)penMats[k].opacity=pen.userData.a;};
function initItem(l){
  l.g=new THREE.Group();root.add(l.g);
  const un=Math.max(.5,l.fs/42);
  // 帯：全面／下線／二度塗り（行って戻る）
  const defs=l.mode===0?[{y:0,h:.9,dir:1,a:0,b:1}]:l.mode===1?[{y:-.24,h:.38,dir:1,a:0,b:1}]:[{y:.1,h:.62,dir:1,a:0,b:.5},{y:-.1,h:.62,dir:-1,a:.5,b:1}];
  l.bands=defs.map((d,k)=>{
    const mat=new THREE.ShaderMaterial({transparent:true,depthTest:false,depthWrite:false,vertexShader:VERT,fragmentShader:BAND_FRAG,
      uniforms:{u_half:{value:new THREE.Vector2(1,1)},u_size:{value:new THREE.Vector2(1,1)},u_alpha:{value:0},u_prog:{value:0},u_dir:{value:d.dir},u_unit:{value:un},
        u_seed:{value:l.r[0]*3+k*1.7},u_skew:{value:(l.r[1]>.5?1:-1)*3.2},u_col:{value:rgb(l.band)}}});
    disposables.push(mat);
    const quad=makeQuad(mat,1,1);quad.renderOrder=l.i*4;l.g.add(quad);disposables.push(quad.geometry);
    return {d,mat,quad};
  });
  l.mesh.renderOrder=l.i*4+2;l.mesh.material.depthTest=false;l.mesh.material.depthWrite=false;
  l.g.add(l.mesh);
  if(l.mode!==1){
    l.dark=createText(l.s,{textStart:l.start,font:l.mesh.font,fontSize:l.fs,anchorX:'center',anchorY:'middle',color:l.hiInk,colorRanges:null});
    l.dark.fillOpacity=0;l.dark.visible=false;l.dark.renderOrder=l.i*4+3;l.dark.material.depthTest=false;l.dark.material.depthWrite=false;
    l.texts.push(l.dark);l.g.add(l.dark);
  }
  l.g.visible=false;
  l.rot0=(l.r[2]-.5)*.05;
}
// 帯の時間割：語ごとに少しずつずらして、ペンが次の語へ移っていく
const slot=N>0?Math.min(.34,life*.42/N):.2,t00=.08;
const startOf=l=>t00+l.i*slot;
const ease=x=>{x=clamp(x);return x*x*(3-2*x)*.5+x*.5};
function tipPoint(l,k,f){      // 語 l の k 番目の帯の上で、進み具合 f（0..1）のペン先の位置
  const b=l.bands[k],d=b.d,x0=-l.pw0/2+l.fs*.1,x1=l.pw0/2-l.fs*.1;
  const x=d.dir>0?mix(x0,x1,f):mix(x1,x0,f);
  return [l.x+x*l.sc,l.y+d.y*l.ph0*l.sc-l.ph0*l.sc*.06];
}
function pose(t){
  const pa=life-t-ostag;
  let last=-1;
  for(const l of items){
    if(!l.pw)continue;
    const s=startOf(l),tau=(t-s)/slot,q=1-clamp((life-t-ostag*(1-l.o))/outro);
    const aIn=smooth((t-(s-.14))*9),a=aIn*(1-smooth((q-.1)/.9));
    l.g.visible=a>.003&&t<life;
    if(!l.g.visible){l.mesh.visible=false;if(l.dark)l.dark.visible=false;continue;}
    l.g.position.set(l.x,l.y+q*q*em*.4,0);
    l.g.rotation.z=l.rot0;
    l.g.scale.setScalar(l.sc);
    const w=l.pw0,h=l.ph0,span=.78;
    let firstEdge=0;
    l.bands.forEach((b,k)=>{
      const d=b.d,bh=d.h*h,mw=l.fs*.1;
      const f=clamp(((tau/span)-d.a)/(d.b-d.a));
      b.quad.position.set(0,d.y*h,0);
      b.quad.scale.set(w+2*mw,bh,1);
      const m=b.mat.uniforms;
      m.u_half.value.set((w+2*mw)/2,bh/2);m.u_size.value.set(w+2*mw,bh);m.u_alpha.value=a;m.u_prog.value=ease(f);
      if(k===0)firstEdge=ease(f);
    });
    // 塗られたところだけ、文字を濃い色に切り替える
    const edge=-w/2+firstEdge*w;
    l.mesh.visible=true;l.mesh.fillOpacity=a;
    if(l.dark){
      l.dark.visible=true;l.dark.fillOpacity=a;
      l.mesh.clipRect=[edge,-big,big,big];l.dark.clipRect=[-big,-big,edge,big];
    }
    if(tau>=0)last=l.i;
  }
  // ペン：いま塗っている語の帯の先にいて、次の語へ移る
  const first=items[0],lastItem=items[N-1];
  if(!first||!first.pw||t>=life){pen.visible=false;return;}
  const tEnd=startOf(lastItem)+slot*.78+.12;
  if(t>tEnd+.2){pen.visible=false;return;}
  let idx=0;
  for(let i=0;i<N;i++)if(t>=startOf(items[i]))idx=i;
  const l=items[idx];
  const tau=(t-startOf(l))/slot;
  let px,py,al=1;
  if(t<startOf(first)){
    const k=smooth(1-(startOf(first)-t)/t00);
    const p0=tipPoint(first,0,0);
    px=p0[0]+(1-k)*em*1.6;py=p0[1]+(1-k)*em*1.3;al=k;
  }else if(tau<.78){
    const f=clamp(tau/.78);
    const kk=l.bands.length;
    const seg=Math.min(kk-1,Math.floor(f*kk)),lf=clamp(f*kk-seg);
    const p=tipPoint(l,seg,ease(lf));
    px=p[0];py=p[1];
  }else{
    const f=smooth((tau-.78)/.22);
    const kk=l.bands.length,pa0=tipPoint(l,kk-1,1);
    if(idx<N-1){const nx=items[idx+1],pb=tipPoint(nx,0,0);px=mix(pa0[0],pb[0],f);py=mix(pa0[1],pb[1],f)+Math.sin(f*Math.PI)*em*.35;}
    else{px=pa0[0]+f*em*1.6;py=pa0[1]+f*em*1.3;al=1-smooth((t-startOf(l)-slot*.78)/.2);}
  }
  const outFade=1-smooth((t-(life-outro))/outro);
  pen.visible=al*outFade>.01;
  pen.position.set(px,py,0);
  pen.userData.a=clamp(al*outFade);
  penMats.tip.color.set(l.band);penMats.stripe.color.set(l.band);
  penAlpha();
}

items.forEach(initItem);
update(0);
return {update,dispose};
文字モーション（コード）10/14 追加

文チケットの半券NEWTICKET-STUB

切手やチケット、ちぎれた半券の紙に文字をのせて、語ごとにぽんと並べます。

メソッド工房の「文字モーション」のサンプルから選びます。

コードを見る
// @fonts
// 語ごとに切手・チケット・ちぎれた半券の紙に文字をのせて、下からぽんと出して並べる。パンチ穴や消印の飾り付き。
const {THREE,scene,createText,text,runs,width,height,fontSize,duration,random,accent,color,fonts,fontsFor,fontURL}=api;
const NL=String.fromCharCode(10);
const clamp=(x,a=0,b=1)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));
const smooth=x=>{x=clamp(x);return x*x*(3-2*x)};
const mix=(a,b,k)=>a+(b-a)*k;
const spring=x=>{x=clamp(x);return 1-(1-x)*Math.exp(-3.4*x)*Math.cos(6.6*x)};
const hash=(a,b)=>{const s=Math.sin(a*127.1+b*311.7)*43758.5453;return s-Math.floor(s)};
const W=Math.max(1,width),H=Math.max(1,height),em=Math.max(8,fontSize||H*.18),life=Math.max(.05,duration);
const hexNum=hex=>{let h=String(hex).trim().replace('#','');if(h.length===3)h=h[0]+h[0]+h[1]+h[1]+h[2]+h[2];return parseInt(h.slice(0,6),16)||0;};
const luma=hex=>{const n=hexNum(hex);return (.299*(n>>16&255)+.587*(n>>8&255)+.114*(n&255))/255};
// シェーダーに渡す色は、色空間の変換をしない生の値で渡す
const rgb=hex=>{const n=hexNum(hex);return new THREE.Vector3((n>>16&255)/255,(n>>8&255)/255,(n&255)/255);};
const PUNCT='、。,.!?！？…「」『』（）()・～〜ー-';
const PART='のはがをにへともやでよねかなてたも';
// 語に当たる run の大きさ・強調・色を、歌詞の文字位置 [start,end) から求める
const runInfo=(start,end)=>{
  let o=0,sum=0,n=0,emph=false,col=null;
  for(const r of runs||[]){
    const len=String(r.text||'').length,a=Math.max(start,o),b=Math.min(end,o+len);
    if(b>a){const s=Number.isFinite(r.scale)&&r.scale>0?r.scale:1;sum+=s*(b-a);n+=b-a;if(r.emphasis)emph=true;if(r.color&&!col)col=r.color;}
    o+=len;
  }
  return {scale:n?sum/n:1,emph,col};
};
// その語の全部の文字を持っている書体だけから選ぶ（なければ選んだ書体）
const pickFont=(s,names,r)=>{
  const cand=[];
  for(const k of names){
    if(fonts&&fonts[k]&&Array.from(s).every(c=>!c.trim()||fontsFor(c).includes(k)))cand.push(fonts[k]);
  }
  cand.push(fontURL);
  return cand[Math.min(cand.length-1,Math.floor(r*cand.length))];
};
const makeQuad=(mat,w,h)=>{const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(w||1,h||1),mat);m.frustumCulled=false;return m;};
const VERT=['varying vec2 vUv;','void main(){vUv=uv;gl_Position=projectionMatrix*modelViewMatrix*vec4(position,1.0);}'].join(NL);
const GLSL_COMMON=[
'precision highp float;',
'varying vec2 vUv;',
'float hash21(vec2 p){return fract(sin(dot(p,vec2(127.1,311.7)))*43758.5453);}',
'float vnoise(vec2 p){vec2 i=floor(p),f=fract(p);f=f*f*(3.-2.*f);',
'  return mix(mix(hash21(i),hash21(i+vec2(1.,0.)),f.x),mix(hash21(i+vec2(0.,1.)),hash21(i+vec2(1.,1.)),f.x),f.y);}',
'float sdRB(vec2 p,vec2 b,float r){vec2 q=abs(p)-b+r;return length(max(q,0.))+min(max(q.x,q.y),0.)-r;}'
].join(NL);

const CFG={cap:7,maxChars:4,fonts:['mincho','round'],gap:.24,vgap:.26,wob:.1,
  pal:[['#f6ecd0','#8a5a2b','#3a2a1a'],['#ffd9a0','#9a4a00','#40210a'],['#cfe8ff','#1f4e8a','#10264a'],['#ffc9d4','#9a2a48','#4a1224'],['#d9f2c2','#2d6a1f','#17340f'],['#fff2a0','#7a6a00','#3a3200']],
  shift:Math.floor(random()*6),kshift:Math.floor(random()*4),
  style(l){
    // 切手(0)・左に半券(1)・右に半券(2)・ちぎれた半券(3)を語ごとに回す。強調語はアクセント色の紙
    l.kind=[0,1,3,2][(l.i+CFG.kshift)%4];
    const given=l.info.col||(l.info.emph?accent:null);
    if(given){l.paper=given;l.ink=luma(given)>.55?'#2a1d12':'#ffffff';l.edge=l.ink;}
    else{const p=CFG.pal[(l.i+CFG.shift)%CFG.pal.length];l.paper=p[0];l.edge=p[1];l.ink=p[2];}
    l.stub=l.kind===0?0:(l.kind===3?l.fs*.45:l.fs*.85);
  },
  size(l){return [l.tw0+l.fs*(l.kind===0?1.1:.95)+l.stub,l.fs*(l.kind===0?1.7:1.55)];}};

// 歌詞を語に区切る（日本語は Intl.Segmenter。助詞と句読点は前の語につけ、長い語は割り、語が多すぎるときはまとめる）
const wordList=(()=>{
  const seg=(typeof Intl!=='undefined'&&Intl.Segmenter)?new Intl.Segmenter('ja',{granularity:'word'}):null;
  const out=[];let off=0;
  String(text||'').split(NL).forEach((line,row)=>{
    const parts=[];
    if(seg){for(const p of seg.segment(line)){if(p.segment.trim())parts.push({a:p.index,b:p.index+p.segment.length});}}
    else{for(let i=0;i<line.length;i+=2)parts.push({a:i,b:Math.min(line.length,i+2)});}
    const m=[];
    for(const p of parts){
      const s=line.slice(p.a,p.b),last=m[m.length-1];
      const isP=Array.from(s).every(c=>PUNCT.includes(c));
      const isJ=s.length===1&&PART.includes(s);
      if(last&&(isP||(isJ&&last.b-last.a<CFG.maxChars)))last.b=p.b;else m.push({a:p.a,b:p.b});
    }
    for(const q of m)out.push({a:q.a,b:q.b,line,row,off});
    off+=line.length+1;
  });
  // run の大きさ・強調・色が変わるところでは語を分ける（私 は 元気 です…と語ごとに大きさを変えられるように）
  const sigAt=pos=>{let o=0;for(const r of runs||[]){const n=String(r.text||'').length;if(pos<o+n)return (Number.isFinite(r.scale)&&r.scale>0?r.scale:1)+'|'+(r.emphasis?1:0)+'|'+(r.color||'');o+=n;}return '1|0|';};
  let list=[];
  for(const q of out){
    let a=q.a;
    for(let i=q.a+1;i<=q.b;i++){
      if(i===q.b||sigAt(q.off+i)!==sigAt(q.off+i-1)){
        const s=q.line.slice(a,i),sig=sigAt(q.off+a);
        if(s.length>CFG.maxChars&&s.charCodeAt(0)>255){
          const k=Math.ceil(s.length/CFG.maxChars),sz=Math.ceil(s.length/k);
          for(let j=0;j<s.length;j+=sz)list.push({...q,a:a+j,b:a+Math.min(s.length,j+sz),sig});
        }else list.push({...q,a,b:i,sig});
        a=i;
      }
    }
  }
  const squash=(strict,limit)=>{
    while(list.length>limit){
      let bi=-1,bl=1e9;
      for(let i=0;i<list.length-1;i++){
        if(list[i].row!==list[i+1].row||(strict&&list[i].sig!==list[i+1].sig))continue;
        const l=list[i+1].b-list[i].a;if(l<bl){bl=l;bi=i;}
      }
      if(bi<0)break;
      list.splice(bi,2,{...list[bi],b:list[bi+1].b});
    }
  };
  squash(true,CFG.cap);
  squash(false,Math.max(CFG.cap,14));
  if(list.length===1&&list[0].b-list[0].a>=4){
    const q=list[0],h=Math.ceil((q.b-q.a)/2);
    list=[{...q,b:q.a+h},{...q,a:q.a+h}];
  }
  return list;
})();
const root=new THREE.Group();scene.add(root);
const disposables=[];
const items=[];
const userScaled=(runs||[]).some(r=>Number.isFinite(r.scale)&&r.scale>0&&r.scale!==1);
wordList.forEach((w,i)=>{
  const s=w.line.slice(w.a,w.b),start=w.off+w.a,info=runInfo(start,start+s.length);
  // 指定がなければ、大・小・大・小…と語ごとに大きさを変える（run の scale があるときはそれを活かす）
  const big=userScaled?1+(random()-.5)*.14:(i%2?.8+.18*random():1.04+.28*random());
  const l={i,s,start,row:w.row,info,sc:info.scale,big,r:[random(),random(),random(),random()]};
  l.fs=em*big;
  CFG.style(l);
  l.mesh=createText(s,{textStart:start,font:pickFont(s,CFG.fonts,l.r[3]),fontSize:l.fs,anchorX:'center',anchorY:'middle',color:l.ink,colorRanges:null});
  l.mesh.fillOpacity=0;l.mesh.visible=false;
  l.texts=[l.mesh];
  items.push(l);
});
const N=items.length;
const spread=N>1?Math.min(life*.3,2.2):0,ent=Math.min(.5,life*.17),outro=Math.min(.5,life*.17),ostag=Math.min(.12,life*.05);
items.forEach((l,k)=>{l.t0=N>1?spread*k/(N-1):0;l.o=N>1?k/(N-1):.5;});
let fit=1,laid=false,dead=false;
function measure(l){
  const b=l.mesh.textRenderInfo&&l.mesh.textRenderInfo.blockBounds;
  l.tw0=(b&&b.length===4&&b.every(Number.isFinite))?Math.max(l.fs*.3,b[2]-b[0]):l.fs*Array.from(l.s).length*.95;
  const z=CFG.size(l);
  l.pw0=z[0];l.ph0=z[1];l.pw=l.pw0*l.sc;l.ph=l.ph0*l.sc;
}
function flow(r,k,gap,limit){
  const total=r.reduce((s,l,j)=>s+l.pw+(j?gap:0),0),cap=k>1?total/k*1.15+gap:limit;
  const out=[];let cur=[],x=0;
  for(const l of r){if(cur.length&&x+gap+l.pw>cap){out.push(cur);cur=[];x=0;}x+=(cur.length?gap:0)+l.pw;cur.push(l);}
  out.push(cur);return out;
}
function layout(){
  const gap=em*CFG.gap,vgap=em*CFG.vgap,limit=W*.84;
  const hard=[];for(const l of items)(hard[l.row]=hard[l.row]||[]).push(l);
  const rowsList=hard.filter(Boolean);
  let best=null;
  for(let k=1;k<=6;k++){
    const lines=[];for(const r of rowsList)lines.push(...flow(r,k,gap,1e9));
    const lh=lines.map(r=>Math.max(...r.map(l=>l.ph)));
    let totalH=0;lines.forEach((r,j)=>{totalH+=lh[j]+(j?vgap:0);});
    const maxW=Math.max(1,...lines.map(r=>r.reduce((s,l,j)=>s+l.pw+(j?gap:0),0)));
    const f=Math.min(1,W*.84/maxW,H*.7/Math.max(1,totalH));
    if(!best||f>best.f+.001)best={f,lines,lh,totalH};
  }
  let y=best.totalH/2;
  best.lines.forEach((r,k)=>{
    const w=r.reduce((s,l,j)=>s+l.pw+(j?gap:0),0);
    let x=-w/2;y-=best.lh[k]/2;
    for(const l of r){l.x=x+l.pw/2;l.y=y+(l.r[2]-.5)*l.ph*CFG.wob;x+=l.pw+gap;}
    y-=best.lh[k]/2+vgap;
  });
  fit=best.f;
  root.scale.setScalar(fit);
}
function update(time){
  if(dead)return;
  const t=clamp(Number.isFinite(time)?time:0,0,life);
  let changed=!laid;
  for(const l of items){const inf=l.mesh.textRenderInfo;if(inf&&l.info2!==inf){l.info2=inf;changed=true;}}
  if(changed){for(const l of items)measure(l);layout();laid=true;}
  pose(t);
}
function dispose(){
  if(dead)return;dead=true;
  for(const l of items)for(const m of l.texts){root.remove(m);m.dispose();}
  for(const d of disposables)d.dispose();
  scene.remove(root);
}

const TICKET_FRAG=[GLSL_COMMON,
'uniform vec2 u_half;uniform vec2 u_size;uniform float u_alpha;uniform float u_unit;uniform float u_seed;uniform float u_kind;uniform float u_stub;',
'uniform float u_lift;uniform float u_punch;uniform vec3 u_paper;uniform vec3 u_ink;',
'float sdBox(vec2 p,vec2 b){vec2 q=abs(p)-b;return length(max(q,0.))+min(max(q.x,q.y),0.);}',
'float shape(vec2 p){',
'  vec2 h=u_half;float U=u_unit;',
'  float d=sdRB(p,h,1.5*U);',
'  if(u_kind<.5){',                                                  // 切手：縁をぐるりと丸く抜く
'    vec2 n=max(floor(2.*h/(7.*U)+.5),vec2(3.));',
'    vec2 pp=2.*h/n;',
'    float r=min(pp.x,pp.y)*.33;',
'    float qx=mod(p.x+h.x,pp.x)-pp.x*.5;',
'    float qy=mod(p.y+h.y,pp.y)-pp.y*.5;',
'    float c1=length(vec2(qx,abs(p.y)-h.y))-r;',
'    float c2=length(vec2(abs(p.x)-h.x,qy))-r;',
'    d=max(sdBox(p,h),-min(c1,c2));',
'  }else{',
'    float rn=h.y*.26;',
'    if(u_kind<2.5){',
'      float nl=length(p-vec2(-h.x,0.))-rn,nr=length(p-vec2(h.x,0.))-rn;',     // 左右の半円の切り欠き
'      d=max(d,-min(nl,nr));',
'    }',
'    if(u_kind>2.5){',                                                  // ちぎれたギザギザの縁
'      float amp=3.*U,per=6.5*U;',
'      float zz=abs(fract(p.y/per)-.5)*2.*amp;',
'      d=max(d,(-h.x+zz)-p.x);',
'    }',
'    if(u_kind>.5&&u_kind<2.5){',                                       // ミシン目
'      float sx=u_kind<1.5?-h.x+u_stub:h.x-u_stub;',
'      float qy=mod(p.y+h.y,5.*U)-2.5*U;',
'      float hole=length(vec2(p.x-sx,qy))-1.05*U;',
'      hole=max(hole,abs(p.y)-(h.y-3.*U));',
'      d=max(d,-hole);',
'    }',
'    if(u_kind>.5&&u_punch>0.){',                                       // 改札のパンチ穴
'      float cx=u_kind<1.5?-h.x+u_stub*.5:(u_kind<2.5?h.x-u_stub*.5:-h.x+u_stub*.5+3.*U);',
'      float R=min(u_stub*.5,h.y*.55)*.5*u_punch;',
'      d=max(d,-(length(p-vec2(cx,0.))-R));',
'    }',
'  }',
'  return d;',
'}',
'void main(){',
'  vec2 p=(vUv-.5)*u_size;',
'  vec2 h=u_half;float U=u_unit;',
'  float d=shape(p);',
'  float a=1.-smoothstep(-.7,.7,d);',
'  float gy=p.y/h.y*.5+.5;',
'  vec3 col=u_paper*(.96+.07*gy)*(.975+.05*vnoise(p*.6+u_seed*9.));',
'  float ln=0.;',
'  float ins=4.2*U;',
'  float l1=1.-smoothstep(0.,.9*U,abs(sdRB(p,h-vec2(ins),U))-.45*U);',
'  ln=max(ln,l1);',
'  if(u_kind<.5){',
'    float l2=1.-smoothstep(0.,.9*U,abs(sdRB(p,h-vec2(ins+3.2*U),U))-.3*U);',
'    ln=max(ln,l2*.8);',
'    float inner=1.-smoothstep(-.6,.6,sdRB(p,h-vec2(ins+3.2*U),U));',
'    col=mix(col,mix(u_paper,vec3(1.),.45),inner*.9);',               // 絵柄の面は少し明るく
'    vec2 c=vec2(h.x*.55,-h.y*.2);float Rm=min(h.x,h.y)*.62;',        // 消印：円と波線
'    float ring=1.-smoothstep(0.,.9*U,abs(length(p-c)-Rm)-.5*U);',
'    float ring2=1.-smoothstep(0.,.9*U,abs(length(p-c)-Rm*.78)-.3*U);',
'    float wl=0.;',
'    for(int k=0;k<4;k++){float fk=float(k);float yy=c.y+(fk-1.5)*3.3*U+sin(p.x*.32/U+fk*1.3)*1.2*U;',
'      wl=max(wl,(1.-smoothstep(0.,.8*U,abs(p.y-yy)-.45*U))*step(p.x,c.x-Rm*.9)*step(c.x-Rm-h.x*.9,p.x));}',
'    col=mix(col,u_ink,max(max(ring,ring2*.8),wl)*.38);',
'  }else{',
'    float sx=u_kind<1.5?-h.x+u_stub:(u_kind<2.5?h.x-u_stub:-h.x+u_stub);',
'    float inStub=u_kind<2.5&&u_kind>.5?(u_kind<1.5?step(p.x,sx-1.5*U):step(sx+1.5*U,p.x)):step(p.x,sx);',
'    float bidx=floor(p.y/(1.9*U));',
'    float bar=step(.42,hash21(vec2(bidx,u_seed*13.)))*step(abs(p.y),h.y-6.*U);',
'    float bx0=u_kind<1.5?-h.x+3.5*U:(u_kind<2.5?h.x-u_stub+3.5*U:-h.x+5.*U);',
'    float bx1=u_kind<1.5?-h.x+u_stub-4.5*U:(u_kind<2.5?h.x-3.5*U:-h.x+u_stub-1.*U);',
'    float inBar=step(bx0,p.x)*step(p.x,bx1);',
'    if(u_kind<3.){ln=max(ln,bar*inBar*inStub*.9);}',
'    else{float dash=step(abs(p.y-h.y*.34),.5*U)+step(abs(p.y+h.y*.34),.5*U);ln=max(ln,dash*inBar*.8);}',
'    float tear=1.-smoothstep(0.,1.*U,abs(p.x-sx)-.3*U);',            // 半券との境の実線
'    if(u_kind>2.5)ln=max(ln,tear*.0);',
'    float rimLeft=0.;',
'  }',
'  col=mix(col,u_ink,ln*.82);',
'  float sh=(1.-smoothstep(-2.*U-u_lift*6.*U,2.*U+u_lift*6.*U,shape(p-vec2(1.4+u_lift*4.,-2.6-u_lift*8.)*U)))*(.36-.12*u_lift);',
'  float oa=a+sh*(1.-a);',
'  vec3 rgb=oa>.001?col*a/oa:vec3(0.);',
'  gl_FragColor=vec4(rgb,oa*u_alpha);',
'}'].join(NL);
const MARGIN=Math.round(Math.max(24,em*.75));
function initItem(l){
  l.outer=new THREE.Group();root.add(l.outer);
  const un=Math.max(.5,l.fs/42);
  l.mat=new THREE.ShaderMaterial({transparent:true,depthTest:false,depthWrite:false,vertexShader:VERT,fragmentShader:TICKET_FRAG,
    uniforms:{u_half:{value:new THREE.Vector2(1,1)},u_size:{value:new THREE.Vector2(1,1)},u_alpha:{value:0},u_unit:{value:un},u_seed:{value:l.r[0]},
      u_kind:{value:l.kind},u_stub:{value:l.stub},u_lift:{value:0},u_punch:{value:0},u_paper:{value:rgb(l.paper)},u_ink:{value:rgb(l.edge)}}});
  disposables.push(l.mat);
  l.quad=makeQuad(l.mat,1,1);l.quad.renderOrder=l.i*4;l.outer.add(l.quad);disposables.push(l.quad.geometry);
  l.mesh.renderOrder=l.i*4+1;l.mesh.material.depthTest=false;l.mesh.material.depthWrite=false;
  l.mesh.position.x=l.kind===1||l.kind===3?l.stub/2:(l.kind===2?-l.stub/2:0);
  l.outer.add(l.mesh);
  l.outer.visible=false;
  l.rot0=(l.r[1]-.5)*.14;
  l.dir=l.i%2?1:-1;
}
function pose(t){
  for(const l of items){
    if(!l.pw)continue;
    const u=clamp((t-l.t0)/ent),q=1-clamp((life-t-ostag*(1-l.o))/outro);
    const a=smooth(u*7)*(1-smooth((q-.2)/.8));
    l.outer.visible=a>.003&&t<life;
    if(!l.outer.visible){l.mesh.visible=false;continue;}
    const e=spring(u);                                         // 下から出てきて、ぽんと弾んで止まる
    const rise=1-Math.min(1.15,e);
    const lift=clamp(1-u*1.6)+q*.8;
    const s=1+.25*rise*(rise>0?1:0)+.04*q;
    l.outer.position.set(l.x+q*q*l.dir*W*.16/fit,l.y-rise*H*.2/fit+q*q*H*.12/fit,0);
    l.outer.rotation.z=l.rot0+rise*l.dir*.35+q*q*l.dir*.7;
    l.outer.scale.setScalar(l.sc*s);
    const w=l.pw0,h=l.ph0;
    l.quad.scale.set(w+2*MARGIN,h+2*MARGIN,1);
    const m=l.mat.uniforms;
    m.u_half.value.set(w/2,h/2);m.u_size.value.set(w+2*MARGIN,h+2*MARGIN);m.u_alpha.value=a;m.u_lift.value=lift;
    m.u_punch.value=smooth((u-.72)*4)*(1-q);
    l.mesh.visible=true;l.mesh.fillOpacity=a*smooth((u-.08)*8);
  }
}

items.forEach(initItem);
update(0);
return {update,dispose};
文字モーション（コード）10/14 追加

文ぺたっと貼るステッカーNEWSTICKER-STICK

語ごとに縁と影のあるステッカーになって、上からペタッと貼られて少し跳ねます。

メソッド工房の「文字モーション」のサンプルから選びます。

コードを見る
// 語ごとに白いダイカットの縁と影を持つステッカーになって、上からペタッと貼られる。色・大きさ・傾き・めくれた角・きらっと光る筋が語ごとに違う
const MAXWORDS=9;
const {THREE,scene,createText,text,width,height,fontSize,duration,random,runs,accent}=api;
const clamp=(x,a=0,b=1)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));
const smooth=x=>{x=clamp(x);return x*x*(3-2*x)};
const spring=x=>{x=clamp(x);return 1-(1-x)*Math.exp(-3.4*x)*Math.cos(6.6*x)};
const back=x=>{x=clamp(x)-1;return 1+2.2*x*x*x+1.2*x*x};
const mix=(a,b,k)=>a+(b-a)*k;
const hash=(a,b)=>{const s=Math.sin(a*127.1+b*311.7)*43758.5453;return s-Math.floor(s)};
const W=Math.max(1,width),H=Math.max(1,height),em=Math.max(8,fontSize||H*.18),life=Math.max(.05,duration);
const outro=Math.min(.5,life*.18);
const root=new THREE.Group();scene.add(root);
const lum=c=>{try{const n=new THREE.Color(c).getHex();return (.299*(n>>16&255)+.587*(n>>8&255)+.114*(n&255))/255}catch(e){return .5}};
const shade=(c,to,k)=>{const a=new THREE.Color(c);a.lerp(new THREE.Color(to),k);return '#'+a.getHexString()};
const V=(x,y)=>new THREE.Vector2(x,y);
// 同じ書式（強調・色・大きさ）の中か
function runAtOff(o){let a=0;for(const r of runs||[]){const n=String(r.text||'').length;if(o<a+n)return r;a+=n;}return null;}
function sameRun(a,b){const x=runAtOff(a)||{},y=runAtOff(b)||{};return !!x.emphasis===!!y.emphasis&&(x.color||'')===(y.color||'')&&(x.scale||1)===(y.scale||1);}
// 歌詞を語に区切る（日本語は Intl.Segmenter。句読点は前の語につける。多すぎる語はまとめ、1語だけなら半分に割る）
function splitWords(maxWords){
  const seg=(typeof Intl!=='undefined'&&Intl.Segmenter)?new Intl.Segmenter('ja',{granularity:'word'}):null;
  const out=[];let off=0;
  String(text||'').replace(/\r/g,'').split('\n').forEach((line,row)=>{
    const parts=[];
    if(seg){for(const p of seg.segment(line)){if(p.segment.trim())parts.push({s:p.segment,start:p.index});}}
    else{for(let i=0;i<line.length;i+=2)parts.push({s:line.slice(i,i+2),start:i});}
    const merged=[];
    for(const p of parts){
      const last=merged[merged.length-1];
      if(last&&/^[、。,.!?！？…「」『』（）()・～〜ー]+$/.test(p.s))last.s+=p.s;
      else if(last&&/^[ぁ-ゟー]{1,3}$/.test(p.s)&&last.s.length+p.s.length<=6&&!/[、。,.!?！？…]$/.test(last.s)&&last.start+last.s.length===p.start&&sameRun(last.start+off,p.start+off))last.s+=p.s;   // 助詞や語尾は前の語につける
      else merged.push({...p});
    }
    for(const m of merged)out.push({s:m.s,start:off+m.start,row});
    off+=line.length+1;
  });
  let list=out;
  if(list.length>maxWords){const c=Math.ceil(list.length/maxWords),m=[];for(let i=0;i<list.length;i+=c){const g=list.slice(i,i+c);if(g.every(x=>x.row===g[0].row))m.push({s:g.map(x=>x.s).join(''),start:g[0].start,row:g[0].row});else g.forEach(x=>m.push(x));}list=m;}
  if(list.length===1&&Array.from(list[0].s).length>=2){const a=Array.from(list[0].s),h=Math.ceil(a.length/2),f=a.slice(0,h).join('');list=[{s:f,start:list[0].start,row:list[0].row},{s:a.slice(h).join(''),start:list[0].start+f.length,row:list[0].row}];}
  return list;
}
// 語の強調・色・大きさ（run の scale）を調べる
function runInfo(start,len){
  let o=0,emph=false,col=null,ws=0,ss=0;
  for(const r of runs||[]){
    const n=String(r.text||'').length,a=Math.max(o,start),b=Math.min(o+n,start+len);
    if(b>a){const sc=Number.isFinite(r.scale)&&r.scale>0?r.scale:1;let w=0;for(const ch of Array.from(String(text).slice(a,b)))w+=ch.codePointAt(0)<256?.6:1;w=Math.max(.3,w);ws+=w;ss+=w*sc;if(r.emphasis||r.color)emph=true;if(r.color&&!col)col=r.color;}
    o+=n;if(o>=start+len)break;
  }
  return {emph,col,scale:ws?ss/ws:1};
}
const SIZES=[1.16,.86,1.04,.9,1.22,.92];
const items=splitWords(MAXWORDS).map((w,i)=>{
  const inf=runInfo(w.start,w.s.length);
  return {...w,i,emph:inf.emph,col:inf.col,rs:clamp(inf.scale,.4,3),sz:SIZES[i%SIZES.length]*(.93+random()*.14)*(inf.emph?1.08:1),tw:0,th:0,bw:0,bh:0,x:0,y:0,dy:0,info:null,fx:[]};
});
const nWords=items.length,spread=Math.min(life*.25,(nWords-1)*.26);
items.forEach(it=>{it.t0=nWords>1?spread*it.i/(nWords-1):0;});
// 語の文字メッシュ（強調語の色・縁取り・中抜き・語の大きさは createText が textStart から自動で効かせる）
function makeText(it,ink,extra){
  const opt={textStart:it.start,fontSize:em*it.sz,anchorX:'center',anchorY:'middle',...(extra||{})};if(ink)opt.color=ink;
  const m=createText(it.s,opt);
  m.fillOpacity=0;m.outlineOpacity=0;m.renderOrder=it.i*16+14;
  if(m.material){m.material.depthTest=false;m.material.depthWrite=false;}
  it.mesh=m;return m;
}
function rrPts(w,h,r,cut,cs){
  r=Math.max(.01,Math.min(r,w/2,h/2));const hw=w/2,hh=h/2,P=[];
  const arc=(cx,cy,a0)=>{for(let k=0;k<=6;k++){const a=a0+k*Math.PI/12;P.push(V(cx+r*Math.cos(a),cy+r*Math.sin(a)));}};
  arc(hw-r,-hh+r,-Math.PI/2);
  if(cut>0){P.push(V(hw,hh-cut),V(hw-cut,hh));}else arc(hw-r,hh-r,0);
  arc(-hw+r,hh-r,Math.PI/2);
  arc(-hw+r,-hh+r,Math.PI);
  if(cs<0)for(const p of P)p.x=-p.x;
  return P;
}
const rrGeo=(w,h,r,cut,cs)=>new THREE.ShapeGeometry(new THREE.Shape(rrPts(w,h,r,cut,cs)));
function starGeo(R,r,n,rot){const P=[];for(let k=0;k<n*2;k++){const a=rot+k*Math.PI/n,rr=k%2?r:R;P.push(V(Math.cos(a)*rr,Math.sin(a)*rr));}return new THREE.ShapeGeometry(new THREE.Shape(P));}
// 筆の線：点列を幅つきの帯にして、描画範囲（drawRange）で一筆書きのように伸ばす
function ribbonGeo(pts,wf){
  const n=pts.length,pos=new Float32Array(n*6),idx=[];
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
    const a=pts[Math.max(0,i-1)],b=pts[Math.min(n-1,i+1)];
    let dx=b.x-a.x,dy=b.y-a.y;const l=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;dx/=l;dy/=l;
    const w=wf(i/(n-1),i)/2,nx=-dy*w,ny=dx*w;
    pos.set([pts[i].x+nx,pts[i].y+ny,0,pts[i].x-nx,pts[i].y-ny,0],i*6);
    if(i<n-1){const k=i*2;idx.push(k,k+1,k+2,k+1,k+3,k+2);}
  }
  const g=new THREE.BufferGeometry();g.setAttribute('position',new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos,3));g.setIndex(idx);g.userData.segs=n-1;return g;
}
const drawTo=(m,p)=>{const g=m.geometry,s=g.userData.segs||0;g.setDrawRange(0,Math.floor(s*clamp(p))*6);m.visible=p>.001;};
const mkMat=(c,o=1)=>new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:c,transparent:true,opacity:o,depthTest:false,depthWrite:false,side:THREE.DoubleSide});
// 装飾メッシュ。it.fx に不透明度の基準を覚えさせ、まとめて薄くできる
function deco(it,geo,c,o,order){
  const m=new THREE.Mesh(geo,mkMat(c,o==null?1:o));m.renderOrder=it.i*16+order;it.fx.push([m.material,o==null?1:o]);return m;
}
function regeo(m,geo){if(m.geometry)m.geometry.dispose();m.geometry=geo;}
function fade(it,a){for(const f of it.fx)f[0].opacity=f[1]*a;}
function measure(it){
  const r=it.mesh&&it.mesh.textRenderInfo,b=r&&r.blockBounds;
  let base;
  if(b&&b.length===4&&b.every(Number.isFinite))base=Math.max(em*it.sz*.3,b[2]-b[0]);
  else{let n=0;for(const ch of Array.from(it.s))n+=ch.codePointAt(0)<256?.6:1;base=em*it.sz*n;}
  it.tw=base*it.rs;it.th=em*it.sz*1.12*Math.max(.7,it.rs);
}
let fit=1,laid=false,dead=false,FITW=.86,FITH=.7,GAPX=.22,GAPY=.1,boxW=1,boxH=1;
function layout(){
  for(const it of items){measure(it);size(it);}
  const gap=em*GAPX,limit=W*FITW,rows=[],hard=[];
  for(const it of items)(hard[it.row]=hard[it.row]||[]).push(it);
  for(const row of hard){if(!row)continue;
    const total=row.reduce((s,l,k)=>s+l.bw+(k?gap:0),0),n=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(total/limit)),cap=n>1?Math.min(limit,total/n*1.12+gap):limit;
    let cur=[],x=0;
    for(const l of row){if(cur.length&&x+gap+l.bw>cap){rows.push(cur);cur=[];x=0;}x+=(cur.length?gap:0)+l.bw;cur.push(l);}
    rows.push(cur);}
  const rowH=rows.map(r=>Math.max(...r.map(l=>l.bh))),vgap=em*GAPY,totH=rowH.reduce((a,h)=>a+h,0)+vgap*(rows.length-1);
  let y=totH/2,maxW=1;
  rows.forEach((row,r)=>{
    let x=0;for(const l of row){l.x=x+l.bw/2;x+=l.bw+gap;}
    const tw=x-gap;maxW=Math.max(maxW,tw);
    const cy=y-rowH[r]/2;for(const l of row){l.x-=tw/2;l.y=cy+l.dy;}
    y-=rowH[r]+vgap;
  });
  fit=Math.min(1,W*FITW/maxW,H*FITH/totH);root.scale.setScalar(fit);boxW=maxW;boxH=totH;
  if(typeof afterLayout==='function')afterLayout();
}
// ---- ステッカー ----
const PAL=['#ffd23f','#ff6f9c','#4fd8ff','#7cf29a','#ff9f43','#b79cff','#fff1cf','#2b2f55'];
const DARK='#1c1a2b',DIE='#fffdf6';
const quadGeo=()=>{const g=new THREE.BufferGeometry();g.setAttribute('position',new THREE.BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(12),3));g.setIndex([0,1,2,0,2,3]);return g;};
for(const it of items){
  const accentCol=it.col||accent;
  it.face=it.emph?(lum(accentCol)>.55?'#2b2f55':'#fff1cf'):PAL[(it.i*3+(random()<.5?0:1))%PAL.length];
  const ink=it.emph?it.face==='#2b2f55'?'#ffffff':DARK:(lum(it.face)>.55?DARK:'#ffffff');
  it.type=(it.i+Math.floor(random()*2))%3;it.cs=random()<.5?1:-1;
  it.tilt=(random()-.5)*.13;it.spin=(random()<.5?-1:1)*(.35+random()*.3);
  it.dy=(random()-.5)*em*.16;
  const g=new THREE.Group();g.visible=false;root.add(g);it.g=g;
  it.shadow=deco(it,new THREE.BufferGeometry(),'#0b0d16',.36,0);
  it.die=deco(it,new THREE.BufferGeometry(),DIE,1,1);
  it.base=deco(it,new THREE.BufferGeometry(),shade(it.face,'#000000',.2),1,2);
  it.panel=deco(it,new THREE.BufferGeometry(),it.face,1,3);
  it.dots=deco(it,new THREE.BufferGeometry(),shade(it.face,'#ffffff',.38),1,4);
  it.shine=deco(it,quadGeo(),'#ffffff',.55,5);
  it.flap=deco(it,new THREE.BufferGeometry(),'#ece6d3',1,6);
  g.add(it.shadow,it.die,it.base,it.panel,it.dots,it.shine,it.flap);
  if(it.emph){
    it.starOut=deco(it,new THREE.BufferGeometry(),DARK,1,7);it.star=deco(it,new THREE.BufferGeometry(),'#ffd23f',1,8);
    g.add(it.starOut,it.star);
  }
  const tx=makeText(it,ink);g.add(tx);
}
function size(it){
  const sz=it.sz,b=em*.09*sz,padX=em*.36*sz,padY=em*.2*sz,cs=it.cs;
  const fw=Math.max(it.tw+2*padX,em*sz*1.3),fh=it.th+2*padY,r=it.type===1?fh/2:it.type===0?fh*.3:fh*.13;
  const cut=Math.min(fh*.36,em*.34*sz),e=fh*.075;
  Object.assign(it,{fw,fh,b,cut,r});
  regeo(it.shadow,rrGeo(fw+2*b,fh+2*b,r+b,cut+b*.586,cs));
  regeo(it.die,rrGeo(fw+2*b,fh+2*b,r+b,cut+b*.586,cs));
  regeo(it.base,rrGeo(fw,fh,r,cut,cs));
  const ph=fh-2*e-fh*.13;
  regeo(it.panel,rrGeo(fw-2*e,ph,Math.max(.01,r-e),Math.max(0,cut-e*.586),cs));
  it.panel.position.y=fh*.065;
  // 水玉・しま
  const shapes=[];
  if(it.type===0){for(const [sx,y] of [[-1,.2],[-1,-.2],[1,-.2],[1,.2]]){if(sx*it.cs>0&&y>0)continue;shapes.push(new THREE.Shape(new THREE.Path().absarc(sx*(fw/2-fh*.15),y*fh,fh*.05,0,Math.PI*2,false).getPoints(10)));}}
  else if(it.type===1){for(let k=-3;k<=3;k++){const x=k*fh*.34;shapes.push(new THREE.Shape([V(x-fh*.07-fh*.2,-fh*.42),V(x+fh*.07-fh*.2,-fh*.42),V(x+fh*.07+fh*.2,fh*.42),V(x-fh*.07+fh*.2,fh*.42)].map(p=>V(clamp(p.x,-fw/2+r*.9,fw/2-r*.9),p.y))));}}
  if(shapes.length)regeo(it.dots,new THREE.ShapeGeometry(shapes));else regeo(it.dots,new THREE.BufferGeometry());
  it.dots.position.y=fh*.065;
  // めくれた角
  const hw=fw/2,hh=fh/2,s=cs,A=V(s*(hw-cut),hh),B=V(s*hw,hh-cut),C=V(s*(hw-cut),hh-cut),M=V((A.x+B.x)/2,(A.y+B.y)/2);
  const L=cut/Math.SQRT2,d=cut/Math.SQRT2;
  regeo(it.flap,new THREE.ShapeGeometry(new THREE.Shape([V(0,-d),V(0,d),V(L,0)])));
  it.flap.position.set(M.x,M.y,0);it.flap.rotation.z=Math.atan2(C.y-M.y,C.x-M.x);
  if(it.star){
    const R=em*.25*sz;
    regeo(it.starOut,starGeo(R*1.18,R*.56,5,Math.PI/2));regeo(it.star,starGeo(R,R*.45,5,Math.PI/2));
    it.starOut.position.set(-s*(hw+b*.3),hh+b*.3,0);it.star.position.copy(it.starOut.position);
    it.starR=R;
  }
  const sm=it.star?it.starR*.8:0;
  it.bw=fw+2*b+em*.1+sm;it.bh=fh+2*b+em*.1+sm*.8;
}
function pose(it,t,lt,q){
  const g=it.g;
  if(lt<0||q<=0.001){g.visible=false;it.mesh.visible=false;return;}
  g.visible=true;
  const L=.2,u=clamp(lt/L),land=Math.max(0,lt-L),a=smooth(lt/.06)*q;
  let s=lt<L?mix(2.0,.88,u*u):1-.12*Math.exp(-9*land)*Math.cos(14*land);
  const k=lt<L?0:Math.exp(-10*land)*Math.cos(17*land);
  let rot=it.tilt+(1-smooth(u))*it.spin+(lt<L?0:.04*Math.exp(-7*land)*Math.sin(19*land)*(it.i%2?1:-1));
  const ex=1-q;s*=1-.25*ex;rot+=it.spin*.5*ex;
  g.position.set(it.x,it.y-em*.18*ex,0);g.rotation.z=rot;g.scale.set(s*(1+.08*k),s*(1-.08*k),1);
  // 影：貼る前は遠くて薄く、貼ると縮んで濃くなる
  const near=smooth(u);
  it.shadow.position.set(it.b*mix(3.2,.7,near),-it.b*mix(4.4,1.1,near),0);
  it.shadow.scale.setScalar(mix(1.08,1,near));
  fade(it,a);it.shadow.material.opacity=.36*a*mix(.35,1,near);
  // きらっと光る筋
  const p=(t-(it.t0+.5+it.i*.05))/.55,sh=it.shine;
  if(p>0&&p<1&&a>.01){
    const fw=it.fw,fh=it.fh,cx=mix(-fw/2-fh*.3,fw/2+fh*.3,p),bwid=fh*.36,sk=fh*.45,lim=fw/2-it.r*.9,yy=fh/2-fh*.075;
    const lo=-lim-(it.cs<0?it.cut*.8:0),hi=lim-(it.cs>0?it.cut*.8:0);
    const c=v=>clamp(v,-lim,lim),ct=v=>clamp(v,lo,hi);
    const pos=sh.geometry.attributes.position.array;
    const pts=[[ct(cx-bwid/2+sk/2),yy],[ct(cx+bwid/2+sk/2),yy],[c(cx+bwid/2-sk/2),-yy],[c(cx-bwid/2-sk/2),-yy]];
    pts.forEach((v,j)=>{pos[j*3]=v[0];pos[j*3+1]=v[1];pos[j*3+2]=0;});
    sh.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate=true;sh.visible=true;sh.material.opacity=.55*a*Math.sin(Math.PI*p);
  }else sh.visible=false;
  it.flap.scale.x=1+.14*Math.sin(t*3.1+it.i*1.7);
  if(it.star){it.star.rotation.z=it.starOut.rotation.z=.25*Math.sin(t*2.6+it.i);}
  it.mesh.visible=a>.002;it.mesh.fillOpacity=a*smooth((lt-.06)/.1);it.mesh.outlineOpacity=it.mesh.fillOpacity;
}
function update(time){
  if(dead)return;
  const t=clamp(Number.isFinite(time)?time:0,0,life);
  let changed=!laid;
  for(const it of items){const r=it.mesh&&it.mesh.textRenderInfo;if(r&&it.info!==r){it.info=r;changed=true;}}
  if(changed){layout();laid=true;}
  const q=smooth(clamp((life-t)/outro));
  for(const it of items)pose(it,t,t-it.t0,q);
  if(typeof frame==='function')frame(t,q);
}
function dispose(){
  if(dead)return;dead=true;
  root.traverse(o=>{
    if(typeof o.dispose==='function'){o.dispose();return;}
    if(o.geometry)o.geometry.dispose();
    if(o.material&&o.material.dispose)o.material.dispose();
  });
  scene.remove(root);
}
update(0);
return {update,dispose};
文字モーション（コード）10/14 追加

文ぽこぽこ膨らむ吹き出しNEWBUBBLE-POP

語ごとに大きさの違う吹き出しが、しっぽの先からぽこぽこ膨らんで現れます。

メソッド工房の「文字モーション」のサンプルから選びます。

コードを見る
// 語ごとに吹き出し（まる・角丸・もくもく雲・とげとげ）になって、しっぽの先からぽこぽこ膨らんで現れる。大小・色・しっぽの向きが語ごとに違い、膨らんだ後は息をするように揺れる
const MAXWORDS=8;
const {THREE,scene,createText,text,width,height,fontSize,duration,random,runs,accent}=api;
const clamp=(x,a=0,b=1)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));
const smooth=x=>{x=clamp(x);return x*x*(3-2*x)};
const spring=x=>{x=clamp(x);return 1-(1-x)*Math.exp(-3.4*x)*Math.cos(6.6*x)};
const back=x=>{x=clamp(x)-1;return 1+2.2*x*x*x+1.2*x*x};
const mix=(a,b,k)=>a+(b-a)*k;
const hash=(a,b)=>{const s=Math.sin(a*127.1+b*311.7)*43758.5453;return s-Math.floor(s)};
const W=Math.max(1,width),H=Math.max(1,height),em=Math.max(8,fontSize||H*.18),life=Math.max(.05,duration);
const outro=Math.min(.5,life*.18);
const root=new THREE.Group();scene.add(root);
const lum=c=>{try{const n=new THREE.Color(c).getHex();return (.299*(n>>16&255)+.587*(n>>8&255)+.114*(n&255))/255}catch(e){return .5}};
const shade=(c,to,k)=>{const a=new THREE.Color(c);a.lerp(new THREE.Color(to),k);return '#'+a.getHexString()};
const V=(x,y)=>new THREE.Vector2(x,y);
// 同じ書式（強調・色・大きさ）の中か
function runAtOff(o){let a=0;for(const r of runs||[]){const n=String(r.text||'').length;if(o<a+n)return r;a+=n;}return null;}
function sameRun(a,b){const x=runAtOff(a)||{},y=runAtOff(b)||{};return !!x.emphasis===!!y.emphasis&&(x.color||'')===(y.color||'')&&(x.scale||1)===(y.scale||1);}
// 歌詞を語に区切る（日本語は Intl.Segmenter。句読点は前の語につける。多すぎる語はまとめ、1語だけなら半分に割る）
function splitWords(maxWords){
  const seg=(typeof Intl!=='undefined'&&Intl.Segmenter)?new Intl.Segmenter('ja',{granularity:'word'}):null;
  const out=[];let off=0;
  String(text||'').replace(/\r/g,'').split('\n').forEach((line,row)=>{
    const parts=[];
    if(seg){for(const p of seg.segment(line)){if(p.segment.trim())parts.push({s:p.segment,start:p.index});}}
    else{for(let i=0;i<line.length;i+=2)parts.push({s:line.slice(i,i+2),start:i});}
    const merged=[];
    for(const p of parts){
      const last=merged[merged.length-1];
      if(last&&/^[、。,.!?！？…「」『』（）()・～〜ー]+$/.test(p.s))last.s+=p.s;
      else if(last&&/^[ぁ-ゟー]{1,3}$/.test(p.s)&&last.s.length+p.s.length<=6&&!/[、。,.!?！？…]$/.test(last.s)&&last.start+last.s.length===p.start&&sameRun(last.start+off,p.start+off))last.s+=p.s;   // 助詞や語尾は前の語につける
      else merged.push({...p});
    }
    for(const m of merged)out.push({s:m.s,start:off+m.start,row});
    off+=line.length+1;
  });
  let list=out;
  if(list.length>maxWords){const c=Math.ceil(list.length/maxWords),m=[];for(let i=0;i<list.length;i+=c){const g=list.slice(i,i+c);if(g.every(x=>x.row===g[0].row))m.push({s:g.map(x=>x.s).join(''),start:g[0].start,row:g[0].row});else g.forEach(x=>m.push(x));}list=m;}
  if(list.length===1&&Array.from(list[0].s).length>=2){const a=Array.from(list[0].s),h=Math.ceil(a.length/2),f=a.slice(0,h).join('');list=[{s:f,start:list[0].start,row:list[0].row},{s:a.slice(h).join(''),start:list[0].start+f.length,row:list[0].row}];}
  return list;
}
// 語の強調・色・大きさ（run の scale）を調べる
function runInfo(start,len){
  let o=0,emph=false,col=null,ws=0,ss=0;
  for(const r of runs||[]){
    const n=String(r.text||'').length,a=Math.max(o,start),b=Math.min(o+n,start+len);
    if(b>a){const sc=Number.isFinite(r.scale)&&r.scale>0?r.scale:1;let w=0;for(const ch of Array.from(String(text).slice(a,b)))w+=ch.codePointAt(0)<256?.6:1;w=Math.max(.3,w);ws+=w;ss+=w*sc;if(r.emphasis||r.color)emph=true;if(r.color&&!col)col=r.color;}
    o+=n;if(o>=start+len)break;
  }
  return {emph,col,scale:ws?ss/ws:1};
}
const SIZES=[1.16,.86,1.04,.9,1.22,.92];
const items=splitWords(MAXWORDS).map((w,i)=>{
  const inf=runInfo(w.start,w.s.length);
  return {...w,i,emph:inf.emph,col:inf.col,rs:clamp(inf.scale,.4,3),sz:SIZES[i%SIZES.length]*(.93+random()*.14)*(inf.emph?1.08:1),tw:0,th:0,bw:0,bh:0,x:0,y:0,dy:0,info:null,fx:[]};
});
const nWords=items.length,spread=Math.min(life*.25,(nWords-1)*.26);
items.forEach(it=>{it.t0=nWords>1?spread*it.i/(nWords-1):0;});
// 語の文字メッシュ（強調語の色・縁取り・中抜き・語の大きさは createText が textStart から自動で効かせる）
function makeText(it,ink,extra){
  const opt={textStart:it.start,fontSize:em*it.sz,anchorX:'center',anchorY:'middle',...(extra||{})};if(ink)opt.color=ink;
  const m=createText(it.s,opt);
  m.fillOpacity=0;m.outlineOpacity=0;m.renderOrder=it.i*16+14;
  if(m.material){m.material.depthTest=false;m.material.depthWrite=false;}
  it.mesh=m;return m;
}
function rrPts(w,h,r,cut,cs){
  r=Math.max(.01,Math.min(r,w/2,h/2));const hw=w/2,hh=h/2,P=[];
  const arc=(cx,cy,a0)=>{for(let k=0;k<=6;k++){const a=a0+k*Math.PI/12;P.push(V(cx+r*Math.cos(a),cy+r*Math.sin(a)));}};
  arc(hw-r,-hh+r,-Math.PI/2);
  if(cut>0){P.push(V(hw,hh-cut),V(hw-cut,hh));}else arc(hw-r,hh-r,0);
  arc(-hw+r,hh-r,Math.PI/2);
  arc(-hw+r,-hh+r,Math.PI);
  if(cs<0)for(const p of P)p.x=-p.x;
  return P;
}
const rrGeo=(w,h,r,cut,cs)=>new THREE.ShapeGeometry(new THREE.Shape(rrPts(w,h,r,cut,cs)));
function starGeo(R,r,n,rot){const P=[];for(let k=0;k<n*2;k++){const a=rot+k*Math.PI/n,rr=k%2?r:R;P.push(V(Math.cos(a)*rr,Math.sin(a)*rr));}return new THREE.ShapeGeometry(new THREE.Shape(P));}
// 筆の線：点列を幅つきの帯にして、描画範囲（drawRange）で一筆書きのように伸ばす
function ribbonGeo(pts,wf){
  const n=pts.length,pos=new Float32Array(n*6),idx=[];
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
    const a=pts[Math.max(0,i-1)],b=pts[Math.min(n-1,i+1)];
    let dx=b.x-a.x,dy=b.y-a.y;const l=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;dx/=l;dy/=l;
    const w=wf(i/(n-1),i)/2,nx=-dy*w,ny=dx*w;
    pos.set([pts[i].x+nx,pts[i].y+ny,0,pts[i].x-nx,pts[i].y-ny,0],i*6);
    if(i<n-1){const k=i*2;idx.push(k,k+1,k+2,k+1,k+3,k+2);}
  }
  const g=new THREE.BufferGeometry();g.setAttribute('position',new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos,3));g.setIndex(idx);g.userData.segs=n-1;return g;
}
const drawTo=(m,p)=>{const g=m.geometry,s=g.userData.segs||0;g.setDrawRange(0,Math.floor(s*clamp(p))*6);m.visible=p>.001;};
const mkMat=(c,o=1)=>new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:c,transparent:true,opacity:o,depthTest:false,depthWrite:false,side:THREE.DoubleSide});
// 装飾メッシュ。it.fx に不透明度の基準を覚えさせ、まとめて薄くできる
function deco(it,geo,c,o,order){
  const m=new THREE.Mesh(geo,mkMat(c,o==null?1:o));m.renderOrder=it.i*16+order;it.fx.push([m.material,o==null?1:o]);return m;
}
function regeo(m,geo){if(m.geometry)m.geometry.dispose();m.geometry=geo;}
function fade(it,a){for(const f of it.fx)f[0].opacity=f[1]*a;}
function measure(it){
  const r=it.mesh&&it.mesh.textRenderInfo,b=r&&r.blockBounds;
  let base;
  if(b&&b.length===4&&b.every(Number.isFinite))base=Math.max(em*it.sz*.3,b[2]-b[0]);
  else{let n=0;for(const ch of Array.from(it.s))n+=ch.codePointAt(0)<256?.6:1;base=em*it.sz*n;}
  it.tw=base*it.rs;it.th=em*it.sz*1.12*Math.max(.7,it.rs);
}
let fit=1,laid=false,dead=false,FITW=.86,FITH=.7,GAPX=.22,GAPY=.1,boxW=1,boxH=1;
function layout(){
  for(const it of items){measure(it);size(it);}
  const gap=em*GAPX,limit=W*FITW,rows=[],hard=[];
  for(const it of items)(hard[it.row]=hard[it.row]||[]).push(it);
  for(const row of hard){if(!row)continue;
    const total=row.reduce((s,l,k)=>s+l.bw+(k?gap:0),0),n=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(total/limit)),cap=n>1?Math.min(limit,total/n*1.12+gap):limit;
    let cur=[],x=0;
    for(const l of row){if(cur.length&&x+gap+l.bw>cap){rows.push(cur);cur=[];x=0;}x+=(cur.length?gap:0)+l.bw;cur.push(l);}
    rows.push(cur);}
  const rowH=rows.map(r=>Math.max(...r.map(l=>l.bh))),vgap=em*GAPY,totH=rowH.reduce((a,h)=>a+h,0)+vgap*(rows.length-1);
  let y=totH/2,maxW=1;
  rows.forEach((row,r)=>{
    let x=0;for(const l of row){l.x=x+l.bw/2;x+=l.bw+gap;}
    const tw=x-gap;maxW=Math.max(maxW,tw);
    const cy=y-rowH[r]/2;for(const l of row){l.x-=tw/2;l.y=cy+l.dy;}
    y-=rowH[r]+vgap;
  });
  fit=Math.min(1,W*FITW/maxW,H*FITH/totH);root.scale.setScalar(fit);boxW=maxW;boxH=totH;
  if(typeof afterLayout==='function')afterLayout();
}
// ---- 吹き出し ----
const FILLS=['#ffffff','#fff3b0','#ffd6e0','#c9f4e4','#cfe6ff','#ffe0b8','#e6dcff'];
const INK='#1c1a2b',LIGHT='#fffdf6',SHADOW='#4a4470';
const ellPts=(rx,ry,n=40,rot=0)=>{const P=[];for(let k=0;k<n;k++){const a=rot+k/n*Math.PI*2;P.push(V(Math.cos(a)*rx,Math.sin(a)*ry));}return P;};
const polyGeo=P=>new THREE.ShapeGeometry(new THREE.Shape(P));
function bodyShapes(it,rx,ry,grow){
  // grow: 縁取りのぶん外へ広げる量
  const o=grow||0;
  if(it.kind==='ell')return [new THREE.Shape(ellPts(rx+o,ry+o))];
  if(it.kind==='rect'){const P=rrPts(2*(rx+o),2*(ry+o),Math.min(rx,ry)*.55+o,0,1);return [new THREE.Shape(P)];}
  if(it.kind==='burst'){
    const n=it.spk.length,P=[];
    for(let k=0;k<n*2;k++){const a=(k/(n*2))*Math.PI*2+.1,spike=k%2===0,j=it.spk[k>>1];const f=spike?1.18+j*.1:.82;P.push(V(Math.cos(a)*(rx*f+o*1.2),Math.sin(a)*(ry*f+o*1.2)));}
    return [new THREE.Shape(P)];
  }
  // 雲：まるを輪に並べ、中を楕円で埋める
  const rc=ry*.58,sh=[new THREE.Shape(ellPts(rx-rc*.45+o,ry-rc*.45+o,32))],n=Math.max(7,Math.round((rx+ry)/(rc*.62)));
  for(let k=0;k<n;k++){const a=k/n*Math.PI*2+.2,cx=Math.cos(a)*(rx-rc*.55),cy=Math.sin(a)*(ry-rc*.55),r=rc*(.86+it.spk[k%it.spk.length]*.3)+o;sh.push(new THREE.Shape(ellPts(r,r,14,0).map(p=>V(p.x+cx,p.y+cy))));}
  return sh;
}
function tailShapes(it,o){
  const {rx,ry,tx,ty,wb}=it;
  if(it.kind==='cloud'){
    const r1=em*.12*it.sz+o,r2=em*.1*it.sz+o;
    const c1=V(tx*.55,-(ry+r1*.1)),c2=V(tx,ty+r2*.9);
    return [new THREE.Shape(ellPts(r1,r1,16).map(p=>V(p.x+c1.x,p.y+c1.y))),new THREE.Shape(ellPts(r2,r2,12).map(p=>V(p.x+c2.x,p.y+c2.y)))];
  }
  const by=-ry*.55,bx=tx*.5,g=o*1.1;
  return [new THREE.Shape([V(bx-wb-g,by),V(bx+wb+g,by),V(tx+Math.sign(tx||1)*g*.6,ty-g*1.4)])];
}
for(const it of items){
  const accentCol=it.col||accent;
  it.kind=it.emph?'burst':['ell','rect','cloud'][(it.i+Math.floor(random()*2))%3];
  it.spk=[random(),random(),random(),random(),random(),random(),random(),random(),random(),random(),random(),random()];
  if(it.kind==='burst')it.spk=it.spk.slice(0,9);
  it.face=it.emph?(lum(accentCol)>.5?'#2a2540':'#fffbe8'):FILLS[(it.i*2+Math.floor(random()*2))%FILLS.length];
  it.line=lum(it.face)<.4?LIGHT:INK;
  const ink=lum(it.face)<.4?'#ffffff':INK;
  it.side=(it.i+(random()<.3?1:0))%2?1:-1;it.tilt=(random()-.5)*.1;
  it.dy=(random()-.5)*em*.2;
  const g=new THREE.Group();g.visible=false;root.add(g);it.g=g;
  const inner=new THREE.Group();g.add(inner);it.inner=inner;
  const parts=[['sB',SHADOW,0],['sT',SHADOW,1],['hB',it.line===INK?LIGHT:INK,2],['hT',it.line===INK?LIGHT:INK,3],['oB',it.line,4],['oT',it.line,5],['fB',it.face,6],['fT',it.face,7]];
  for(const [k,c,ord] of parts){it[k]=deco(it,new THREE.BufferGeometry(),c,1,ord);inner.add(it[k]);}
  it.glint=deco(it,new THREE.BufferGeometry(),'#ffffff',.7,8);inner.add(it.glint);
  const tx=makeText(it,ink);inner.add(tx);
}
function size(it){
  const sz=it.sz,o=em*.062*Math.min(1.2,sz);
  let rx,ry;
  if(it.kind==='rect'){rx=it.tw/2+em*.36*sz;ry=it.th/2+em*.2*sz;}
  else if(it.kind==='cloud'){rx=it.tw/2*1.2+em*.28*sz;ry=it.th/2*1.35+em*.16*sz;}
  else if(it.kind==='burst'){rx=it.tw/2*1.18+em*.3*sz;ry=it.th/2*1.2+em*.2*sz;}
  else{rx=it.tw/2*1.34+em*.12*sz;ry=it.th/2*1.4+em*.1*sz;}
  const tl=Math.min(em*.5*sz,ry*.8)+(it.kind==='cloud'?ry*.15:0);
  Object.assign(it,{rx,ry,o,tl,tx:it.side*rx*(.38+.12*hash(it.i,4)),ty:-(ry+tl),wb:Math.max(em*.08*sz,Math.min(rx*.14,em*.2*sz))});
  const off=o*1.1;
  regeo(it.sB,new THREE.ShapeGeometry(bodyShapes(it,rx,ry,o)));regeo(it.sT,new THREE.ShapeGeometry(tailShapes(it,o)));
  it.sB.position.set(off,-off,0);it.sT.position.set(off,-off,0);
  regeo(it.hB,new THREE.ShapeGeometry(bodyShapes(it,rx,ry,o*1.6)));regeo(it.hT,new THREE.ShapeGeometry(tailShapes(it,o*1.6)));
  regeo(it.oB,new THREE.ShapeGeometry(bodyShapes(it,rx,ry,o)));regeo(it.oT,new THREE.ShapeGeometry(tailShapes(it,o)));
  regeo(it.fB,new THREE.ShapeGeometry(bodyShapes(it,rx,ry,0)));regeo(it.fT,new THREE.ShapeGeometry(tailShapes(it,-o*.2)));
  // 左上のつや
  const gw=rx*.34,gh=Math.max(em*.045,ry*.09);
  regeo(it.glint,new THREE.ShapeGeometry(new THREE.Shape(rrPts(gw,gh,gh/2,0,1))));
  it.glint.position.set(-rx*.45,ry*.58,0);it.glint.rotation.z=.32;
  it.glint.visible=it.kind!=='burst'&&lum(it.face)<.97;
  it.inner.position.set(0,tl/2,0);
  it.bw=2*(rx+o)*(it.kind==='burst'?1.18:1)+o*2.4;it.bh=2*(ry+o)*(it.kind==='burst'?1.18:1)+tl+o*2.4;
  it.pivot=V(it.tx,tl/2+ty0(it));
}
function ty0(it){return -(it.ry+it.tl);}
function pose(it,t,lt,q){
  const g=it.g;
  if(lt<0||q<=0.001){g.visible=false;it.mesh.visible=false;return;}
  const u=clamp(lt/.55),pop=lt<.55?spring(u):1;
  const live=smooth((lt-.6)/.4),breathe=1+.022*live*Math.sin(t*3.6+it.i*1.9);
  let s=Math.max(0,pop)*breathe*q;
  const wob=Math.exp(-5*lt)*Math.sin(lt*21+it.i)*.07;
  const rot=it.tilt+wob;
  const sx=s*(1-.28*(pop-1)),sy=s*(1+.28*(pop-1));
  g.visible=s>.004;
  // しっぽの先を支点にして膨らむ（支点の位置は箱の中心から見たしっぽの先）
  const px=it.pivot.x,py=it.pivot.y,c=Math.cos(rot),n=Math.sin(rot);
  g.position.set(it.x+px-(px*c*sx-py*n*sy),it.y+py-(px*n*sx+py*c*sy),0);
  g.rotation.z=rot;g.scale.set(sx,sy,1);
  const a=smooth((lt-.14)/.12)*smooth(q*1.5);
  it.mesh.visible=g.visible&&a>.002;it.mesh.fillOpacity=a;it.mesh.outlineOpacity=a;
}
function update(time){
  if(dead)return;
  const t=clamp(Number.isFinite(time)?time:0,0,life);
  let changed=!laid;
  for(const it of items){const r=it.mesh&&it.mesh.textRenderInfo;if(r&&it.info!==r){it.info=r;changed=true;}}
  if(changed){layout();laid=true;}
  const q=smooth(clamp((life-t)/outro));
  for(const it of items)pose(it,t,t-it.t0,q);
  if(typeof frame==='function')frame(t,q);
}
function dispose(){
  if(dead)return;dead=true;
  root.traverse(o=>{
    if(typeof o.dispose==='function'){o.dispose();return;}
    if(o.geometry)o.geometry.dispose();
    if(o.material&&o.material.dispose)o.material.dispose();
  });
  scene.remove(root);
}
update(0);
return {update,dispose};
文字モーション（コード）10/14 追加

文手描きの丸囲みと下線NEWHANDDRAWN-MARK

語のまわりに手描きの丸や下線が、一筆書きのようにシュッと伸びて描かれます。

メソッド工房の「文字モーション」のサンプルから選びます。

コードを見る
// 語ごとに手描きの印が一筆書きで伸びる。強調語は丸で囲み、そのほかは下線・波線・二重線・蛍光ペンの帯。線は手描きらしく細かく震え、語の大きさと傾きも語ごとに違う
const MAXWORDS=8;
const {THREE,scene,createText,text,width,height,fontSize,duration,random,runs,accent}=api;
const clamp=(x,a=0,b=1)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));
const smooth=x=>{x=clamp(x);return x*x*(3-2*x)};
const spring=x=>{x=clamp(x);return 1-(1-x)*Math.exp(-3.4*x)*Math.cos(6.6*x)};
const back=x=>{x=clamp(x)-1;return 1+2.2*x*x*x+1.2*x*x};
const mix=(a,b,k)=>a+(b-a)*k;
