const hash=(a,b)=>{const s=Math.sin(a*127.1+b*311.7)*43758.5453;return s-Math.floor(s)};
const W=Math.max(1,width),H=Math.max(1,height),em=Math.max(8,fontSize||H*.18),life=Math.max(.05,duration);
const outro=Math.min(.5,life*.18);
const root=new THREE.Group();scene.add(root);
const lum=c=>{try{const n=new THREE.Color(c).getHex();return (.299*(n>>16&255)+.587*(n>>8&255)+.114*(n&255))/255}catch(e){return .5}};
const shade=(c,to,k)=>{const a=new THREE.Color(c);a.lerp(new THREE.Color(to),k);return '#'+a.getHexString()};
const V=(x,y)=>new THREE.Vector2(x,y);
// 同じ書式（強調・色・大きさ）の中か
function runAtOff(o){let a=0;for(const r of runs||[]){const n=String(r.text||'').length;if(o<a+n)return r;a+=n;}return null;}
function sameRun(a,b){const x=runAtOff(a)||{},y=runAtOff(b)||{};return !!x.emphasis===!!y.emphasis&&(x.color||'')===(y.color||'')&&(x.scale||1)===(y.scale||1);}
// 歌詞を語に区切る（日本語は Intl.Segmenter。句読点は前の語につける。多すぎる語はまとめ、1語だけなら半分に割る）
function splitWords(maxWords){
  const seg=(typeof Intl!=='undefined'&&Intl.Segmenter)?new Intl.Segmenter('ja',{granularity:'word'}):null;
  const out=[];let off=0;
  String(text||'').replace(/\r/g,'').split('\n').forEach((line,row)=>{
    const parts=[];
    if(seg){for(const p of seg.segment(line)){if(p.segment.trim())parts.push({s:p.segment,start:p.index});}}
    else{for(let i=0;i<line.length;i+=2)parts.push({s:line.slice(i,i+2),start:i});}
    const merged=[];
    for(const p of parts){
      const last=merged[merged.length-1];
      if(last&&/^[、。,.!?！？…「」『』（）()・～〜ー]+$/.test(p.s))last.s+=p.s;
      else if(last&&/^[ぁ-ゟー]{1,3}$/.test(p.s)&&last.s.length+p.s.length<=6&&!/[、。,.!?！？…]$/.test(last.s)&&last.start+last.s.length===p.start&&sameRun(last.start+off,p.start+off))last.s+=p.s;   // 助詞や語尾は前の語につける
      else merged.push({...p});
    }
    for(const m of merged)out.push({s:m.s,start:off+m.start,row});
    off+=line.length+1;
  });
  let list=out;
  if(list.length>maxWords){const c=Math.ceil(list.length/maxWords),m=[];for(let i=0;i<list.length;i+=c){const g=list.slice(i,i+c);if(g.every(x=>x.row===g[0].row))m.push({s:g.map(x=>x.s).join(''),start:g[0].start,row:g[0].row});else g.forEach(x=>m.push(x));}list=m;}
  if(list.length===1&&Array.from(list[0].s).length>=2){const a=Array.from(list[0].s),h=Math.ceil(a.length/2),f=a.slice(0,h).join('');list=[{s:f,start:list[0].start,row:list[0].row},{s:a.slice(h).join(''),start:list[0].start+f.length,row:list[0].row}];}
  return list;
}
// 語の強調・色・大きさ（run の scale）を調べる
function runInfo(start,len){
  let o=0,emph=false,col=null,ws=0,ss=0;
  for(const r of runs||[]){
    const n=String(r.text||'').length,a=Math.max(o,start),b=Math.min(o+n,start+len);
    if(b>a){const sc=Number.isFinite(r.scale)&&r.scale>0?r.scale:1;let w=0;for(const ch of Array.from(String(text).slice(a,b)))w+=ch.codePointAt(0)<256?.6:1;w=Math.max(.3,w);ws+=w;ss+=w*sc;if(r.emphasis||r.color)emph=true;if(r.color&&!col)col=r.color;}
    o+=n;if(o>=start+len)break;
  }
  return {emph,col,scale:ws?ss/ws:1};
}
const SIZES=[1.16,.86,1.04,.9,1.22,.92];
const items=splitWords(MAXWORDS).map((w,i)=>{
  const inf=runInfo(w.start,w.s.length);
  return {...w,i,emph:inf.emph,col:inf.col,rs:clamp(inf.scale,.4,3),sz:SIZES[i%SIZES.length]*(.93+random()*.14)*(inf.emph?1.08:1),tw:0,th:0,bw:0,bh:0,x:0,y:0,dy:0,info:null,fx:[]};
});
const nWords=items.length,spread=Math.min(life*.25,(nWords-1)*.26);
items.forEach(it=>{it.t0=nWords>1?spread*it.i/(nWords-1):0;});
// 語の文字メッシュ（強調語の色・縁取り・中抜き・語の大きさは createText が textStart から自動で効かせる）
function makeText(it,ink,extra){
  const opt={textStart:it.start,fontSize:em*it.sz,anchorX:'center',anchorY:'middle',...(extra||{})};if(ink)opt.color=ink;
  const m=createText(it.s,opt);
  m.fillOpacity=0;m.outlineOpacity=0;m.renderOrder=it.i*16+14;
  if(m.material){m.material.depthTest=false;m.material.depthWrite=false;}
  it.mesh=m;return m;
}
function rrPts(w,h,r,cut,cs){
  r=Math.max(.01,Math.min(r,w/2,h/2));const hw=w/2,hh=h/2,P=[];
  const arc=(cx,cy,a0)=>{for(let k=0;k<=6;k++){const a=a0+k*Math.PI/12;P.push(V(cx+r*Math.cos(a),cy+r*Math.sin(a)));}};
  arc(hw-r,-hh+r,-Math.PI/2);
  if(cut>0){P.push(V(hw,hh-cut),V(hw-cut,hh));}else arc(hw-r,hh-r,0);
  arc(-hw+r,hh-r,Math.PI/2);
  arc(-hw+r,-hh+r,Math.PI);
  if(cs<0)for(const p of P)p.x=-p.x;
  return P;
}
const rrGeo=(w,h,r,cut,cs)=>new THREE.ShapeGeometry(new THREE.Shape(rrPts(w,h,r,cut,cs)));
function starGeo(R,r,n,rot){const P=[];for(let k=0;k<n*2;k++){const a=rot+k*Math.PI/n,rr=k%2?r:R;P.push(V(Math.cos(a)*rr,Math.sin(a)*rr));}return new THREE.ShapeGeometry(new THREE.Shape(P));}
// 筆の線：点列を幅つきの帯にして、描画範囲（drawRange）で一筆書きのように伸ばす
function ribbonGeo(pts,wf){
  const n=pts.length,pos=new Float32Array(n*6),idx=[];
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
    const a=pts[Math.max(0,i-1)],b=pts[Math.min(n-1,i+1)];
    let dx=b.x-a.x,dy=b.y-a.y;const l=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;dx/=l;dy/=l;
    const w=wf(i/(n-1),i)/2,nx=-dy*w,ny=dx*w;
    pos.set([pts[i].x+nx,pts[i].y+ny,0,pts[i].x-nx,pts[i].y-ny,0],i*6);
    if(i<n-1){const k=i*2;idx.push(k,k+1,k+2,k+1,k+3,k+2);}
  }
  const g=new THREE.BufferGeometry();g.setAttribute('position',new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos,3));g.setIndex(idx);g.userData.segs=n-1;return g;
}
const drawTo=(m,p)=>{const g=m.geometry,s=g.userData.segs||0;g.setDrawRange(0,Math.floor(s*clamp(p))*6);m.visible=p>.001;};
const mkMat=(c,o=1)=>new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:c,transparent:true,opacity:o,depthTest:false,depthWrite:false,side:THREE.DoubleSide});
// 装飾メッシュ。it.fx に不透明度の基準を覚えさせ、まとめて薄くできる
function deco(it,geo,c,o,order){
  const m=new THREE.Mesh(geo,mkMat(c,o==null?1:o));m.renderOrder=it.i*16+order;it.fx.push([m.material,o==null?1:o]);return m;
}
function regeo(m,geo){if(m.geometry)m.geometry.dispose();m.geometry=geo;}
function fade(it,a){for(const f of it.fx)f[0].opacity=f[1]*a;}
function measure(it){
  const r=it.mesh&&it.mesh.textRenderInfo,b=r&&r.blockBounds;
  let base;
  if(b&&b.length===4&&b.every(Number.isFinite))base=Math.max(em*it.sz*.3,b[2]-b[0]);
  else{let n=0;for(const ch of Array.from(it.s))n+=ch.codePointAt(0)<256?.6:1;base=em*it.sz*n;}
  it.tw=base*it.rs;it.th=em*it.sz*1.12*Math.max(.7,it.rs);
}
let fit=1,laid=false,dead=false,FITW=.86,FITH=.7,GAPX=.22,GAPY=.1,boxW=1,boxH=1;
function layout(){
  for(const it of items){measure(it);size(it);}
  const gap=em*GAPX,limit=W*FITW,rows=[],hard=[];
  for(const it of items)(hard[it.row]=hard[it.row]||[]).push(it);
  for(const row of hard){if(!row)continue;
    const total=row.reduce((s,l,k)=>s+l.bw+(k?gap:0),0),n=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(total/limit)),cap=n>1?Math.min(limit,total/n*1.12+gap):limit;
    let cur=[],x=0;
    for(const l of row){if(cur.length&&x+gap+l.bw>cap){rows.push(cur);cur=[];x=0;}x+=(cur.length?gap:0)+l.bw;cur.push(l);}
    rows.push(cur);}
  const rowH=rows.map(r=>Math.max(...r.map(l=>l.bh))),vgap=em*GAPY,totH=rowH.reduce((a,h)=>a+h,0)+vgap*(rows.length-1);
  let y=totH/2,maxW=1;
  rows.forEach((row,r)=>{
    let x=0;for(const l of row){l.x=x+l.bw/2;x+=l.bw+gap;}
    const tw=x-gap;maxW=Math.max(maxW,tw);
    const cy=y-rowH[r]/2;for(const l of row){l.x-=tw/2;l.y=cy+l.dy;}
    y-=rowH[r]+vgap;
  });
  fit=Math.min(1,W*FITW/maxW,H*FITH/totH);root.scale.setScalar(fit);boxW=maxW;boxH=totH;
  if(typeof afterLayout==='function')afterLayout();
}
// ---- 手描きの丸囲み・下線 ----
const PEN=['#ff5d73','#ffd23f','#4fd8ff','#7cf29a','#ff9f43','#c79bff'];
const MARK=['#ffd83a','#ff7fb5','#58d0ff','#9be84a'];
for(const it of items){
  const pen=it.emph?(it.col||accent):PEN[(it.i*5+Math.floor(random()*2))%PEN.length];
  it.pen=pen;it.style=it.emph?'circle':['circle','swoosh','mark','wave','double','circle'][it.i%6];
  it.ph=random()*6.28;it.dir=random()<.5?1:-1;it.tilt=(random()-.5)*.12;it.dy=(random()-.5)*em*.14;
  it.r1=random();it.r2=random();it.r3=random();
  const g=new THREE.Group();g.visible=false;root.add(g);it.g=g;
  const sg=new THREE.Group();g.add(sg);it.sg=sg;
  const tx=makeText(it,null,{outlineWidth:'4%',outlineColor:'#101819'});g.add(tx);
  it.strokes=[];
  const add=(c,o,ord)=>{const m=deco(it,new THREE.BufferGeometry(),c,o,ord);sg.add(m);it.strokes.push(m);return m;};
  if(it.style==='circle'){it.s1=add(pen,1,2);it.s2=add(pen,1,3);it.s3=add(pen,1,4);it.s4=add(pen,1,5);}
  else if(it.style==='mark'){it.s1=add(MARK[it.i%MARK.length],.85,1);it.s2=add(pen,1,2);}
  else if(it.style==='double'){it.s1=add(pen,1,2);it.s2=add(pen,1,3);}
  else{it.s1=add(pen,1,2);it.s2=add(pen,1,3);}
}
function size(it){
  const sz=it.sz,tw=it.tw,th=it.th,lw=Math.max(3,em*.12*Math.min(1.2,sz));it.lw=lw;
  const rx=tw/2+em*.3*sz,ry=th/2+em*.17*sz;
  it.bw=it.style==='circle'?2*rx*1.2:tw+em*.8*sz+em*.2*sz;it.bh=it.style==='circle'?2*(ry*1.16+em*sz*.3):th+em*.55*sz+lw*3;
  const R=it.r1;
  if(it.style==='circle'){
    const N=72,P=[],a0=Math.PI*.82,sweep=Math.PI*2*1.1*it.dir;
    for(let k=0;k<N;k++){const u=k/(N-1),a=a0+sweep*u,wob=1+.035*Math.sin(u*11+it.ph),grow=.96+.1*u;
      P.push(V(Math.cos(a)*rx*grow*wob+u*rx*.03,Math.sin(a)*ry*grow*wob*(1+.05*Math.sin(u*7+it.ph*2))));}
    regeo(it.s1,ribbonGeo(P,u=>lw*1.15*(.35+.65*smooth(u/.12))*(1-.75*smooth((u-.86)/.14))));
    // きらきらの短い線（右上）：楕円の縁から外向きに
    [it.s2,it.s3,it.s4].forEach((m,j)=>{
      const a=1.0+(j-1)*.42+it.r2*.15,px=Math.cos(a)*rx*1.1,py=Math.sin(a)*ry*1.1;
      let nx=Math.cos(a)/rx,ny=Math.sin(a)/ry;const nl=Math.hypot(nx,ny);nx/=nl;ny/=nl;
      const len=em*sz*(.24+.05*(j%2));
      const pts=[];for(let k=0;k<7;k++){const u=k/6;pts.push(V(px+nx*len*u,py+ny*len*u));}
      regeo(m,ribbonGeo(pts,u=>lw*.9*(1-.6*u)));
    });
  }else if(it.style==='mark'){
    const w=tw+em*.34*sz,h=th*.62,sk=h*.28;
    regeo(it.s1,new THREE.ShapeGeometry(new THREE.Shape([V(0,-h/2),V(w-sk,-h/2),V(w,h/2),V(sk*.4,h/2)])));
    it.s1.position.set(-w/2,-th*.1,0);it.s1.rotation.z=-.025;
    const y0=-th/2-lw*.2,pts=[];for(let k=0;k<30;k++){const u=k/29;pts.push(V(-tw/2-em*.1+u*(tw+em*.2),y0-Math.sin(u*Math.PI)*lw*.9+(it.r2-.5)*lw));}
    regeo(it.s2,ribbonGeo(pts,u=>lw*(.3+.7*Math.sin(Math.PI*u)**.6)));
  }else{
    const w=tw+em*.24*sz,n=44,y0=-th*.5+em*.02*sz;
    const line=(len,dy,wav)=>{const pts=[];for(let k=0;k<n;k++){const u=k/(n-1),x=-len/2+u*len;
      let y=y0+dy-Math.sin(u*Math.PI)*lw*.7*(it.dir)+(it.r2-.5)*lw*1.6*(u-.5);
      if(wav)y+=Math.sin(u*Math.PI*2*Math.max(2,Math.round(len/(em*.5)))+it.ph)*lw*1.1;
      pts.push(V(x,y));}return pts;};
    const R=em*.2*sz;it.spR=R;
    if(it.style!=='double'){regeo(it.s2,starGeo(R,R*.28,4,Math.PI/2));it.s2.position.set(w/2+R*.7,-th*.5+R*.9,0);}
    if(it.style==='wave')regeo(it.s1,ribbonGeo(line(w,0,true),u=>lw*(.45+.55*Math.sin(Math.PI*u)**.5)));
    else regeo(it.s1,ribbonGeo(line(w,0,false),u=>lw*(.3+.7*Math.sin(Math.PI*u)**.7)));
    if(it.style==='double')regeo(it.s2,ribbonGeo(line(w*.72,-lw*2.6,false),u=>lw*.8*(.3+.7*Math.sin(Math.PI*u)**.7)));
  }
}
function drawSpan(lt,t0,d){return clamp((lt-t0)/d);}
function pose(it,t,lt,q){
  const g=it.g;
  if(lt<0||q<=0.001){g.visible=false;it.mesh.visible=false;return;}
  g.visible=true;
  const u=clamp(lt/.28),pop=back(u),a=smooth(lt/.1)*q;
  const ex=1-q;
  const f=Math.floor(t*9);
  g.position.set(it.x,it.y-em*.1*ex,0);
  g.rotation.z=it.tilt+(1-pop)*.25*(it.i%2?1:-1);
  g.scale.setScalar(mix(.55,1,pop)*(1-.12*ex));
  // 手描きの線は細かく震える（時刻から決める）
  it.sg.position.set((hash(f,it.i)-.5)*em*.022,(hash(f,it.i+9)-.5)*em*.022,0);it.sg.rotation.z=(hash(f,it.i+17)-.5)*.014;
  fade(it,a);
  const s=it.style;
  if(s==='circle'){
    drawTo(it.s1,drawSpan(lt,.2,.62));
    drawTo(it.s2,drawSpan(lt,.78,.1));drawTo(it.s3,drawSpan(lt,.86,.1));drawTo(it.s4,drawSpan(lt,.94,.1));
  }else if(s==='mark'){
    it.s1.scale.x=Math.max(.001,smooth(drawSpan(lt,.18,.4)));it.s1.visible=it.s1.scale.x>.002;
    drawTo(it.s2,drawSpan(lt,.5,.3));
  }else if(s==='double'){
    drawTo(it.s1,drawSpan(lt,.2,.36));drawTo(it.s2,drawSpan(lt,.48,.3));
  }else{drawTo(it.s1,drawSpan(lt,.2,.45));const k=back(drawSpan(lt,.6,.3));it.s2.scale.setScalar(Math.max(.001,k));it.s2.rotation.z=(1-k)*2;it.s2.visible=k>.01;}
  it.mesh.visible=a>.002;it.mesh.fillOpacity=a;it.mesh.outlineOpacity=a;
}
function update(time){
  if(dead)return;
  const t=clamp(Number.isFinite(time)?time:0,0,life);
  let changed=!laid;
  for(const it of items){const r=it.mesh&&it.mesh.textRenderInfo;if(r&&it.info!==r){it.info=r;changed=true;}}
  if(changed){layout();laid=true;}
  const q=smooth(clamp((life-t)/outro));
  for(const it of items)pose(it,t,t-it.t0,q);
  if(typeof frame==='function')frame(t,q);
}
function dispose(){
  if(dead)return;dead=true;
  root.traverse(o=>{
    if(typeof o.dispose==='function'){o.dispose();return;}
    if(o.geometry)o.geometry.dispose();
    if(o.material&&o.material.dispose)o.material.dispose();
  });
  scene.remove(root);
}
update(0);
return {update,dispose};
文字モーション（コード）10/14 追加

文黒板にチョークで書くNEWCHALK-BOARD

黒板に語ごとのチョーク文字と下線が書かれ、かすれや粉が時刻とともに揺らぎます。

メソッド工房の「文字モーション」のサンプルから選びます。

コードを見る
// 黒板に、語ごとにチョークで書いた文字と下線・星の落書きが現れる。文字は震えるかすれと粉で、チョークの粉っぽさは時刻から決める。黒板は枠とチョーク受けつきで、縁の線が一周して描かれる
const MAXWORDS=8;
const {THREE,scene,createText,text,width,height,fontSize,duration,random,runs,accent}=api;
const clamp=(x,a=0,b=1)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));
const smooth=x=>{x=clamp(x);return x*x*(3-2*x)};
const spring=x=>{x=clamp(x);return 1-(1-x)*Math.exp(-3.4*x)*Math.cos(6.6*x)};
const back=x=>{x=clamp(x)-1;return 1+2.2*x*x*x+1.2*x*x};
const mix=(a,b,k)=>a+(b-a)*k;
const hash=(a,b)=>{const s=Math.sin(a*127.1+b*311.7)*43758.5453;return s-Math.floor(s)};
const W=Math.max(1,width),H=Math.max(1,height),em=Math.max(8,fontSize||H*.18),life=Math.max(.05,duration);
const outro=Math.min(.5,life*.18);
const root=new THREE.Group();scene.add(root);
const lum=c=>{try{const n=new THREE.Color(c).getHex();return (.299*(n>>16&255)+.587*(n>>8&255)+.114*(n&255))/255}catch(e){return .5}};
const shade=(c,to,k)=>{const a=new THREE.Color(c);a.lerp(new THREE.Color(to),k);return '#'+a.getHexString()};
const V=(x,y)=>new THREE.Vector2(x,y);
// 同じ書式（強調・色・大きさ）の中か
function runAtOff(o){let a=0;for(const r of runs||[]){const n=String(r.text||'').length;if(o<a+n)return r;a+=n;}return null;}
function sameRun(a,b){const x=runAtOff(a)||{},y=runAtOff(b)||{};return !!x.emphasis===!!y.emphasis&&(x.color||'')===(y.color||'')&&(x.scale||1)===(y.scale||1);}
// 歌詞を語に区切る（日本語は Intl.Segmenter。句読点は前の語につける。多すぎる語はまとめ、1語だけなら半分に割る）
function splitWords(maxWords){
  const seg=(typeof Intl!=='undefined'&&Intl.Segmenter)?new Intl.Segmenter('ja',{granularity:'word'}):null;
  const out=[];let off=0;
  String(text||'').replace(/\r/g,'').split('\n').forEach((line,row)=>{
    const parts=[];
    if(seg){for(const p of seg.segment(line)){if(p.segment.trim())parts.push({s:p.segment,start:p.index});}}
    else{for(let i=0;i<line.length;i+=2)parts.push({s:line.slice(i,i+2),start:i});}
    const merged=[];
    for(const p of parts){
      const last=merged[merged.length-1];
      if(last&&/^[、。,.!?！？…「」『』（）()・～〜ー]+$/.test(p.s))last.s+=p.s;
      else if(last&&/^[ぁ-ゟー]{1,3}$/.test(p.s)&&last.s.length+p.s.length<=6&&!/[、。,.!?！？…]$/.test(last.s)&&last.start+last.s.length===p.start&&sameRun(last.start+off,p.start+off))last.s+=p.s;   // 助詞や語尾は前の語につける
      else merged.push({...p});
    }
    for(const m of merged)out.push({s:m.s,start:off+m.start,row});
    off+=line.length+1;
  });
  let list=out;
  if(list.length>maxWords){const c=Math.ceil(list.length/maxWords),m=[];for(let i=0;i<list.length;i+=c){const g=list.slice(i,i+c);if(g.every(x=>x.row===g[0].row))m.push({s:g.map(x=>x.s).join(''),start:g[0].start,row:g[0].row});else g.forEach(x=>m.push(x));}list=m;}
  if(list.length===1&&Array.from(list[0].s).length>=2){const a=Array.from(list[0].s),h=Math.ceil(a.length/2),f=a.slice(0,h).join('');list=[{s:f,start:list[0].start,row:list[0].row},{s:a.slice(h).join(''),start:list[0].start+f.length,row:list[0].row}];}
  return list;
}
// 語の強調・色・大きさ（run の scale）を調べる
function runInfo(start,len){
  let o=0,emph=false,col=null,ws=0,ss=0;
  for(const r of runs||[]){
    const n=String(r.text||'').length,a=Math.max(o,start),b=Math.min(o+n,start+len);
    if(b>a){const sc=Number.isFinite(r.scale)&&r.scale>0?r.scale:1;let w=0;for(const ch of Array.from(String(text).slice(a,b)))w+=ch.codePointAt(0)<256?.6:1;w=Math.max(.3,w);ws+=w;ss+=w*sc;if(r.emphasis||r.color)emph=true;if(r.color&&!col)col=r.color;}
    o+=n;if(o>=start+len)break;
  }
  return {emph,col,scale:ws?ss/ws:1};
}
const SIZES=[1.16,.86,1.04,.9,1.22,.92];
const items=splitWords(MAXWORDS).map((w,i)=>{
  const inf=runInfo(w.start,w.s.length);
  return {...w,i,emph:inf.emph,col:inf.col,rs:clamp(inf.scale,.4,3),sz:SIZES[i%SIZES.length]*(.93+random()*.14)*(inf.emph?1.08:1),tw:0,th:0,bw:0,bh:0,x:0,y:0,dy:0,info:null,fx:[]};
});
const nWords=items.length,spread=Math.min(life*.25,(nWords-1)*.26);
items.forEach(it=>{it.t0=nWords>1?spread*it.i/(nWords-1):0;});
// 語の文字メッシュ（強調語の色・縁取り・中抜き・語の大きさは createText が textStart から自動で効かせる）
function makeText(it,ink,extra){
  const opt={textStart:it.start,fontSize:em*it.sz,anchorX:'center',anchorY:'middle',...(extra||{})};if(ink)opt.color=ink;
  const m=createText(it.s,opt);
  m.fillOpacity=0;m.outlineOpacity=0;m.renderOrder=it.i*16+14;
  if(m.material){m.material.depthTest=false;m.material.depthWrite=false;}
  it.mesh=m;return m;
}
function rrPts(w,h,r,cut,cs){
  r=Math.max(.01,Math.min(r,w/2,h/2));const hw=w/2,hh=h/2,P=[];
  const arc=(cx,cy,a0)=>{for(let k=0;k<=6;k++){const a=a0+k*Math.PI/12;P.push(V(cx+r*Math.cos(a),cy+r*Math.sin(a)));}};
  arc(hw-r,-hh+r,-Math.PI/2);
  if(cut>0){P.push(V(hw,hh-cut),V(hw-cut,hh));}else arc(hw-r,hh-r,0);
  arc(-hw+r,hh-r,Math.PI/2);
  arc(-hw+r,-hh+r,Math.PI);
  if(cs<0)for(const p of P)p.x=-p.x;
  return P;
}
const rrGeo=(w,h,r,cut,cs)=>new THREE.ShapeGeometry(new THREE.Shape(rrPts(w,h,r,cut,cs)));
function starGeo(R,r,n,rot){const P=[];for(let k=0;k<n*2;k++){const a=rot+k*Math.PI/n,rr=k%2?r:R;P.push(V(Math.cos(a)*rr,Math.sin(a)*rr));}return new THREE.ShapeGeometry(new THREE.Shape(P));}
// 筆の線：点列を幅つきの帯にして、描画範囲（drawRange）で一筆書きのように伸ばす
function ribbonGeo(pts,wf){
  const n=pts.length,pos=new Float32Array(n*6),idx=[];
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
    const a=pts[Math.max(0,i-1)],b=pts[Math.min(n-1,i+1)];
    let dx=b.x-a.x,dy=b.y-a.y;const l=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;dx/=l;dy/=l;
    const w=wf(i/(n-1),i)/2,nx=-dy*w,ny=dx*w;
    pos.set([pts[i].x+nx,pts[i].y+ny,0,pts[i].x-nx,pts[i].y-ny,0],i*6);
    if(i<n-1){const k=i*2;idx.push(k,k+1,k+2,k+1,k+3,k+2);}
  }
  const g=new THREE.BufferGeometry();g.setAttribute('position',new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos,3));g.setIndex(idx);g.userData.segs=n-1;return g;
}
const drawTo=(m,p)=>{const g=m.geometry,s=g.userData.segs||0;g.setDrawRange(0,Math.floor(s*clamp(p))*6);m.visible=p>.001;};
const mkMat=(c,o=1)=>new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:c,transparent:true,opacity:o,depthTest:false,depthWrite:false,side:THREE.DoubleSide});
// 装飾メッシュ。it.fx に不透明度の基準を覚えさせ、まとめて薄くできる
function deco(it,geo,c,o,order){
  const m=new THREE.Mesh(geo,mkMat(c,o==null?1:o));m.renderOrder=it.i*16+order;it.fx.push([m.material,o==null?1:o]);return m;
}
function regeo(m,geo){if(m.geometry)m.geometry.dispose();m.geometry=geo;}
function fade(it,a){for(const f of it.fx)f[0].opacity=f[1]*a;}
function measure(it){
  const r=it.mesh&&it.mesh.textRenderInfo,b=r&&r.blockBounds;
  let base;
  if(b&&b.length===4&&b.every(Number.isFinite))base=Math.max(em*it.sz*.3,b[2]-b[0]);
  else{let n=0;for(const ch of Array.from(it.s))n+=ch.codePointAt(0)<256?.6:1;base=em*it.sz*n;}
  it.tw=base*it.rs;it.th=em*it.sz*1.12*Math.max(.7,it.rs);
}
let fit=1,laid=false,dead=false,FITW=.86,FITH=.7,GAPX=.22,GAPY=.1,boxW=1,boxH=1;
function layout(){
  for(const it of items){measure(it);size(it);}
  const gap=em*GAPX,limit=W*FITW,rows=[],hard=[];
  for(const it of items)(hard[it.row]=hard[it.row]||[]).push(it);
  for(const row of hard){if(!row)continue;
    const total=row.reduce((s,l,k)=>s+l.bw+(k?gap:0),0),n=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(total/limit)),cap=n>1?Math.min(limit,total/n*1.12+gap):limit;
    let cur=[],x=0;
    for(const l of row){if(cur.length&&x+gap+l.bw>cap){rows.push(cur);cur=[];x=0;}x+=(cur.length?gap:0)+l.bw;cur.push(l);}
    rows.push(cur);}
  const rowH=rows.map(r=>Math.max(...r.map(l=>l.bh))),vgap=em*GAPY,totH=rowH.reduce((a,h)=>a+h,0)+vgap*(rows.length-1);
  let y=totH/2,maxW=1;
  rows.forEach((row,r)=>{
    let x=0;for(const l of row){l.x=x+l.bw/2;x+=l.bw+gap;}
    const tw=x-gap;maxW=Math.max(maxW,tw);
    const cy=y-rowH[r]/2;for(const l of row){l.x-=tw/2;l.y=cy+l.dy;}
    y-=rowH[r]+vgap;
  });
  fit=Math.min(1,W*FITW/maxW,H*FITH/totH);root.scale.setScalar(fit);boxW=maxW;boxH=totH;
  if(typeof afterLayout==='function')afterLayout();
}
// ---- 黒板チョーク ----
FITW=.64;FITH=.44;GAPX=.3;GAPY=.16;
const CH=['#f7f5e8','#ffe28a','#ffb7c5','#aee3ff','#c9f2a6','#f7f5e8'];
const SLATE='#25433a',DUSTC='#f2efe0';
const bd=new THREE.Group();root.add(bd);
const bdFx=[];
function bdMesh(c,o,ord){const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BufferGeometry(),mkMat(c,o));m.renderOrder=-60+ord;bdFx.push([m.material,o]);bd.add(m);return m;}
const frameM=bdMesh('#9a7449',1,0),slateM=bdMesh(SLATE,.97,1),sm1=bdMesh('#294a40',1,2),sm2=bdMesh('#294a40',1,2),trayM=bdMesh('#7d5c38',1,0),ch1=bdMesh('#f7f5e8',1,3),ch2=bdMesh('#ffb7c5',1,3),borderM=bdMesh('#e9e6d6',.7,4);
for(const it of items){
  const ink=CH[(it.i*2+Math.floor(random()*2))%CH.length];
  it.ink=ink;it.tilt=(random()-.5)*.08;it.dy=(random()-.5)*em*.12;
  it.under=it.emph||it.i%3===1;it.r1=random();it.r2=random();
  const g=new THREE.Group();g.visible=false;root.add(g);it.g=g;
  const sg=new THREE.Group();g.add(sg);it.sg=sg;
  const ghost=makeText(it,ink);ghost.renderOrder=it.i*16+13;
  const base=makeText(it,ink);
  it.ghost=ghost;g.add(ghost);g.add(base);
  const mk=(c,o)=>{const m=deco(it,new THREE.BufferGeometry(),c,o,10);m.renderOrder=it.i*16+9;sg.add(m);return m;};
  const uc=it.emph?(it.col||accent):it.ink;
  it.u1=mk(uc,.9);it.u2=mk(uc,.55);
  it.star=it.emph?mk(uc,.9):null;
}
function size(it){
  const sz=it.sz,tw=it.tw,th=it.th,lw=Math.max(2,em*.062*Math.min(1.2,sz));
  it.bw=tw+em*.3*sz+(it.emph?em*.35*sz:0);it.bh=th+em*.3*sz+(it.under?lw*2:0);
  const chalkW=(len,seed,k0)=>u=>lw*k0*(.3+.7*hash(Math.floor(u*60),seed))*(hash(Math.floor(u*38)+3,seed)>.84?.15:1)*(.45+.55*Math.sin(Math.PI*Math.min(1,u*1.1)));
  const n=48,y0=-th*.5+lw*.2;
  const pts=(len,dy,wob)=>{const P=[];for(let k=0;k<n;k++){const u=k/(n-1);P.push(V(-len/2+u*len,y0+dy+Math.sin(u*3+it.r1*6)*lw*wob-u*lw*.8*(it.r2-.5)));}return P;};
  const len=tw+em*.2*sz;
  regeo(it.u1,ribbonGeo(pts(len,0,.5),chalkW(len,it.i+1,1.1)));
  regeo(it.u2,ribbonGeo(pts(len*.8,-lw*1.7,.6).map(p=>V(p.x+len*.1,p.y)),chalkW(len,it.i+7,.7)));
  if(it.star){
    const R=em*.2*sz,P=[],cx=tw/2+em*.12*sz,cy=th/2;
    for(let k=0;k<=10;k++){const a=Math.PI/2+k*Math.PI*4/5,u=k/10;P.push(V(cx+Math.cos(a)*R,cy+Math.sin(a)*R));}
    regeo(it.star,ribbonGeo(P,u=>lw*.75*(1-.4*u)));
  }
}
// 粉：文字の欠け（板の色の点）と、落ちる粉
const SPK=Math.min(46,MAXWORDS*8),DST=Math.min(30,MAXWORDS*4);
function pointsMesh(n,col,sizePx,op,ord){
  const geo=new THREE.BufferGeometry();geo.setAttribute('position',new THREE.BufferAttribute(new Float32Array(n*3),3));
  const m=new THREE.Points(geo,new THREE.PointsMaterial({color:col,size:sizePx,sizeAttenuation:false,transparent:true,opacity:op,depthTest:false,depthWrite:false}));
  m.renderOrder=ord;m.frustumCulled=false;root.add(m);return m;
}
const specks=pointsMesh(SPK,SLATE,Math.max(1.8,H/130),1,5000);
const dust=pointsMesh(DST,DUSTC,Math.max(1.6,H/150),.6,5001);
const spk=[],dst=[];
for(let k=0;k<SPK;k++)spk.push({u:random()-.5,v:random()-.5,s:random()});
for(let k=0;k<DST;k++)dst.push({u:random()-.5,s:random()});
function afterLayout(){
  const mB=Math.min(em*.5,W*.06),bw=boxW*fit+2*mB,bh=boxH*fit+2*mB,fr=Math.max(3,em*.1);
  bd.scale.setScalar(1/fit);
  const rr=(w,h,r)=>rrGeo(w,h,r,0,1);
  regeo(frameM,rr(bw+2*fr,bh+2*fr,fr*1.4));regeo(slateM,rr(bw,bh,fr*.5));
  regeo(sm1,new THREE.ShapeGeometry(new THREE.Shape(ellPtsC(bw*.3,bh*.16,28,-bw*.18,bh*.2))));
  regeo(sm2,new THREE.ShapeGeometry(new THREE.Shape(ellPtsC(bw*.22,bh*.12,28,bw*.2,-bh*.24))));
  const th=fr*1.5;regeo(trayM,rr(bw*.86,th,th*.3));trayM.position.set(0,-(bh/2+fr+th*.35),0);
  regeo(ch1,rr(bw*.12,th*.55,th*.2));ch1.position.set(-bw*.2,-(bh/2+fr+th*.35)+th*.55,0);
  regeo(ch2,rr(bw*.07,th*.5,th*.2));ch2.position.set(-bw*.06,-(bh/2+fr+th*.35)+th*.5,0);ch2.rotation.z=.05;
  // 縁の線（一周して描かれる）
  const ins=mB*.4,w2=bw-2*ins,h2=bh-2*ins,P=[],per=2*(w2+h2),N=90;
  for(let k=0;k<=N;k++){let d=k/N*per,x,y;
    if(d<w2){x=-w2/2+d;y=h2/2;}else if((d-=w2)<h2){x=w2/2;y=h2/2-d;}else if((d-=h2)<w2){x=w2/2-d;y=-h2/2;}else{d-=w2;x=-w2/2;y=-h2/2+d;}
    P.push(V(x+Math.sin(k*.7)*.7,y+Math.cos(k*.9)*.7));}
  regeo(borderM,ribbonGeo(P,(u,i)=>Math.max(1.5,em*.028)*(.4+.6*hash(i,5))*(hash(i,9)>.88?.1:1)));
  it0.mB=mB;bdInfo.bw=bw;bdInfo.bh=bh;
}
const it0={},bdInfo={bw:1,bh:1};
function ellPtsC(rx,ry,n,cx,cy){const P=[];for(let k=0;k<n;k++){const a=k/n*Math.PI*2;P.push(V(cx+Math.cos(a)*rx,cy+Math.sin(a)*ry));}return P;}
function pose(it,t,lt,q){
  const g=it.g;
  if(lt<0||q<=0.001){g.visible=false;it.mesh.visible=false;it.ghost.visible=false;return;}
  g.visible=true;
  const u=clamp(lt/.22),a=smooth(lt/.1)*q,f=Math.floor(t*10);
  g.position.set(it.x,it.y,0);g.rotation.z=it.tilt;
  const s=mix(1.12,1,smooth(u));g.scale.setScalar(s);
  // かすれて揺れる：下書きの影が、時刻ごとに少しずれる
  it.ghost.position.set((hash(f,it.i)-.5)*em*.035,(hash(f,it.i+5)-.5)*em*.035,0);
  it.sg.position.set((hash(f,it.i+9)-.5)*em*.016,(hash(f,it.i+13)-.5)*em*.016,0);
  const w=.82+.18*hash(Math.floor(t*7),it.i+21);
  it.mesh.visible=a>.002;it.mesh.fillOpacity=a*.96*w;it.mesh.outlineOpacity=0;
  it.ghost.visible=a>.002;it.ghost.fillOpacity=a*.5*(1.15-w);it.ghost.outlineOpacity=0;
  fade(it,a);
  const d0=.3+(it.i%2)*.04;
  if(it.under){drawTo(it.u1,clamp((lt-d0)/.4));drawTo(it.u2,clamp((lt-d0-.32)/.3));}else{it.u1.visible=false;it.u2.visible=false;}
  if(it.star)drawTo(it.star,clamp((lt-d0-.55)/.35));
}
function frame(t,q){
  // 黒板：上からふわっと現れる
  const u=clamp(t/.4),a=smooth(t/.15)*q;
  bd.scale.set(1/fit*mix(.94,1,spring(u)),1/fit*mix(.7,1,spring(u)),1);
  for(const f of bdFx)f[0].opacity=f[1]*a;
  borderM.visible=t>.15;if(t>.15)drawTo(borderM,clamp((t-.15)/.7));
  // 文字の欠け
  const sp=specks.geometry.attributes.position.array,fl=Math.floor(t*6);
  for(let k=0;k<SPK;k++){
    const it=items[k%items.length],c=spk[k],on=it.g.visible&&q>.98&&t-it.t0>.3&&hash(k,fl)>.4;
    if(on){sp[k*3]=it.x+c.u*it.tw*1.02;sp[k*3+1]=it.y+c.v*it.th*.8;sp[k*3+2]=0;}else{sp[k*3]=1e5;sp[k*3+1]=1e5;sp[k*3+2]=0;}
  }
  specks.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate=true;specks.material.opacity=a;
  // 落ちる粉
  const dp=dust.geometry.attributes.position.array;
  for(let k=0;k<DST;k++){
    const it=items[k%items.length],c=dst[k],age=((t*.9+c.s)%1+1)%1,on=it.g.visible&&q>.5&&t-it.t0>.3&&age<.9;
    if(on){dp[k*3]=it.x+c.u*it.tw+Math.sin(age*9+k)*em*.03;dp[k*3+1]=it.y-it.th*.3-age*em*.42;dp[k*3+2]=0;}else{dp[k*3]=1e5;dp[k*3+1]=1e5;dp[k*3+2]=0;}
  }
  dust.geometry.attributes.position.needsUpdate=true;dust.material.opacity=.6*q*(.6+.4*Math.sin(t*5));
}
function update(time){
  if(dead)return;
  const t=clamp(Number.isFinite(time)?time:0,0,life);
  let changed=!laid;
  for(const it of items){const r=it.mesh&&it.mesh.textRenderInfo;if(r&&it.info!==r){it.info=r;changed=true;}}
  if(changed){layout();laid=true;}
  const q=smooth(clamp((life-t)/outro));
  for(const it of items)pose(it,t,t-it.t0,q);
  if(typeof frame==='function')frame(t,q);
}
function dispose(){
  if(dead)return;dead=true;
  root.traverse(o=>{
    if(typeof o.dispose==='function'){o.dispose();return;}
    if(o.geometry)o.geometry.dispose();
    if(o.material&&o.material.dispose)o.material.dispose();
  });
  scene.remove(root);
}
update(0);
return {update,dispose};
文字モーション（コード）10/14 追加

文くるっと回るワッペンNEWPATCH-BADGE

語ごとに縫い目つきのワッペンになり、コインのように返ったり回ったりして止まります。

メソッド工房の「文字モーション」のサンプルから選びます。

コードを見る
// 語ごとに縁取りと縫い目のあるワッペンになって、くるっと回って止まる。丸・波打つ縁・六角・角丸が混ざり、回り方（コインのように返る／くるくる回る）も語ごとに違う
const MAXWORDS=8;
const {THREE,scene,createText,text,width,height,fontSize,duration,random,runs,accent}=api;
const clamp=(x,a=0,b=1)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));
const smooth=x=>{x=clamp(x);return x*x*(3-2*x)};
const spring=x=>{x=clamp(x);return 1-(1-x)*Math.exp(-3.4*x)*Math.cos(6.6*x)};
const back=x=>{x=clamp(x)-1;return 1+2.2*x*x*x+1.2*x*x};
const mix=(a,b,k)=>a+(b-a)*k;
const hash=(a,b)=>{const s=Math.sin(a*127.1+b*311.7)*43758.5453;return s-Math.floor(s)};
const W=Math.max(1,width),H=Math.max(1,height),em=Math.max(8,fontSize||H*.18),life=Math.max(.05,duration);
const outro=Math.min(.5,life*.18);
const root=new THREE.Group();scene.add(root);
const lum=c=>{try{const n=new THREE.Color(c).getHex();return (.299*(n>>16&255)+.587*(n>>8&255)+.114*(n&255))/255}catch(e){return .5}};
const shade=(c,to,k)=>{const a=new THREE.Color(c);a.lerp(new THREE.Color(to),k);return '#'+a.getHexString()};
const V=(x,y)=>new THREE.Vector2(x,y);
// 同じ書式（強調・色・大きさ）の中か
function runAtOff(o){let a=0;for(const r of runs||[]){const n=String(r.text||'').length;if(o<a+n)return r;a+=n;}return null;}
function sameRun(a,b){const x=runAtOff(a)||{},y=runAtOff(b)||{};return !!x.emphasis===!!y.emphasis&&(x.color||'')===(y.color||'')&&(x.scale||1)===(y.scale||1);}
// 歌詞を語に区切る（日本語は Intl.Segmenter。句読点は前の語につける。多すぎる語はまとめ、1語だけなら半分に割る）
function splitWords(maxWords){
  const seg=(typeof Intl!=='undefined'&&Intl.Segmenter)?new Intl.Segmenter('ja',{granularity:'word'}):null;
  const out=[];let off=0;
  String(text||'').replace(/\r/g,'').split('\n').forEach((line,row)=>{
    const parts=[];
    if(seg){for(const p of seg.segment(line)){if(p.segment.trim())parts.push({s:p.segment,start:p.index});}}
    else{for(let i=0;i<line.length;i+=2)parts.push({s:line.slice(i,i+2),start:i});}
    const merged=[];
    for(const p of parts){
      const last=merged[merged.length-1];
      if(last&&/^[、。,.!?！？…「」『』（）()・～〜ー]+$/.test(p.s))last.s+=p.s;
      else if(last&&/^[ぁ-ゟー]{1,3}$/.test(p.s)&&last.s.length+p.s.length<=6&&!/[、。,.!?！？…]$/.test(last.s)&&last.start+last.s.length===p.start&&sameRun(last.start+off,p.start+off))last.s+=p.s;   // 助詞や語尾は前の語につける
      else merged.push({...p});
    }
    for(const m of merged)out.push({s:m.s,start:off+m.start,row});
    off+=line.length+1;
  });
  let list=out;
  if(list.length>maxWords){const c=Math.ceil(list.length/maxWords),m=[];for(let i=0;i<list.length;i+=c){const g=list.slice(i,i+c);if(g.every(x=>x.row===g[0].row))m.push({s:g.map(x=>x.s).join(''),start:g[0].start,row:g[0].row});else g.forEach(x=>m.push(x));}list=m;}
  if(list.length===1&&Array.from(list[0].s).length>=2){const a=Array.from(list[0].s),h=Math.ceil(a.length/2),f=a.slice(0,h).join('');list=[{s:f,start:list[0].start,row:list[0].row},{s:a.slice(h).join(''),start:list[0].start+f.length,row:list[0].row}];}
  return list;
}
// 語の強調・色・大きさ（run の scale）を調べる
function runInfo(start,len){
  let o=0,emph=false,col=null,ws=0,ss=0;
  for(const r of runs||[]){
    const n=String(r.text||'').length,a=Math.max(o,start),b=Math.min(o+n,start+len);
    if(b>a){const sc=Number.isFinite(r.scale)&&r.scale>0?r.scale:1;let w=0;for(const ch of Array.from(String(text).slice(a,b)))w+=ch.codePointAt(0)<256?.6:1;w=Math.max(.3,w);ws+=w;ss+=w*sc;if(r.emphasis||r.color)emph=true;if(r.color&&!col)col=r.color;}
    o+=n;if(o>=start+len)break;
  }
  return {emph,col,scale:ws?ss/ws:1};
}
const SIZES=[1.16,.86,1.04,.9,1.22,.92];
const items=splitWords(MAXWORDS).map((w,i)=>{
  const inf=runInfo(w.start,w.s.length);
  return {...w,i,emph:inf.emph,col:inf.col,rs:clamp(inf.scale,.4,3),sz:SIZES[i%SIZES.length]*(.93+random()*.14)*(inf.emph?1.08:1),tw:0,th:0,bw:0,bh:0,x:0,y:0,dy:0,info:null,fx:[]};
});
const nWords=items.length,spread=Math.min(life*.25,(nWords-1)*.26);
items.forEach(it=>{it.t0=nWords>1?spread*it.i/(nWords-1):0;});
// 語の文字メッシュ（強調語の色・縁取り・中抜き・語の大きさは createText が textStart から自動で効かせる）
function makeText(it,ink,extra){
  const opt={textStart:it.start,fontSize:em*it.sz,anchorX:'center',anchorY:'middle',...(extra||{})};if(ink)opt.color=ink;
  const m=createText(it.s,opt);
  m.fillOpacity=0;m.outlineOpacity=0;m.renderOrder=it.i*16+14;
  if(m.material){m.material.depthTest=false;m.material.depthWrite=false;}
  it.mesh=m;return m;
}
function rrPts(w,h,r,cut,cs){
  r=Math.max(.01,Math.min(r,w/2,h/2));const hw=w/2,hh=h/2,P=[];
  const arc=(cx,cy,a0)=>{for(let k=0;k<=6;k++){const a=a0+k*Math.PI/12;P.push(V(cx+r*Math.cos(a),cy+r*Math.sin(a)));}};
  arc(hw-r,-hh+r,-Math.PI/2);
  if(cut>0){P.push(V(hw,hh-cut),V(hw-cut,hh));}else arc(hw-r,hh-r,0);
  arc(-hw+r,hh-r,Math.PI/2);
  arc(-hw+r,-hh+r,Math.PI);
  if(cs<0)for(const p of P)p.x=-p.x;
  return P;
}
const rrGeo=(w,h,r,cut,cs)=>new THREE.ShapeGeometry(new THREE.Shape(rrPts(w,h,r,cut,cs)));
function starGeo(R,r,n,rot){const P=[];for(let k=0;k<n*2;k++){const a=rot+k*Math.PI/n,rr=k%2?r:R;P.push(V(Math.cos(a)*rr,Math.sin(a)*rr));}return new THREE.ShapeGeometry(new THREE.Shape(P));}
// 筆の線：点列を幅つきの帯にして、描画範囲（drawRange）で一筆書きのように伸ばす
function ribbonGeo(pts,wf){
  const n=pts.length,pos=new Float32Array(n*6),idx=[];
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
    const a=pts[Math.max(0,i-1)],b=pts[Math.min(n-1,i+1)];
    let dx=b.x-a.x,dy=b.y-a.y;const l=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;dx/=l;dy/=l;
    const w=wf(i/(n-1),i)/2,nx=-dy*w,ny=dx*w;
    pos.set([pts[i].x+nx,pts[i].y+ny,0,pts[i].x-nx,pts[i].y-ny,0],i*6);
    if(i<n-1){const k=i*2;idx.push(k,k+1,k+2,k+1,k+3,k+2);}
  }
  const g=new THREE.BufferGeometry();g.setAttribute('position',new THREE.BufferAttribute(pos,3));g.setIndex(idx);g.userData.segs=n-1;return g;
}
const drawTo=(m,p)=>{const g=m.geometry,s=g.userData.segs||0;g.setDrawRange(0,Math.floor(s*clamp(p))*6);m.visible=p>.001;};
const mkMat=(c,o=1)=>new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:c,transparent:true,opacity:o,depthTest:false,depthWrite:false,side:THREE.DoubleSide});
// 装飾メッシュ。it.fx に不透明度の基準を覚えさせ、まとめて薄くできる
function deco(it,geo,c,o,order){
  const m=new THREE.Mesh(geo,mkMat(c,o==null?1:o));m.renderOrder=it.i*16+order;it.fx.push([m.material,o==null?1:o]);return m;
}
function regeo(m,geo){if(m.geometry)m.geometry.dispose();m.geometry=geo;}
function fade(it,a){for(const f of it.fx)f[0].opacity=f[1]*a;}
function measure(it){
  const r=it.mesh&&it.mesh.textRenderInfo,b=r&&r.blockBounds;
  let base;
  if(b&&b.length===4&&b.every(Number.isFinite))base=Math.max(em*it.sz*.3,b[2]-b[0]);
  else{let n=0;for(const ch of Array.from(it.s))n+=ch.codePointAt(0)<256?.6:1;base=em*it.sz*n;}
  it.tw=base*it.rs;it.th=em*it.sz*1.12*Math.max(.7,it.rs);
}
let fit=1,laid=false,dead=false,FITW=.86,FITH=.7,GAPX=.22,GAPY=.1,boxW=1,boxH=1;
function layout(){
  for(const it of items){measure(it);size(it);}
  const gap=em*GAPX,limit=W*FITW,rows=[],hard=[];
  for(const it of items)(hard[it.row]=hard[it.row]||[]).push(it);
  for(const row of hard){if(!row)continue;
    const total=row.reduce((s,l,k)=>s+l.bw+(k?gap:0),0),n=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(total/limit)),cap=n>1?Math.min(limit,total/n*1.12+gap):limit;
    let cur=[],x=0;
    for(const l of row){if(cur.length&&x+gap+l.bw>cap){rows.push(cur);cur=[];x=0;}x+=(cur.length?gap:0)+l.bw;cur.push(l);}
    rows.push(cur);}
  const rowH=rows.map(r=>Math.max(...r.map(l=>l.bh))),vgap=em*GAPY,totH=rowH.reduce((a,h)=>a+h,0)+vgap*(rows.length-1);
  let y=totH/2,maxW=1;
  rows.forEach((row,r)=>{
    let x=0;for(const l of row){l.x=x+l.bw/2;x+=l.bw+gap;}
    const tw=x-gap;maxW=Math.max(maxW,tw);
    const cy=y-rowH[r]/2;for(const l of row){l.x-=tw/2;l.y=cy+l.dy;}
    y-=rowH[r]+vgap;
  });
  fit=Math.min(1,W*FITW/maxW,H*FITH/totH);root.scale.setScalar(fit);boxW=maxW;boxH=totH;
  if(typeof afterLayout==='function')afterLayout();
}
// ---- ワッペン・バッジ ----
// [地, ふち, 縫い糸, 文字]
const PAIRS=[['#1f3a8a','#f4c430','#f6e7a1','#ffffff'],['#c0392b','#fff4e0','#ffe0a8','#ffffff'],['#14746f','#f2d16b','#fdf3c8','#ffffff'],['#f7e7b4','#2b2d42','#2b2d42','#2b2d42'],['#7b4fc4','#ffd6e8','#ffe9f2','#ffffff'],['#f2a43a','#2b2d42','#2b2d42','#2b2d42'],['#2e8b57','#fff4e0','#d8f5df','#ffffff']];
function ringPts(kind,w,h){
  const rx=w/2,ry=h/2,P=[];
  if(kind==='circle'){for(let k=0;k<48;k++){const a=k/48*Math.PI*2;P.push(V(Math.cos(a)*rx,Math.sin(a)*ry));}}
  else if(kind==='scallop'){for(let k=0;k<120;k++){const a=k/120*Math.PI*2,m=1+.06*Math.cos(a*13);P.push(V(Math.cos(a)*rx*m,Math.sin(a)*ry*m));}}
  else if(kind==='hex'){const c=Math.min(ry*.9,rx*.5);P.push(V(-rx,0),V(-rx+c,ry),V(rx-c,ry),V(rx,0),V(rx-c,-ry),V(-rx+c,-ry));}
  else return rrPts(w,h,Math.min(rx,ry)*.5,0,1);
  return P;
}
function resample(P,step){
  const out=[],n=P.length;let carry=0;
  for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
    const a=P[i],b=P[(i+1)%n],len=Math.hypot(b.x-a.x,b.y-a.y);
    let d=carry;
    while(d<len){const k=d/len;out.push(V(a.x+(b.x-a.x)*k,a.y+(b.y-a.y)*k));d+=step;}
    carry=d-len;
  }
  return out;
}
for(const it of items){
  const nCh=Array.from(it.s).length;
  it.kind=it.emph?'scallop':nCh<=2?(it.i%2?'circle':'scallop'):(it.i%2?'rrect':'hex');
  let pair=PAIRS[(it.i*3+Math.floor(random()*2))%PAIRS.length];
  if(it.emph)pair=lum(it.col||accent)>.5?['#1f2a5a','#ffd23f','#fff3c0','#ffffff']:['#fff4e0','#c0392b','#e8a08f','#2b2d42'];
  it.pair=pair;it.mode=it.i%2;it.dir=random()<.5?1:-1;it.tilt=(random()-.5)*.12;it.dy=(random()-.5)*em*.14;
  const g=new THREE.Group();g.visible=false;root.add(g);it.g=g;
  it.shadow=deco(it,new THREE.BufferGeometry(),'#0b0d16',.38,0);
  it.rim=deco(it,new THREE.BufferGeometry(),pair[1],1,1);
  it.face=deco(it,new THREE.BufferGeometry(),pair[0],1,2);
  it.back=deco(it,new THREE.BufferGeometry(),pair[2],1,3);
  it.stitch=deco(it,new THREE.BufferGeometry(),pair[2],1,4);
  it.stars=deco(it,new THREE.BufferGeometry(),pair[2],1,5);
  it.veil=deco(it,new THREE.BufferGeometry(),'#000000',0,9);
  g.add(it.shadow,it.rim,it.face,it.back,it.stitch,it.stars,it.veil);
  const tx=makeText(it,pair[3]);g.add(tx);
}
function size(it){
  const sz=it.sz,tw=it.tw,th=it.th,k=it.kind;
  let fw,fh;
  if(k==='circle'||k==='scallop'){fw=tw*1.28+em*.34*sz;fh=th*1.26+em*.22*sz;if(nChars(it)===1){fw=fh=Math.max(fw,fh);}}
  else{fw=tw+em*.9*sz;fh=th+em*.34*sz;}
  const e=Math.max(3,em*.1*sz),s=Math.max(2,em*.07*sz);
  Object.assign(it,{fw,fh,e});
  const pts=P=>new THREE.ShapeGeometry(new THREE.Shape(P));
  regeo(it.shadow,pts(ringPts(k,fw+2*e,fh+2*e)));it.shadow.position.set(e*.7,-e*1.1,0);
  regeo(it.rim,pts(ringPts(k,fw+2*e,fh+2*e)));
  regeo(it.face,pts(ringPts(k,fw,fh)));
  // 縫い目：内側に沿って短い糸を並べる
  const ring=resample(ringPts(k,fw-2*s*1.6,fh-2*s*1.6),Math.max(5,em*.12*sz)),shapes=[],dl=Math.max(2.5,em*.06*sz),dw=Math.max(1.2,em*.024*sz);
  const cap=Math.min(ring.length,110);
  for(let j=0;j<cap;j++){
    const a=ring[j],b=ring[(j+1)%ring.length];let dx=b.x-a.x,dy=b.y-a.y;const l=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;dx/=l;dy/=l;
    const nx=-dy*dw/2,ny=dx*dw/2,ex=dx*dl;
    shapes.push(new THREE.Shape([V(a.x+nx,a.y+ny),V(a.x+ex+nx,a.y+dy*dl+ny),V(a.x+ex-nx,a.y+dy*dl-ny),V(a.x-nx,a.y-ny)]));
  }
  regeo(it.stitch,new THREE.ShapeGeometry(shapes));
  // 左右の小さな星（横長のとき）
  const sh=[];
  if((k==='rrect'||k==='hex')){const R=em*.1*sz,x=tw/2+(fw-tw)/4;for(const sx of [-1,1]){const P=[];for(let j=0;j<10;j++){const a=Math.PI/2+j*Math.PI/5,r=j%2?R*.45:R;P.push(V(sx*x+Math.cos(a)*r,Math.sin(a)*r));}sh.push(new THREE.Shape(P));}}
  regeo(it.stars,sh.length?new THREE.ShapeGeometry(sh):new THREE.BufferGeometry());
  {const R=Math.min(fw,fh)*.3,P=[];for(let j=0;j<10;j++){const a=Math.PI/2+j*Math.PI/5,r=j%2?R*.42:R;P.push(V(Math.cos(a)*r,Math.sin(a)*r));}regeo(it.back,pts(P));}
  regeo(it.veil,pts(ringPts(k,fw+2*e,fh+2*e)));
  it.bw=fw+2*e+e*2;it.bh=fh+2*e+e*2+em*.12;
}
function nChars(it){return Array.from(it.s).length;}
function pose(it,t,lt,q){
  const g=it.g;
  if(lt<0||q<=0.001){g.visible=false;it.mesh.visible=false;return;}
  g.visible=true;
  const D=.7,u=clamp(lt/D),e=1-Math.pow(1-u,3),land=Math.max(0,lt-D);
  const ex=1-q;
  let sx=1,front=true,rot=it.tilt;
  const hop=Math.sin(Math.PI*u)*em*.38*(1-.4*u);
  if(it.mode===0){const th=(1-e)*Math.PI*3;const c=Math.cos(th);sx=Math.max(.03,Math.abs(c));front=c>0;}
  else{rot+=(1-e)*Math.PI*4*it.dir;}
  const pop=mix(.25,1,smooth(lt/.22))*(lt>D?1-.1*Math.exp(-8*land)*Math.cos(16*land):1)*(1-.3*ex);
  rot+=land>0?.025*Math.sin(t*2.3+it.i*1.3)*smooth(land*3):0;
  g.position.set(it.x,it.y+hop-em*.1*ex,0);g.rotation.z=rot;g.scale.set(pop*sx,pop,1);
  const a=smooth(lt/.08)*q;
  fade(it,a);it.shadow.material.opacity=.38*a;
  it.veil.material.opacity=(1-sx)*.5*a;
  it.back.visible=!front&&a>.01;it.stitch.visible=front&&a>.01;it.stars.visible=front&&a>.01;
  it.mesh.visible=front&&a>.002;it.mesh.fillOpacity=a;it.mesh.outlineOpacity=a;
}
function update(time){
  if(dead)return;
  const t=clamp(Number.isFinite(time)?time:0,0,life);
  let changed=!laid;
  for(const it of items){const r=it.mesh&&it.mesh.textRenderInfo;if(r&&it.info!==r){it.info=r;changed=true;}}
  if(changed){layout();laid=true;}
  const q=smooth(clamp((life-t)/outro));
  for(const it of items)pose(it,t,t-it.t0,q);
  if(typeof frame==='function')frame(t,q);
}
function dispose(){
  if(dead)return;dead=true;
  root.traverse(o=>{
    if(typeof o.dispose==='function'){o.dispose();return;}
    if(o.geometry)o.geometry.dispose();
    if(o.material&&o.material.dispose)o.material.dispose();
  });
  scene.remove(root);
}
update(0);
return {update,dispose};
文字モーション（コード）10/14 追加

落落書きになって散るSCRIBBLE-SCATTER

絵が線画の落書きになって、揺れながらばらばらに散っていきます。

コードを見る
// 絵が線画の落書きになって、揺れながらばらばらに散っていく
vec3 userMethod(vec2 p, float amount) {
  vec2 c = partC(), l = p - c;
  float e = smoothstep(ph(1.) * .2, .45 + ph(1.) * .3, amount);
  float a = ph(2.) * 6.2832 + uTime * (3. + ph(3.) * 4.);
  vec2 scrawl = vec2(cos(a), sin(a * 1.37)) * (.05 + .14 * ph(4.)) * e;
  vec2 drift = (vec2(ph(5.), ph(6.)) - .5) * 1.6 * e;
  vec2 q = c + rot((ph(7.) - .5) * 2.4 * e) * l * (1. + e * 1.2) + scrawl + drift;
  gKind = 9.;
  return vec3(q, (ph(8.) - .5) * .5 * e);
}

絡線がぐるぐる絡むLINE-TANGLE

線画になった粒が、渦を巻きながら中心へ絡まっていきます。

コードを見る
// 線画になった粒が、渦を巻きながら中心へ絡まっていく
vec3 userMethod(vec2 p, float amount) {
  vec2 c = partC(), l = p - c;
  float r = length(c) / 3.;
  float e = smoothstep(r * .15, .5 + r * .3, amount);
  float turn = e * (3. + 2.5 * sin(r * 7. + uTime * .5)) * (1.2 - r);
  vec2 q = rot(turn) * c * (1. - .5 * e) + rot(turn * 1.5) * l * (1. + e * 1.6);
  q += vec2(sin(c.y * 6. + uTime * 2.), cos(c.x * 6. + uTime * 2.3)) * .05 * e;
  gKind = 9.;
  return vec3(q, sin(turn) * .3 * e);
}

殴ペンで殴り描きPEN-SHAKE

絵が線画になり、ペンで殴り描きするように細かく震えます。

コードを見る
// 絵が線画になり、ペンで殴り描きするように細かく震える
vec3 userMethod(vec2 p, float amount) {
  vec2 c = partC(), l = p - c;
  float e = smoothstep(0., .4, amount);
  vec2 jit = vec2(sin(c.y * 11. + uTime * 17.), cos(c.x * 11. + uTime * 13.)) * .035 * e
           + vec2(sin(uTime * 29. + c.x * 5.), cos(uTime * 31. + c.y * 5.)) * .02 * e;
  vec2 q = c + l * (1. + .9 * e) + jit;
  gKind = 9.;
  return vec3(q, 0.);
}

崩線画が崩れ落ちるLINE-FALL

線画になった絵が、上から順に切れ落ちるように崩れていきます。

コードを見る
// 線画になった絵が、上から順に切れ落ちるように崩れていく
vec3 userMethod(vec2 p, float amount) {
  vec2 c = partC(), l = p - c;
  float top = clamp(.5 - c.y / uSize.y, 0., 1.);
  float e = smoothstep(top * .35 + ph(1.) * .12, top * .35 + .38 + ph(1.) * .12, amount);
  float fall = e * e * (1.2 + ph(2.) * 1.8);
  vec2 q = c + rot((ph(3.) - .5) * 3. * e) * l + vec2((ph(4.) - .5) * .5 * e, -fall);
  gKind = 9.;
  return vec3(q, (ph(5.) - .5) * .4 * e);
}

糸ほどけて糸になるTHREAD-UNRAVEL

絵がほどけて、細い糸のように長く伸びて流れていきます。

コードを見る
// 絵がほどけて、細い糸のように長く伸びて流れていく
vec3 userMethod(vec2 p, float amount) {
  vec2 c = partC(), l = p - c;
  float e = smoothstep(ph(1.) * .25, .5 + ph(1.) * .25, amount);
  float ang = (ph(2.) - .5) * .9 + sin(c.y * 2. + uTime) * .2;
  vec2 along = vec2(cos(ang), sin(ang)), across = vec2(-along.y, along.x);
  vec2 stretched = along * dot(l, along) * (1. + e * 5.) + across * dot(l, across) * (1. - .5 * e);
  vec2 q = c + stretched + along * e * (.4 + ph(3.) * 1.2) + vec2(0., sin(uTime * 1.5 + c.x * 2.) * .1 * e);
  gKind = 9.;
  return vec3(q, 0.);
}

滲色が滲んで広がるCOLOR-BLEED

色が水に滲むように粒がぼやけて広がり、まわりと混ざります。

コードを見る
// 色が水に滲むように、粒がぼやけて広がり、まわりの色と混ざっていく
vec3 userMethod(vec2 p, float amount) {
  vec2 c = partC(), l = p - c;
  float e = smoothstep(ph(1.) * .2, .5 + ph(1.) * .25, amount);
  vec2 flow = vec2(sin(c.y * 1.8 + uTime * .6), cos(c.x * 1.4 + uTime * .5)) * .15 * e;
  vec2 q = c + l * (1. + e * 3.4) + flow;
  gKind = 2.; gLocal = 1.;
  gAlpha = mix(1., .5, e);
  return vec3(q, -e * .15 * ph(2.));
}

// 絵の具が溶けて、色の筋になって下へ垂れていく
vec3 userMethod(vec2 p, float amount) {
  vec2 c = partC(), l = p - c;
  float e = smoothstep(ph(1.) * .3, .55 + ph(1.) * .25, amount);
  float len = .3 + ph(2.) * 1.6;
  vec2 q = c + vec2(l.x * (1. - .35 * e) + sin(uTime * 1.2 + ph(3.) * 6.) * .03 * e, l.y * (1. + e * 6.) - e * len);
  gKind = 2.; gLocal = 1.;
  gAlpha = mix(1., .7, e);
  return vec3(q, 0.);
}

溢中心から滲み出すBLEED-OUT

色が中心から外へ滲み出して、やわらかく広がっていきます。

コードを見る
// 色が中心から外へ滲み出して、やわらかく広がっていく
vec3 userMethod(vec2 p, float amount) {
  vec2 c = partC(), l = p - c;
  float r = length(c);
  float e = smoothstep(r * .12, .45 + r * .12 + ph(1.) * .15, amount);
  vec2 dir = normalize(c + vec2(.001));
  vec2 q = c + dir * e * (.5 + ph(2.) * .9) + l * (1. + e * 2.8);
  gKind = 2.; gLocal = 1.;
  gAlpha = mix(1., .55, e);
  return vec3(q, 0.);
}

文切り貼り文字（ゆっくり）RANSOM-SLOW

書体も紙も傾きも違う切り抜き文字を、1拍に1文字ずつゆっくり貼ります。

メソッド工房の「文字モーション」のサンプルから選びます。

コードを見る
// @fonts
// 切り貼り文字（ゆっくり）: 1文字ごとに書体・紙の色・大きさ・傾きが違う切り抜き文字を、拍に合わせて1文字ずつ貼っていく。貼るたびに画面が拍で揺れる。
// 貼る速さは1拍の何分の1か（1）と、貼り終えるまでに使う割合（0.8）、歌詞の「動きの速さ」で変わる。api.fonts / api.fontsFor は「// @fonts」の行があると使える。
const {THREE,scene,createText,text,width,height,fontSize,duration,bpm,random,fonts,fontsFor,fontURL,color,accent,runs}=api;
const clamp=(x,a=0,b=1)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));
const smooth=x=>{x=clamp(x);return x*x*(3-2*x)};
const back=x=>{x=clamp(x)-1;return 1+2.4*x*x*x+1.4*x*x};
const mix=(a,b,k)=>a+(b-a)*k;
const hash=(a,b)=>{const s=Math.sin(a*127.1+b*311.7)*43758.5453;return s-Math.floor(s)};
const W=Math.max(1,width),H=Math.max(1,height),em=Math.max(8,fontSize||H*.15),life=Math.max(.05,duration);
const SHAKE=0.8,DIV=1,FILL=0.8;      // FILL: 歌詞の表示時間のうち、貼り終えるまでに使う割合の上限
// 紙と文字の色の組（切り抜きの紙、文字）
const PAPERS=[['#faf9f4','#141316'],['#ece8dc','#141316'],['#cec9bc','#141316'],['#141316','#faf9f4'],['#60b6ba','#141316'],['#e45a8c','#faf9f4'],['#f4d632','#141316'],['#faf9f4','#e45a8c']];
const luma=hex=>{const n=parseInt(String(hex).replace('#','').slice(0,6),16)||0;return (.299*(n>>16&255)+.587*(n>>8&255)+.114*(n&255))/255};
const runAt=offset=>{let o=0;for(const r of runs||[]){const n=String(r.text||'').length;if(offset<o+n)return r;o+=n}return null};
// 文字を1つずつ作る（空白は間、改行は行替え）
const rows=[[]];let offset=0,visibleCount=0;
for(const ch of Array.from(String(text||'').replace(/\r/g,''))){
  const len=ch.length;
  if(ch==='\n'){rows.push([]);offset+=len;continue}
  if(/\s/.test(ch)){rows[rows.length-1].push({gap:true,w:em*.4});offset+=len;continue}
  const run=runAt(offset),cp=ch.codePointAt(0);
  const sc=.84+random()*.4,fs=em*sc,adv=(cp<256?.62:1)*fs;
  const choices=[...fontsFor(ch).map(k=>fonts[k]),fontURL];          // その字を持っている書体だけ
  const font=choices[Math.floor(random()*choices.length)];
  let paper,ink;
  if(run&&run.color){paper=run.color;ink=luma(run.color)>.55?'#141316':'#faf9f4'}
  else if(run&&run.emphasis){paper=accent;ink=luma(accent)>.55?'#141316':'#faf9f4'}
  else{const p=PAPERS[Math.floor(random()*PAPERS.length)];paper=p[0];ink=p[1]}
  rows[rows.length-1].push({ch,font,fs,w:adv+fs*.3,h:fs*1.18,paper,ink,rot:(random()-.5)*.31,dy:(random()-.5)*.2*fs,tape:random()<.18,tapeRot:(random()-.5)*1.05,jit:[random(),random(),random(),random(),random(),random(),random(),random()],slam:(random()-.5)*.7,idx:visibleCount++});
  offset+=len;
}
// 行に分ける（はみ出す行は折り返し、全体が画面に収まるよう縮める）
const limit=W*.86,lines=[];
for(const row of rows){let cur=[],w=0;for(const it of row){const iw=it.w*.94;if(cur.length&&w+iw>limit&&!it.gap){lines.push(cur);cur=[];w=0}cur.push(it);w+=iw}lines.push(cur)}
const rowW=r=>r.reduce((a,it)=>a+it.w*.94,0),rowH=r=>Math.max(em*.9,...r.filter(it=>!it.gap).map(it=>it.h));
const totalH=lines.reduce((a,r)=>a+rowH(r)*1.12,0);
const k=Math.min(1,limit/Math.max(1,...lines.map(rowW)),H*.74/Math.max(1,totalH));
const root=new THREE.Group();scene.add(root);
const mats=[],geos=[];
const chars=[];
let y=totalH*k/2;
for(const r of lines){
  const rh=rowH(r)*k*1.12;y-=rh/2;
  let x=-rowW(r)*k/2;
  for(const it of r){
    const w=it.w*k*.94;
    if(it.gap){x+=w;continue}
    const fs=it.fs*k,tw=it.w*k,th=it.h*k,g=new THREE.Group(),j=it.jit;
    const jt=n=>(j[n]-.5)*fs*.1;
    const shape=new THREE.Shape([new THREE.Vector2(-tw/2+jt(0),-th/2+jt(1)),new THREE.Vector2(tw/2+jt(2),-th/2+jt(3)),new THREE.Vector2(tw/2+jt(4),th/2+jt(5)),new THREE.Vector2(-tw/2+jt(6),th/2+jt(7))]);
    const geo=new THREE.ShapeGeometry(shape);geos.push(geo);
    const mk=(c,o)=>{const m=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:c,transparent:true,opacity:o,depthWrite:false,depthTest:false});mats.push(m);return m};
    const shadow=new THREE.Mesh(geo,mk('#000000',.36)),tile=new THREE.Mesh(geo,mk(it.paper,1));
    shadow.position.set(fs*.06,-fs*.08,0);
    const base=it.idx*4;shadow.renderOrder=base;tile.renderOrder=base+1;
    const label=createText(it.ch,{font:it.font,fontSize:fs,color:it.ink,colorRanges:null,anchorX:'center',anchorY:'middle'});
    label.renderOrder=base+2;label.material.depthTest=false;label.material.depthWrite=false;
    g.add(shadow,tile,label);
    let tape=null;
    if(it.tape){tape=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(tw*.9,th*.2),mk('#eee8c8',.55));geos.push(tape.geometry);tape.position.set(0,th*.52,0);tape.rotation.z=it.tapeRot;tape.renderOrder=base+3;g.add(tape)}
    g.position.set(x+w/2,y+it.dy*k,0);g.rotation.z=it.rot;g.visible=false;
    root.add(g);
    chars.push({g,it,label,shadow,tile,tape,x:g.position.x,y:g.position.y});
    x+=w;
  }
  y-=rh/2;
}
const n=Math.max(1,chars.length),step=Math.max(.03,Math.min(60/Math.max(40,bpm||120)/DIV,life*FILL/n)),t0=.05,exit=Math.min(.35,life*.15);
function update(t){
  const tick=Math.floor(t*12),ph=((t*Math.max(40,bpm||120)/60)%1+1)%1,punch=Math.exp(-ph*6);
  const sh=em*.03*SHAKE*(.5+punch);
  root.position.set((hash(tick,1)-.5)*2*sh,(hash(tick,2)-.5)*2*sh,0);
  root.rotation.z=(hash(tick,3)-.5)*.012*SHAKE;
  const zoom=1+.018*punch*SHAKE;root.scale.set(zoom,zoom,1);
  const fade=t>=life?0:smooth((life-t)/exit);
  for(const c of chars){
    const ti=t0+c.it.idx*step,u=(t-ti)/.15;
    if(u<=0||fade<=0){c.g.visible=false;continue}
    c.g.visible=true;
    const e=back(u),a=smooth(u*4)*fade;
    c.g.scale.setScalar(mix(1.7,1,e));
    c.g.position.set(c.x+(hash(c.it.idx,tick)-.5)*em*.035,c.y+(hash(c.it.idx,tick+7)-.5)*em*.035,0);
    c.g.rotation.z=c.it.rot+(1-e)*c.it.slam+(hash(c.it.idx,tick+3)-.5)*.016;
    c.label.fillOpacity=a;c.shadow.material.opacity=.36*a;c.tile.material.opacity=a;if(c.tape)c.tape.material.opacity=.55*a;
  }
}
function dispose(){for(const m of mats)m.dispose();for(const g of geos)g.dispose();scene.remove(root)}
update(0);
return {update,dispose};
文字モーション（コード）10/12 追加

文切り貼り文字RANSOM-PASTE

書体も紙も傾きも違う切り抜き文字を、拍に合わせて貼っていきます。

メソッド工房の「文字モーション」のサンプルから選びます。

コードを見る
// @fonts
// 切り貼り文字: 1文字ごとに書体・紙の色・大きさ・傾きが違う切り抜き文字を、拍に合わせて1文字ずつ貼っていく。貼るたびに画面が拍で揺れる。
// 貼る速さは1拍の何分の1か（2）と、貼り終えるまでに使う割合（0.6）、歌詞の「動きの速さ」で変わる。api.fonts / api.fontsFor は「// @fonts」の行があると使える。
const {THREE,scene,createText,text,width,height,fontSize,duration,bpm,random,fonts,fontsFor,fontURL,color,accent,runs}=api;
const clamp=(x,a=0,b=1)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));
const smooth=x=>{x=clamp(x);return x*x*(3-2*x)};
const back=x=>{x=clamp(x)-1;return 1+2.4*x*x*x+1.4*x*x};
const mix=(a,b,k)=>a+(b-a)*k;
const hash=(a,b)=>{const s=Math.sin(a*127.1+b*311.7)*43758.5453;return s-Math.floor(s)};
const W=Math.max(1,width),H=Math.max(1,height),em=Math.max(8,fontSize||H*.15),life=Math.max(.05,duration);
const SHAKE=1,DIV=2,FILL=0.6;      // FILL: 歌詞の表示時間のうち、貼り終えるまでに使う割合の上限
// 紙と文字の色の組（切り抜きの紙、文字）
const PAPERS=[['#faf9f4','#141316'],['#ece8dc','#141316'],['#cec9bc','#141316'],['#141316','#faf9f4'],['#60b6ba','#141316'],['#e45a8c','#faf9f4'],['#f4d632','#141316'],['#faf9f4','#e45a8c']];
const luma=hex=>{const n=parseInt(String(hex).replace('#','').slice(0,6),16)||0;return (.299*(n>>16&255)+.587*(n>>8&255)+.114*(n&255))/255};
const runAt=offset=>{let o=0;for(const r of runs||[]){const n=String(r.text||'').length;if(offset<o+n)return r;o+=n}return null};
// 文字を1つずつ作る（空白は間、改行は行替え）
const rows=[[]];let offset=0,visibleCount=0;
for(const ch of Array.from(String(text||'').replace(/\r/g,''))){
  const len=ch.length;
  if(ch==='\n'){rows.push([]);offset+=len;continue}
  if(/\s/.test(ch)){rows[rows.length-1].push({gap:true,w:em*.4});offset+=len;continue}
  const run=runAt(offset),cp=ch.codePointAt(0);
  const sc=.84+random()*.4,fs=em*sc,adv=(cp<256?.62:1)*fs;
  const choices=[...fontsFor(ch).map(k=>fonts[k]),fontURL];          // その字を持っている書体だけ
  const font=choices[Math.floor(random()*choices.length)];
  let paper,ink;
  if(run&&run.color){paper=run.color;ink=luma(run.color)>.55?'#141316':'#faf9f4'}
  else if(run&&run.emphasis){paper=accent;ink=luma(accent)>.55?'#141316':'#faf9f4'}
  else{const p=PAPERS[Math.floor(random()*PAPERS.length)];paper=p[0];ink=p[1]}
  rows[rows.length-1].push({ch,font,fs,w:adv+fs*.3,h:fs*1.18,paper,ink,rot:(random()-.5)*.31,dy:(random()-.5)*.2*fs,tape:random()<.18,tapeRot:(random()-.5)*1.05,jit:[random(),random(),random(),random(),random(),random(),random(),random()],slam:(random()-.5)*.7,idx:visibleCount++});
  offset+=len;
}
// 行に分ける（はみ出す行は折り返し、全体が画面に収まるよう縮める）
const limit=W*.86,lines=[];
for(const row of rows){let cur=[],w=0;for(const it of row){const iw=it.w*.94;if(cur.length&&w+iw>limit&&!it.gap){lines.push(cur);cur=[];w=0}cur.push(it);w+=iw}lines.push(cur)}
const rowW=r=>r.reduce((a,it)=>a+it.w*.94,0),rowH=r=>Math.max(em*.9,...r.filter(it=>!it.gap).map(it=>it.h));
const totalH=lines.reduce((a,r)=>a+rowH(r)*1.12,0);
const k=Math.min(1,limit/Math.max(1,...lines.map(rowW)),H*.74/Math.max(1,totalH));
const root=new THREE.Group();scene.add(root);
const mats=[],geos=[];
const chars=[];
let y=totalH*k/2;
for(const r of lines){
  const rh=rowH(r)*k*1.12;y-=rh/2;
  let x=-rowW(r)*k/2;
  for(const it of r){
    const w=it.w*k*.94;
    if(it.gap){x+=w;continue}
    const fs=it.fs*k,tw=it.w*k,th=it.h*k,g=new THREE.Group(),j=it.jit;
    const jt=n=>(j[n]-.5)*fs*.1;
    const shape=new THREE.Shape([new THREE.Vector2(-tw/2+jt(0),-th/2+jt(1)),new THREE.Vector2(tw/2+jt(2),-th/2+jt(3)),new THREE.Vector2(tw/2+jt(4),th/2+jt(5)),new THREE.Vector2(-tw/2+jt(6),th/2+jt(7))]);
    const geo=new THREE.ShapeGeometry(shape);geos.push(geo);
    const mk=(c,o)=>{const m=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:c,transparent:true,opacity:o,depthWrite:false,depthTest:false});mats.push(m);return m};
    const shadow=new THREE.Mesh(geo,mk('#000000',.36)),tile=new THREE.Mesh(geo,mk(it.paper,1));
    shadow.position.set(fs*.06,-fs*.08,0);
    const base=it.idx*4;shadow.renderOrder=base;tile.renderOrder=base+1;
    const label=createText(it.ch,{font:it.font,fontSize:fs,color:it.ink,colorRanges:null,anchorX:'center',anchorY:'middle'});
    label.renderOrder=base+2;label.material.depthTest=false;label.material.depthWrite=false;
    g.add(shadow,tile,label);
    let tape=null;
    if(it.tape){tape=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(tw*.9,th*.2),mk('#eee8c8',.55));geos.push(tape.geometry);tape.position.set(0,th*.52,0);tape.rotation.z=it.tapeRot;tape.renderOrder=base+3;g.add(tape)}
    g.position.set(x+w/2,y+it.dy*k,0);g.rotation.z=it.rot;g.visible=false;
    root.add(g);
    chars.push({g,it,label,shadow,tile,tape,x:g.position.x,y:g.position.y});
    x+=w;
  }
  y-=rh/2;
}
const n=Math.max(1,chars.length),step=Math.max(.03,Math.min(60/Math.max(40,bpm||120)/DIV,life*FILL/n)),t0=.05,exit=Math.min(.35,life*.15);
function update(t){
  const tick=Math.floor(t*12),ph=((t*Math.max(40,bpm||120)/60)%1+1)%1,punch=Math.exp(-ph*6);
  const sh=em*.03*SHAKE*(.5+punch);
  root.position.set((hash(tick,1)-.5)*2*sh,(hash(tick,2)-.5)*2*sh,0);
  root.rotation.z=(hash(tick,3)-.5)*.012*SHAKE;
  const zoom=1+.018*punch*SHAKE;root.scale.set(zoom,zoom,1);
  const fade=t>=life?0:smooth((life-t)/exit);
  for(const c of chars){
    const ti=t0+c.it.idx*step,u=(t-ti)/.15;
    if(u<=0||fade<=0){c.g.visible=false;continue}
    c.g.visible=true;
    const e=back(u),a=smooth(u*4)*fade;
    c.g.scale.setScalar(mix(1.7,1,e));
    c.g.position.set(c.x+(hash(c.it.idx,tick)-.5)*em*.035,c.y+(hash(c.it.idx,tick+7)-.5)*em*.035,0);
    c.g.rotation.z=c.it.rot+(1-e)*c.it.slam+(hash(c.it.idx,tick+3)-.5)*.016;
    c.label.fillOpacity=a;c.shadow.material.opacity=.36*a;c.tile.material.opacity=a;if(c.tape)c.tape.material.opacity=.55*a;
  }
}
function dispose(){for(const m of mats)m.dispose();for(const g of geos)g.dispose();scene.remove(root)}
update(0);
return {update,dispose};
文字モーション（コード）10/12 追加

文切り貼り文字（連打）RANSOM-RAPID

切り抜き文字を拍の4分で素早く貼り、画面を大きく揺らします。

メソッド工房の「文字モーション」のサンプルから選びます。

コードを見る

// 切り貼り文字（連打）: 1文字ごとに書体・紙の色・大きさ・傾きが違う切り抜き文字を、拍に合わせて1文字ずつ貼っていく。貼るたびに画面が拍で揺れる。
// 貼る速さは1拍の何分の1か（4）と、貼り終えるまでに使う割合（0.45）、歌詞の「動きの速さ」で変わる。api.fonts / api.fontsFor は「// @fonts」の行があると使える。

const clamp=(x,a=0,b=1)=>Math.max(a,Math.min(b,x));

const back=x=>{x=clamp(x)-1;return 1+2.4*x*x*x+1.4*x*x};
const mix=(a,b,k)=>a+(b-a)*k;
const hash=(a,b)=>{const s=Math.sin(a*127.1+b*311.7)*43758.5453;return s-Math.floor(s)};
const W=Math.max(1,width),H=Math.max(1,height),em=Math.max(8,fontSize||H*.15),life=Math.max(.05,duration);
const SHAKE=1.7,DIV=4,FILL=0.45;      // FILL: 歌詞の表示時間のうち、貼り終えるまでに使う割合の上限

const runAt=offset=>{let o=0;for(const r of runs||[]){const n=String(r.text||'').length;if(offset<o+n)return r;o+=n}return null};
// 文字を1つずつ作る（空白は間、改行は行替え）

for(const ch of Array.from(String(text||'').replace(/\r/g,''))){

  if(ch==='\n'){rows.push([]);offset+=len;continue}
  if(/\s/.test(ch)){rows[rows.length-1].push({gap:true,w:em*.4});offset+=len;continue}
  const run=runAt(offset),cp=ch.codePointAt(0);
  const sc=.84+random()*.4,fs=em*sc,adv=(cp<256?.62:1)*fs;
  const choices=[...fontsFor(ch).map(k=>fonts[k]),fontURL];          // その字を持っている書体だけ

  let paper,ink;
  if(run&&run.color){paper=run.color;ink=luma(run.color)>.55?'#141316':'#faf9f4'}
  else if(run&&run.emphasis){paper=accent;ink=luma(accent)>.55?'#141316':'#faf9f4'}
  else{const p=PAPERS[Math.floor(random()*PAPERS.length)];paper=p[0];ink=p[1]}
  rows[rows.length-1].push({ch,font,fs,w:adv+fs*.3,h:fs*1.18,paper,ink,rot:(random()-.5)*.31,dy:(random()-.5)*.2*fs,tape:random()<.18,tapeRot:(random()-.5)*1.05,jit:[random(),random(),random(),random(),random(),random(),random(),random()],slam:(random()-.5)*.7,idx:visibleCount++});
  offset+=len;
}
// 行に分ける（はみ出す行は折り返し、全体が画面に収まるよう縮める）
const limit=W*.86,lines=[];
for(const row of rows){let cur=[],w=0;for(const it of row){const iw=it.w*.94;if(cur.length&&w+iw>limit&&!it.gap){lines.push(cur);cur=[];w=0}cur.push(it);w+=iw}lines.push(cur)}
const rowW=r=>r.reduce((a,it)=>a+it.w*.94,0),rowH=r=>Math.max(em*.9,...r.filter(it=>!it.gap).map(it=>it.h));

    const shape=new THREE.Shape([new THREE.Vector2(-tw/2+jt(0),-th/2+jt(1)),new THREE.Vector2(tw/2+jt(2),-th/2+jt(3)),new THREE.Vector2(tw/2+jt(4),th/2+jt(5)),new THREE.Vector2(-tw/2+jt(6),th/2+jt(7))]);

    const mk=(c,o)=>{const m=new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:c,transparent:true,opacity:o,depthWrite:false,depthTest:false});mats.push(m);return m};

    shadow.position.set(fs*.06,-fs*.08,0);
    const base=it.idx*4;shadow.renderOrder=base;tile.renderOrder=base+1;
    const label=createText(it.ch,{font:it.font,fontSize:fs,color:it.ink,colorRanges:null,anchorX:'center',anchorY:'middle'});
    label.renderOrder=base+2;label.material.depthTest=false;label.material.depthWrite=false;
    g.add(shadow,tile,label);
    let tape=null;
    if(it.tape){tape=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(tw*.9,th*.2),mk('#eee8c8',.55));geos.push(tape.geometry);tape.position.set(0,th*.52,0);tape.rotation.z=it.tapeRot;tape.renderOrder=base+3;g.add(tape)}
    g.position.set(x+w/2,y+it.dy*k,0);g.rotation.z=it.rot;g.visible=false;
    root.add(g);
    chars.push({g,it,label,shadow,tile,tape,x:g.position.x,y:g.position.y});
    x+=w;
  }
  y-=rh/2;
}
const n=Math.max(1,chars.length),step=Math.max(.03,Math.min(60/Math.max(40,bpm||120)/DIV,life*FILL/n)),t0=.05,exit=Math.min(.35,life*.15);
function update(t){
  const tick=Math.floor(t*12),ph=((t*Math.max(40,bpm||120)/60)%1+1)%1,punch=Math.exp(-ph*6);
  const sh=em*.03*SHAKE*(.5+punch);
  root.position.set((hash(tick,1)-.5)*2*sh,(hash(tick,2)-.5)*2*sh,0);
  root.rotation.z=(hash(tick,3)-.5)*.012*SHAKE;

    const e=back(u),a=smooth(u*4)*fade;

    c.g.position.set(c.x+(hash(c.it.idx,tick)-.5)*em*.035,c.y+(hash(c.it.idx,tick+7)-.5)*em*.035,0);
    c.g.rotation.z=c.it.rot+(1-e)*c.it.slam+(hash(c.it.idx,tick+3)-.5)*.016;
    c.label.fillOpacity=a;c.shadow.material.opacity=.36*a;c.tile.material.opacity=a;if(c.tape)c.tape.material.opacity=.55*a;
  }
}

update(0);
return {update,dispose};
文字モーション（コード）10/12 追加

✦波打つ市松の背景BG-CHECKER-WAVE

市松模様が波のようにうねりながら流れる、文字なしの背景です。

メソッド工房の「エフェクト」のサンプルから選びます（文字の後ろや周りに重なる装飾です）。

コードを見る
// 市松模様の角丸の四角が、波のようにうねりながら脈打つ背景（文字は描かない）。文字モーションの下に重ねて使う
const {THREE,scene,width,height,duration,accent}=api;
const smooth=x=>{x=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,x));return x*x*(3-2*x)};
const enter=Math.min(.7,duration*.25),exit=Math.min(.6,duration*.2);
const VERT=`varying vec2 vUv;
void main(){vUv=uv;gl_Position=projectionMatrix*modelViewMatrix*vec4(position,1.0);}`;
const FRAG=`precision highp float;
#define TAU 6.28318530718
uniform float u_time;
uniform float u_fade;
uniform vec2 u_resolution;
uniform vec3 u_accent;
varying vec2 vUv;
vec3 pal(float k){return .55+.45*cos(TAU*(k+vec3(0.,.33,.67)));}
float hash(vec2 p){return fract(sin(dot(p,vec2(127.1,311.7)))*43758.5453);}
mat2 rot(float a){return mat2(cos(a),-sin(a),sin(a),cos(a));}
float vn(vec2 p){vec2 i=floor(p),f=fract(p);f=f*f*(3.-2.*f);
  return mix(mix(hash(i),hash(i+vec2(1.,0.)),f.x),mix(hash(i+vec2(0.,1.)),hash(i+vec2(1.,1.)),f.x),f.y);}
void main(){
  float aspect=u_resolution.x/u_resolution.y;
  vec2 p=(vUv-.5)*vec2(aspect,1.)*9.;
  p.y+=sin(p.x*.8+u_time*1.5)*.6;p.x+=cos(p.y*.7+u_time*1.1)*.4;
  vec2 g=floor(p),f=fract(p)-.5;
  float chk=mod(g.x+g.y,2.);
  float sq=1.-smoothstep(.34,.46,max(abs(f.x),abs(f.y)));
  float pulse=.55+.45*sin(u_time*2.+g.x*.6+g.y*.8);
  vec3 col=mix(u_accent,vec3(1.),.4+.3*sin(g.x*.5+u_time*.8));
  float a=sq*chk*pulse*.6;
  float edge=smoothstep(.025,.05,min(vUv.x,1.-vUv.x))*smoothstep(.025,.05,min(vUv.y,1.-vUv.y));
  gl_FragColor=vec4(col,a*edge*u_fade);
}`;
const material=new THREE.ShaderMaterial({transparent:true,depthTest:false,depthWrite:false,vertexShader:VERT,fragmentShader:FRAG,
  uniforms:{u_time:{value:0},u_fade:{value:0},u_resolution:{value:new THREE.Vector2(width,height)},u_accent:{value:new THREE.Color(accent)}}});
const quad=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(width,height),material);
quad.position.z=-10;quad.renderOrder=-5;scene.add(quad);
function update(t){material.uniforms.u_time.value=t;material.uniforms.u_fade.value=smooth(t/enter)*smooth((duration-t)/exit);}
function dispose(){quad.geometry.dispose();material.dispose();scene.remove(quad);}
update(0);
return {update,dispose};
エフェクト（文字の後ろや周りの装飾）10/12 追加

✦放射状の光線の背景BG-SUNBURST

中心から放射状の光線が、ゆっくり回りながら広がる背景です。

メソッド工房の「エフェクト」のサンプルから選びます（文字の後ろや周りに重なる装飾です）。

コードを見る
// 中心から放射状に伸びる光線が、ゆっくり回転する背景（文字は描かない）。文字モーションの下に重ねて使う
const {THREE,scene,width,height,duration,accent}=api;
const smooth=x=>{x=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,x));return x*x*(3-2*x)};
const enter=Math.min(.7,duration*.25),exit=Math.min(.6,duration*.2);
const VERT=`varying vec2 vUv;
void main(){vUv=uv;gl_Position=projectionMatrix*modelViewMatrix*vec4(position,1.0);}`;
const FRAG=`precision highp float;
#define TAU 6.28318530718
uniform float u_time;
uniform float u_fade;
uniform vec2 u_resolution;
uniform vec3 u_accent;
varying vec2 vUv;
vec3 pal(float k){return .55+.45*cos(TAU*(k+vec3(0.,.33,.67)));}
float hash(vec2 p){return fract(sin(dot(p,vec2(127.1,311.7)))*43758.5453);}
mat2 rot(float a){return mat2(cos(a),-sin(a),sin(a),cos(a));}
float vn(vec2 p){vec2 i=floor(p),f=fract(p);f=f*f*(3.-2.*f);
  return mix(mix(hash(i),hash(i+vec2(1.,0.)),f.x),mix(hash(i+vec2(0.,1.)),hash(i+vec2(1.,1.)),f.x),f.y);}
void main(){
  float aspect=u_resolution.x/u_resolution.y;
  vec2 p=(vUv-.5)*vec2(aspect,1.);
  float ang=atan(p.y,p.x),r=length(p);
  float k=ang/TAU*16.+u_time*.18;
  float f=fract(k);
  float ray=smoothstep(.0,.1,f)*(1.-smoothstep(.34,.44,f));
  float fall=smoothstep(.04,.22,r)*(.4+.6*smoothstep(.0,.9,r));
  vec3 col=mix(u_accent,vec3(1.,.95,.8),.5+.5*sin(k*.7));
  float a=ray*fall*.6;
  float edge=smoothstep(.025,.05,min(vUv.x,1.-vUv.x))*smoothstep(.025,.05,min(vUv.y,1.-vUv.y));
  gl_FragColor=vec4(col,a*edge*u_fade);
}`;
const material=new THREE.ShaderMaterial({transparent:true,depthTest:false,depthWrite:false,vertexShader:VERT,fragmentShader:FRAG,
  uniforms:{u_time:{value:0},u_fade:{value:0},u_resolution:{value:new THREE.Vector2(width,height)},u_accent:{value:new THREE.Color(accent)}}});
const quad=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(width,height),material);
quad.position.z=-10;quad.renderOrder=-5;scene.add(quad);
function update(t){material.uniforms.u_time.value=t;material.uniforms.u_fade.value=smooth(t/enter)*smooth((duration-t)/exit);}
function dispose(){quad.geometry.dispose();material.dispose();scene.remove(quad);}
update(0);
return {update,dispose};
エフェクト（文字の後ろや周りの装飾）10/12 追加

✦歪む方眼の背景BG-GRID-WARP

方眼の線が波のように歪んで揺れる、文字なしの背景です。

メソッド工房の「エフェクト」のサンプルから選びます（文字の後ろや周りに重なる装飾です）。

コードを見る
// 方眼の線が波のように歪んで揺れる背景（文字は描かない）。文字モーションの下に重ねて使う
const {THREE,scene,width,height,duration,accent}=api;
const smooth=x=>{x=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,x));return x*x*(3-2*x)};
const enter=Math.min(.7,duration*.25),exit=Math.min(.6,duration*.2);
const VERT=`varying vec2 vUv;
void main(){vUv=uv;gl_Position=projectionMatrix*modelViewMatrix*vec4(position,1.0);}`;
const FRAG=`precision highp float;
#define TAU 6.28318530718
uniform float u_time;
uniform float u_fade;
uniform vec2 u_resolution;
uniform vec3 u_accent;
varying vec2 vUv;
vec3 pal(float k){return .55+.45*cos(TAU*(k+vec3(0.,.33,.67)));}
float hash(vec2 p){return fract(sin(dot(p,vec2(127.1,311.7)))*43758.5453);}
mat2 rot(float a){return mat2(cos(a),-sin(a),sin(a),cos(a));}
float vn(vec2 p){vec2 i=floor(p),f=fract(p);f=f*f*(3.-2.*f);
  return mix(mix(hash(i),hash(i+vec2(1.,0.)),f.x),mix(hash(i+vec2(0.,1.)),hash(i+vec2(1.,1.)),f.x),f.y);}
void main(){
  float aspect=u_resolution.x/u_resolution.y;
  vec2 p=(vUv-.5)*vec2(aspect,1.)*7.;
  p+=vec2(sin(p.y*1.1+u_time*1.3),cos(p.x*1.0+u_time*1.1))*.45;
  vec2 f=abs(fract(p)-.5);
  float line=smoothstep(.40,.46,max(f.x,f.y));
  float glow=.5+.5*sin(u_time*1.7+p.x*.9+p.y*.6);
  vec3 col=mix(u_accent,vec3(1.),glow*.6);
  float a=line*(.35+.4*glow);
  float edge=smoothstep(.025,.05,min(vUv.x,1.-vUv.x))*smoothstep(.025,.05,min(vUv.y,1.-vUv.y));
  gl_FragColor=vec4(col,a*edge*u_fade);
}`;
const material=new THREE.ShaderMaterial({transparent:true,depthTest:false,depthWrite:false,vertexShader:VERT,fragmentShader:FRAG,
  uniforms:{u_time:{value:0},u_fade:{value:0},u_resolution:{value:new THREE.Vector2(width,height)},u_accent:{value:new THREE.Color(accent)}}});
const quad=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(width,height),material);
quad.position.z=-10;quad.renderOrder=-5;scene.add(quad);
function update(t){material.uniforms.u_time.value=t;material.uniforms.u_fade.value=smooth(t/enter)*smooth((duration-t)/exit);}
function dispose(){quad.geometry.dispose();material.dispose();scene.remove(quad);}
update(0);
return {update,dispose};
エフェクト（文字の後ろや周りの装飾）10/12 追加

✦ぼかしの光の玉の背景BG-BOKEH

やわらかい光の玉がゆっくり漂う、文字なしの背景です。

メソッド工房の「エフェクト」のサンプルから選びます（文字の後ろや周りに重なる装飾です）。

コードを見る
// やわらかい光の玉が漂うボケの背景（文字は描かない）。文字モーションの下に重ねて使う
const {THREE,scene,width,height,duration,accent}=api;
const smooth=x=>{x=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,x));return x*x*(3-2*x)};
const enter=Math.min(.7,duration*.25),exit=Math.min(.6,duration*.2);
const VERT=`varying vec2 vUv;
void main(){vUv=uv;gl_Position=projectionMatrix*modelViewMatrix*vec4(position,1.0);}`;
const FRAG=`precision highp float;
#define TAU 6.28318530718
uniform float u_time;
uniform float u_fade;
uniform vec2 u_resolution;
uniform vec3 u_accent;
varying vec2 vUv;
vec3 pal(float k){return .55+.45*cos(TAU*(k+vec3(0.,.33,.67)));}
float hash(vec2 p){return fract(sin(dot(p,vec2(127.1,311.7)))*43758.5453);}
mat2 rot(float a){return mat2(cos(a),-sin(a),sin(a),cos(a));}
float vn(vec2 p){vec2 i=floor(p),f=fract(p);f=f*f*(3.-2.*f);
  return mix(mix(hash(i),hash(i+vec2(1.,0.)),f.x),mix(hash(i+vec2(0.,1.)),hash(i+vec2(1.,1.)),f.x),f.y);}
void main(){
  float aspect=u_resolution.x/u_resolution.y;
  vec2 p=(vUv-.5)*vec2(aspect,1.);
  vec3 acc=vec3(0.);float al=0.;
  for(int i=0;i<14;i++){
    float fi=float(i);
    float h1=hash(vec2(fi,1.)),h2=hash(vec2(fi,2.)),h3=hash(vec2(fi,3.)),h4=hash(vec2(fi,4.));
    vec2 c=vec2((h1-.5)*aspect*.95,(h2-.5)*.9);
    c+=vec2(sin(u_time*(.15+h3*.25)+fi*1.3),cos(u_time*(.12+h4*.22)+fi*2.1))*.08;
    float r=.05+h3*.08;
    float d=length(p-c);
    float disc=smoothstep(r,r*.55,d)*(.3+.3*h4);
    float rim=smoothstep(r*.78,r,d)*smoothstep(r*1.08,r,d)*.4;
    float k=disc+rim;
    acc+=mix(pal(h1+u_time*.03),u_accent,.4)*k;al+=k;
  }
  vec3 col=acc/max(al,.001);
  float a=min(.75,al);
  float edge=smoothstep(.025,.05,min(vUv.x,1.-vUv.x))*smoothstep(.025,.05,min(vUv.y,1.-vUv.y));
  gl_FragColor=vec4(col,a*edge*u_fade);
}`;
const material=new THREE.ShaderMaterial({transparent:true,depthTest:false,depthWrite:false,vertexShader:VERT,fragmentShader:FRAG,
  uniforms:{u_time:{value:0},u_fade:{value:0},u_resolution:{value:new THREE.Vector2(width,height)},u_accent:{value:new THREE.Color(accent)}}});
const quad=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(width,height),material);
quad.position.z=-10;quad.renderOrder=-5;scene.add(quad);
function update(t){material.uniforms.u_time.value=t;material.uniforms.u_fade.value=smooth(t/enter)*smooth((duration-t)/exit);}
function dispose(){quad.geometry.dispose();material.dispose();scene.remove(quad);}
update(0);
return {update,dispose};
エフェクト（文字の後ろや周りの装飾）10/12 追加

✦プラズマの等高線の背景BG-PLASMA-LINES

色がうねる等高線のような線が流れる、文字なしの背景です。

メソッド工房の「エフェクト」のサンプルから選びます（文字の後ろや周りに重なる装飾です）。

コードを見る
// うねるプラズマの等高線が流れる背景（文字は描かない）。文字モーションの下に重ねて使う
const {THREE,scene,width,height,duration,accent}=api;
const smooth=x=>{x=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,x));return x*x*(3-2*x)};
const enter=Math.min(.7,duration*.25),exit=Math.min(.6,duration*.2);
const VERT=`varying vec2 vUv;
void main(){vUv=uv;gl_Position=projectionMatrix*modelViewMatrix*vec4(position,1.0);}`;
const FRAG=`precision highp float;
#define TAU 6.28318530718
uniform float u_time;
uniform float u_fade;
uniform vec2 u_resolution;
uniform vec3 u_accent;
varying vec2 vUv;
vec3 pal(float k){return .55+.45*cos(TAU*(k+vec3(0.,.33,.67)));}
float hash(vec2 p){return fract(sin(dot(p,vec2(127.1,311.7)))*43758.5453);}
mat2 rot(float a){return mat2(cos(a),-sin(a),sin(a),cos(a));}
float vn(vec2 p){vec2 i=floor(p),f=fract(p);f=f*f*(3.-2.*f);
  return mix(mix(hash(i),hash(i+vec2(1.,0.)),f.x),mix(hash(i+vec2(0.,1.)),hash(i+vec2(1.,1.)),f.x),f.y);}
void main(){
  float aspect=u_resolution.x/u_resolution.y;
  vec2 p=(vUv-.5)*vec2(aspect,1.)*3.;
  float t=u_time*.6;
  float v=sin(p.x*2.+t)+sin(p.y*2.3-t*1.2)+sin((p.x+p.y)*1.7+t*.7)+sin(length(p)*3.5-t*1.4);
  float line=smoothstep(.40,.47,abs(fract(v*.6)-.5));
  vec3 col=mix(u_accent,pal(v*.12+.2),.6);
  float a=line*.65;
  float edge=smoothstep(.025,.05,min(vUv.x,1.-vUv.x))*smoothstep(.025,.05,min(vUv.y,1.-vUv.y));
  gl_FragColor=vec4(col,a*edge*u_fade);
}`;
const material=new THREE.ShaderMaterial({transparent:true,depthTest:false,depthWrite:false,vertexShader:VERT,fragmentShader:FRAG,
  uniforms:{u_time:{value:0},u_fade:{value:0},u_resolution:{value:new THREE.Vector2(width,height)},u_accent:{value:new THREE.Color(accent)}}});
const quad=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(width,height),material);
quad.position.z=-10;quad.renderOrder=-5;scene.add(quad);
function update(t){material.uniforms.u_time.value=t;material.uniforms.u_fade.value=smooth(t/enter)*smooth((duration-t)/exit);}
function dispose(){quad.geometry.dispose();material.dispose();scene.remove(quad);}
update(0);
return {update,dispose};

✦六角形が脈打つ背景BG-HEX-PULSE

六角形のタイルが順番に光って脈打つ、文字なしの背景です。

メソッド工房の「エフェクト」のサンプルから選びます（文字の後ろや周りに重なる装飾です）。

コードを見る
// 六角形のタイルが波のように順番に光る背景（文字は描かない）。文字モーションの下に重ねて使う
const {THREE,scene,width,height,duration,accent}=api;
const smooth=x=>{x=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,x));return x*x*(3-2*x)};
const enter=Math.min(.7,duration*.25),exit=Math.min(.6,duration*.2);
const VERT=`varying vec2 vUv;
void main(){vUv=uv;gl_Position=projectionMatrix*modelViewMatrix*vec4(position,1.0);}`;
const FRAG=`precision highp float;
#define TAU 6.28318530718
uniform float u_time;
uniform float u_fade;
uniform vec2 u_resolution;
uniform vec3 u_accent;
varying vec2 vUv;
vec3 pal(float k){return .55+.45*cos(TAU*(k+vec3(0.,.33,.67)));}
float hash(vec2 p){return fract(sin(dot(p,vec2(127.1,311.7)))*43758.5453);}
mat2 rot(float a){return mat2(cos(a),-sin(a),sin(a),cos(a));}
float vn(vec2 p){vec2 i=floor(p),f=fract(p);f=f*f*(3.-2.*f);
  return mix(mix(hash(i),hash(i+vec2(1.,0.)),f.x),mix(hash(i+vec2(0.,1.)),hash(i+vec2(1.,1.)),f.x),f.y);}
void main(){
  float aspect=u_resolution.x/u_resolution.y;
  vec2 p=(vUv-.5)*vec2(aspect,1.)*6.;
  vec2 r=vec2(1.,1.7320508),h=r*.5;
  vec2 ga=mod(p,r)-h,gb=mod(p-h,r)-h;
  vec2 gv=dot(ga,ga)<dot(gb,gb)?ga:gb;
  vec2 id=p-gv;
  vec2 q=abs(gv);
  float hd=max(dot(q,normalize(vec2(1.,1.7320508))),q.x);
  float wave=sin(u_time*1.8-length(id)*1.1+hash(floor(id*4.+.5))*3.)*.5+.5;
  float on=smoothstep(.55,.75,wave);
  float fill=smoothstep(.5,.4,hd)*on*.55;
  float rim=smoothstep(.38,.44,hd)*smoothstep(.5,.46,hd)*on*.45;
  vec3 col=mix(u_accent,vec3(1.),.35+.4*wave);
  float a=max(fill,rim);
  float edge=smoothstep(.025,.05,min(vUv.x,1.-vUv.x))*smoothstep(.025,.05,min(vUv.y,1.-vUv.y));
  gl_FragColor=vec4(col,a*edge*u_fade);
}`;
const material=new THREE.ShaderMaterial({transparent:true,depthTest:false,depthWrite:false,vertexShader:VERT,fragmentShader:FRAG,
  uniforms:{u_time:{value:0},u_fade:{value:0},u_resolution:{value:new THREE.Vector2(width,height)},u_accent:{value:new THREE.Color(accent)}}});
const quad=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(width,height),material);
quad.position.z=-10;quad.renderOrder=-5;scene.add(quad);
function update(t){material.uniforms.u_time.value=t;material.uniforms.u_fade.value=smooth(t/enter)*smooth((duration-t)/exit);}
function dispose(){quad.geometry.dispose();material.dispose();scene.remove(quad);}
update(0);
return {update,dispose};
エフェクト（文字の後ろや周りの装飾）10/12 追加

✦ぐちゃぐちゃ線の背景BG-SCRIBBLE

何本もの線がくねくね絡み合って走る、落書きのような背景です。

メソッド工房の「エフェクト」のサンプルから選びます（文字の後ろや周りに重なる装飾です）。

コードを見る
// 何本もの線がくねくねと絡み合って走る、落書きのような背景（文字は描かない）。文字モーションの下に重ねて使う
const {THREE,scene,width,height,duration,accent}=api;
const smooth=x=>{x=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,x));return x*x*(3-2*x)};
const enter=Math.min(.7,duration*.25),exit=Math.min(.6,duration*.2);
const VERT=`varying vec2 vUv;
void main(){vUv=uv;gl_Position=projectionMatrix*modelViewMatrix*vec4(position,1.0);}`;
const FRAG=`precision highp float;
#define TAU 6.28318530718
uniform float u_time;
uniform float u_fade;
uniform vec2 u_resolution;
uniform vec3 u_accent;
varying vec2 vUv;
vec3 pal(float k){return .55+.45*cos(TAU*(k+vec3(0.,.33,.67)));}
float hash(vec2 p){return fract(sin(dot(p,vec2(127.1,311.7)))*43758.5453);}
mat2 rot(float a){return mat2(cos(a),-sin(a),sin(a),cos(a));}
float vn(vec2 p){vec2 i=floor(p),f=fract(p);f=f*f*(3.-2.*f);
  return mix(mix(hash(i),hash(i+vec2(1.,0.)),f.x),mix(hash(i+vec2(0.,1.)),hash(i+vec2(1.,1.)),f.x),f.y);}
void main(){
  float aspect=u_resolution.x/u_resolution.y;
  vec2 p=(vUv-.5)*vec2(aspect,1.);
  float a=0.;vec3 col=u_accent;
  for(int i=0;i<8;i++){
    float fi=float(i),ph=fi*1.7;
    float y=.34*sin(p.x*(2.2+fi*.55)+u_time*(.7+fi*.18)+ph)*cos(p.x*(.9+fi*.3)-u_time*.45+ph*1.3)+.1*sin(p.x*9.+u_time*1.5+ph);
    float l=smoothstep(.014,.004,abs(p.y-y));
    if(l>a){a=l;col=mix(u_accent,vec3(1.),mod(fi,2.)*.75);}
  }
  a*=.75;
  float edge=smoothstep(.025,.05,min(vUv.x,1.-vUv.x))*smoothstep(.025,.05,min(vUv.y,1.-vUv.y));
  gl_FragColor=vec4(col,a*edge*u_fade);
}`;
const material=new THREE.ShaderMaterial({transparent:true,depthTest:false,depthWrite:false,vertexShader:VERT,fragmentShader:FRAG,
  uniforms:{u_time:{value:0},u_fade:{value:0},u_resolution:{value:new THREE.Vector2(width,height)},u_accent:{value:new THREE.Color(accent)}}});
const quad=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(width,height),material);
quad.position.z=-10;quad.renderOrder=-5;scene.add(quad);
function update(t){material.uniforms.u_time.value=t;material.uniforms.u_fade.value=smooth(t/enter)*smooth((duration-t)/exit);}
function dispose(){quad.geometry.dispose();material.dispose();scene.remove(quad);}
update(0);
return {update,dispose};

✦斜めの雨筋の背景BG-RAIN-STREAKS

void main(){vUv=uv;gl_Position=projectionMatrix*modelViewMatrix*vec4(position,1.0);}`;

#define TAU 6.28318530718
uniform float u_time;
uniform float u_fade;
uniform vec2 u_resolution;
uniform vec3 u_accent;
varying vec2 vUv;
vec3 pal(float k){return .55+.45*cos(TAU*(k+vec3(0.,.33,.67)));}

void main(){
  float aspect=u_resolution.x/u_resolution.y;
  vec2 p=rot(-.35)*((vUv-.5)*vec2(aspect,1.));
  float cols=24.;

function dispose(){quad.geometry.dispose();material.dispose();scene.remove(quad);}
update(0);
return {update,dispose};

update(0);
return {update,dispose};
